Conclusion: 空间复用策略：将 GPU 流多处理器（SM）分区，重叠 Base 与 LoRA 计算；SLO 感知调度器：基于请求级 slack 解耦请求执行，优先紧急任务并用空闲预算做流量整形，缓解资源竞争

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become essential in privacy-sensitive sectors like hospitals and government agencies, the on-premise LLM servers offer a cost-effective and secure alternative to public cloud services. However, these resource-constrained servers struggle to guarantee heterogeneous Service Level Objectives (SLOs) when serving multiple LoRA-adapted services simultaneously. Existing serving frameworks suffer from severe SLO violations due to the computational overhead of LoRA layers and the rigid nature of batch scheduling. To address this, we propose HALO, a scheduling method tailored for LoRA-assisted on-premise LLM deployment. HALO introduces two key innovations: a spatial multiplexing strategy that overlaps Base and LoRA computations by partitioning GPU Streaming Multiprocessors (SMs), and an SLO-aware scheduler that decouples request execution based on "request-level slack." By prioritizing urgent tasks and utilizing idle budget for traffic shaping, HALO significantly mitigates resource contention. Our evaluation demonstrates that HALO minimizes SLO violations while improving throughput compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: In multilingual scenarios, queries with equivalent semantics but in different languages could guide the model into different reasoning trajectories, leading to performance disparities. To mitigate this gap, previous studies typically apply a one-size-fits-all query rewriting strategy, such as translation, which overlooks the fact that different scenarios require diverse types of semantic transformations. In this paper, we propose mRewriter-R1, an agentic multilingual query rewriting framework with reinforcement learning. Unlike single-turn rewriting, mRewriter-R1 formulates multilingual query rewriting as a multi-turn sequential decision-making process, where the model dynamically performs multi-aspect optimization through adaptive operator selection. Experimental results demonstrate that mRewriter-R1 outperforms all strong multilingual rewriting baselines on different large reasoning backbones. Further analyses show that the learned policy can adaptively decide on rewriting operators according to query characteristics, exhibiting strong generalization ability across diverse reasoning tasks, and plug-and-play compatibility with heterogeneous reasoning language models.

</details>

*Kishor Kumar Bhaumik,Nicolas Roque dos Santos,Neil Shah,Jia Chen,Evangelos E. Papalexakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SilK，利用聚类有效性指标（简化轮廓系数）作为自监督目标微调文本编码器，无需负样本或图数据，在BERT-base上比最快基线快1.46倍、内存少45.4%，线性探测性能与最佳基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不需要多视图数据、负样本对或辅助图网络的条件下，对预训练语言编码器进行高效的自监督微调，以改进文本表示空间。

Method: SilK（Silhouette-guided K-means）的端到端流程：输入为无标签文档语料，首先使用预训练语言编码器（BERT-base）获取文档表示，然后在每轮训练中对当前语料表示执行聚类（K-means），得到k个聚类中心；随后对每个文档计算简化轮廓系数，该系数衡量文档与其所属聚类中心和其他聚类中心的相对距离，并将其回归到目标值。训练目标为最小化预测的简化轮廓系数与目标值之间的差异。由于每个文档仅与k个聚类中心进行比较，无需文档间对比、数据增强或负样本对，也无需辅助图网络。推理时，可直接使用微调后的编码器生成文本表示，无需聚类步骤。该设计解决了传统自监督微调依赖多视图/负样本或辅助图导致的计算开销问题，实现了更快的训练速度和更低的内存占用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "每epoch训练速度", "value": "比评估的最快基线快1.46倍", "setting": "BERT-base模型"}, {"metric": "峰值GPU内存", "value": "比最省内存的基线减少45.4%", "setting": "BERT-base模型"}, {"metric": "下游任务线性探测性能", "value": "在三个下游任务上与最佳基线保持竞争力", "setting": "冻结编码器线性探测"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 使用聚类有效性指标（简化轮廓系数）作为自监督目标；将每篇文档仅与k个聚类中心比较，避免文档间负样本和增强视图；SilK方法在BERT-base上实现比最快基线快1.46倍的每epoch训练速度，并比最省内存基线减少45.4%峰值GPU内存

Abstract: Self-supervised fine-tuning refines the embedding space of a pretrained language encoder without labels. However, the commonly used approaches are computationally expensive. Specifically, contrastive learning-based methods need multiview data and in-batch negative examples, while negative-free approaches require auxiliary graphs/networks. An interesting question arises: can self-supervised fine-tuning be done without relying on either additional negatives or graph data? To answer this question, we introduce SilK (Silhouette-guided K-means), which trains on a Cluster Validation Index, an internal measure of cluster quality without using labels. SilK clusters the corpus and then regresses a simplified silhouette toward a target value. Each document is compared only against the k cluster centroids, never against other documents, so the method needs no augmentation, no negative pairs and one view per document. On BERT-base, SilK trains 1.46x faster per epoch than the fastest baseline we evaluate and uses 45.4% less peak GPU memory than the leanest one. Under frozen-encoder linear probing, SilK stays competitive with the best baselines on three downstream tasks.

</details>

*Noam Elata,Itay Lamprecht,Mikey Shechter,Daniel Ohayon,Itay Hubara,Daniel Soudry*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAGA（稀疏非对称组查询注意力）和Atop-N稀疏注意力，通过解耦键头与值头数量实现LLM解码加速，在1.5B模型上长上下文获得超过2倍端到端加速，质量接近GQA，并提供高效微调转换方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自回归生成受限于注意力机制的内存和计算开销，现有稀疏注意力方法在头数对称时query-key步骤成为瓶颈。

Method: SAGA（Sparse Asymmetric Group-Query Attention）将查询、键、值头的数量解耦：键头数量少于值头数量，以利用稀疏注意力中概率-值乘法开销可忽略、瓶颈转移到query-key步骤的现象。Atop-N注意力选择注意力矩阵中高概率的条目（近似top-N），进一步降低计算。理论上，减少键头可以在解码时降低query-key计算成本，而保留更多值头则保持模型容量，且额外解码成本有限。训练目标与标准LLM相同（自回归语言建模），并提供了微调方法将预训练GQA模型转换为SAGA架构。推理时，根据稀疏性选择注意力条目，结合非对称头数实现加速。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "端到端解码加速", "value": ">2×", "setting": "长上下文，相对于全注意力GQA基线"}, {"metric": "质量", "value": "接近可比GQA变体", "setting": "从头训练SAGA，在评估基准上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出SAGA (Sparse Asymmetric Group-Query Attention)，解耦键头和值头的数量以利用稀疏注意力下KV乘法开销降低的特性；提出Atop-N稀疏注意力方法，简单近似top-N，用于研究稀疏性与非对称头数的交互；从理论上形式化非对称头数带来的优势；提出高效微调方法将预训练模型转换为SAGA架构，避免从头训练

Abstract: utoregressive generation in Large Language Models (LLMs) is constrained by the memory and computational demands of attention mechanisms. Sparse attention methods mitigate this cost by selecting only high-probability entries of the attention matrix. We observe that in many such methods, this renders the probability-value multiplication negligible, shifting the bottleneck to the query-key step. Key heads can therefore be reduced to accelerate inference, while retaining more value heads preserves capacity with limited additional decoding cost. We introduce Sparse Asymmetric Group-Query Attention (SAGA), which decouples key and value head counts to exploit this principle, and pair it with approximate top-N (Atop-N) attention, a simple sparse attention method designed to study the interaction between sparsity and head-count asymmetry. We formalize the benefits of this asymmetry theoretically and validate them empirically through latency measurements and quality evaluations on models up to 1.5B parameters. Together, SAGA and Atop-N achieve end-to-end decoding speedups exceeding $2\times$ over our full-attention GQA baseline at long contexts. Models trained from scratch with SAGA nearly match the quality of comparable GQA variants on the evaluated benchmarks. To facilitate adoption, we introduce an efficient fine-tuning method that converts pretrained models to the SAGA architecture, enabling practitioners to benefit from our approach without costly retraining.

</details>


### [35] [Steering Speech-Language Models: Training-Free Task Specialization via Contrastive Activation Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04683)
*Séverin Baroudi,Yanis Labrak,Pierfrancesco Melucci,Sergio Burdisso,Petr Motlicek,Hervé Bredin,Mirco Ravanelli,Ricard Marxer*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不重新训练的情况下，通过激活引导使语音语言模型更好地执行特定语音任务（如ASR、情感识别），并提升跨域泛化能力。

Method: 论文提出一种免训练的对比激活加法（CAA）协议，用于语音语言模型（SpeechLLM）。该方法首先从少量标注话语中提取正负样本对，计算模型在特定层表示空间中的激活差异，得到任务特定的引导向量。在推理时，将这些引导向量加到模型的隐藏表示中，从而在不更新参数的情况下引导模型偏向目标语音任务。该方法可与提示结合使用，进一步增强任务执行效果。实验表明，在自动语音识别（ASR）和情感识别等任务上，结合提示后性能一致优于仅提示基线，并且引导向量能迁移到域外数据。此外，通过脚本归一化方向可以强制模型输出特定语言的文字脚本。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出免训练的对比激活加法（CAA）协议，从少量标注话语中推导出语音任务专用引导向量。；将引导向量与提示结合，在多数评估任务上一致优于仅使用提示的方法。；证明引导向量可迁移到域外数据，并展示脚本归一化方向可用于强制目标语言脚本。

Abstract: Activation steering has proven effective for controlling the behavior of Large Language Models (LLMs) at inference time, but its application to SpeechLLMs remains new, and training-free steering approaches for such models are still largely unexplored. We propose a training-free Contrastive Activation Addition (CAA) protocol that derives steering vectors for common speech tasks (e.g. transcription) in SpeechLLMs from a small number of labeled utterances. We showcase that adding these vectors in the representation space, at inference time, enforces better the targeted speech task. We further show that, when combined with prompting, these vectors yield to consistent improvement over prompting alone on most evaluated tasks such as Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) or Emotion Recognition (ER), and transfer to out-of-domain data. We additionally demonstrate the usefulness of script-normalization directions to enforce the target script of a specific language.

</details>

*Ananya Malik,Mai ElSherief*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出PaSS，用迭代零空间投影（INLP）在LLM隐空间中提取多维persona子空间，实现推理期无重训练的persona调制；相比单方向激活引导能在保持内容保真和任务性能的前提下实现更强、更细粒度的行为控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不重训练、无监督对比样本的条件下，对LLM中已隐含的persona特质进行细粒度放大或抑制（modulation），而非像prompt或单方向激活引导那样把persona压缩为单一主导方向从而丢失嵌套的次要特质。

Method: PaSS为推理期控制范式，不需要训练或微调。输入为带有persona语境的提示与内容表示；在目标LLM的隐空间（激活）上，利用Iterative Nullspace Projection（INLP）迭代地执行概念擦除：每一轮训练一个线性分类器区分目标persona特质是否存在，取其权重方向作为一个persona相关方向，将激活投影到该方向的零空间以移除该方向上的分量，循环多轮后得到一组正交（或近正交）方向，构成该persona的多维子空间。推理时，调制通过在这组子空间方向上对激活施加缩放（放大/抑制）实现，从而在已有persona语境基础上增强或削弱相应特质；该过程逐方向可解释，可单独分析每个被剥离方向的语义作用。评估在MATH-500、TinyAlpaca、GSM8K、IFEval等任务上对六种persona进行因果评估，比较单方向加性方法与迭代子空间方法在调制强度、内容保真度和任务性能上的差异。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出modulation这一新设定：persona上下文已嵌入被操控内容中，控制方法需放大/抑制已有特质而非从零注入；提出PaSS推理期控制范式，将persona建模为隐空间中的多维子空间而非单一方向；使用Iterative Nullspace Projection (INLP)通过迭代概念擦除线性地逐层剥离persona特有方向，且无需监督对比样本、无需重训练；对子空间内被逐层剥离的单个方向进行解释性研究，揭示其编码的persona行为的不同侧面

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can adopt distinct personas to tune their semantics, expertise, and perspective to different users and tasks. Precise control over these traits is critical to ensure safety and reliability in model behavior. Existing methods like activation steering and prompt-based persona induction reduce a persona to a single dominant direction, missing the finer, nested traits that emerge only once that dominant signal is factored out. We introduce modulation as a setting where the persona context is already embedded in the content being manipulated, requiring control methods to amplify or suppress a trait already present rather than inject it from scratch. PaSS is an inference-time control paradigm that models personas as multi-dimensional subspaces in a model's latent space without supervised contrastive examples. The persona subspaces are extracted via iterative concept erasure and applied to modulate persona-guided generation without retraining. To extract this subspace, we use Iterative Nullspace Projections (INLP) to linearly and iteratively isolate persona-specific directions. We causally evaluate six personas against diverse tasks like MATH-500, TinyAlpaca, GSM8K, and IFEval, showing that discriminative, iterative subspace extraction captures diverse traits underlying a given persona, enabling stronger and larger modulation than single-direction additive methods, while maintaining content fidelity. We further study individual peeled directions within each subspace to uncover the distinct aspects of persona behavior they encode. Overall, we show that persona subspaces offer a controllable, interpretable, and generalizable framework for modulating LLM behavior without sacrificing task performance.

</details>


### [37] [Grounding Probes: Generator-Independent Hallucination Detection from Observer Model Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04642)
*Michael Rathmayr,Ádám Kovács,Gábor Recski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Grounding Probe：用独立观察者模型的中间层隐藏状态加逻辑回归检测 RAG 回答是否被上下文支撑，与生成器解耦，在 RAGTruth 上取得 0.879-0.894 AUROC，融合 span 检测器后达 0.924 AUROC / 0.820 F1@0.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于隐藏状态的幻觉探针依赖生成模型自身激活，换生成器即失效，且闭源权重模型不可用；采样式方法又需要额外生成、成本高。本文要解决如何与生成器解耦地检测 RAG 回答是否被上下文支撑。

Method: 系统由生成侧与观察侧解耦组成：RAG 生成器产生 response，Grounding Probe 使用一个独立的观察者语言模型，将 context、question、response 拼接为单次前向输入，不生成任何 token。观察者模型中间层对 response token 的隐藏状态做 mean-pooling，得到固定维度表示；该表示输入逻辑回归分类器，预测 response 是否被上下文支撑。训练目标为二分类交叉熵，仅优化探针参数，观察者模型冻结。设计上通过只池化 response token、选择中间层、限制分类器容量来抑制过拟合，使 train-test AUROC 差距缩小至 0.009-0.013。推理时只需一次观察者前向与一次逻辑回归，无需采样多个生成结果；同一探针可跨六个生成器使用，实现生成器无关的部署。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AUROC", "value": "0.879-0.894", "setting": "RAGTruth test 集，四个观察者模型，15,090 条标注响应上拟合"}, {"metric": "平均 AUROC", "value": "0.924", "setting": "与有监督 span 检测器融合后"}, {"metric": "F1@0.5", "value": "0.820", "setting": "与有监督 span 检测器融合后"}, {"metric": "AUROC 提升", "value": "0.060", "setting": "融合方法相比单独 span 检测器"}, {"metric": "train-test AUROC 差距", "value": "0.009-0.013", "setting": "采用 mean-pooling response token、中间层、控制容量配方后"}, {"metric": "AUROC 下降", "value": "至少 0.166", "setting": "直接询问观察者模型而非读取隐藏状态，四个模型上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Grounding Probe：用独立的观察者语言模型单次前向读取 context、question、response 的隐藏状态，不做生成；明确给出有效配方：对 response token 做 mean-pooling、读取中间层、控制容量，将 train-test AUROC 差距从 0.087-0.202 降至 0.009-0.013；验证直接询问观察者模型（而非读隐藏状态）在四个模型上至少低 0.166 AUROC，说明隐藏状态读取优于显式判断；一个探针可跨六个生成器保持有效，hold-out 控制下去生成器耦合的成本约 0.02 AUROC；与有监督 span 检测器融合，平均达到 0.924 AUROC / 0.820 F1@0.5，比单独检测器高 0.060

Abstract: Detecting responses that retrieval-augmented generation does not ground in its context trades speed against accuracy: surface checks miss paraphrased fabrication, sampling-based methods cost extra generations. Hidden-state probes sit between the two, but every existing one reads the generating model's own activations, so a change of generator invalidates the detector and a closed-weight generator is out of reach. This paper removes that coupling. The Grounding Probe is logistic regression over the mean-pooled middle-layer hidden states of an observer language model that reads the context, question, and response in one forward pass and generates nothing, with the recipe it needs: pool over response tokens, read a middle layer, and control capacity, which closes the train-test AUROC gap from 0.087-0.202 to 0.009-0.013. Asking the observer outright, rather than reading its hidden state, costs at least +0.166 AUROC in every one of four models. Fitted on 15,090 annotated responses it reaches 0.879-0.894 AUROC on RAGTruth test across four observers, and 0.924 AUROC with 0.820 F1@0.5 averaged with a supervised span detector, 0.060 above that detector alone. One probe holds across six generators, and hold-out controls, including one in which no evaluation prompt appears in training, bound the cost of removing a generator at about 0.02 AUROC. Code, probes, and predictions are released.

</details>


### [38] [DV-Lens: Revealing the Functional Organization of Language Model Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04489)
*Chenhang Cui,Jian Yu,Shuyi Miao,Xiaohao Liu,Rui Huang,Fei Shen,An Zhang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DV-Lens 是一个参数级可解释性框架，通过下游雅可比矩阵将 LLM 参数列映射到词汇空间，揭示参数功能组织与模型能力的关系，并在 48 个模型上发现 DV-Complexity 与能力排名高度相关（Spearman 0.904）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将不同模块（注意力 Q/K/V/O 投影和 FFN）的参数与可验证的输出效果（下游词汇响应）联系起来，并刻画参数功能组织与模型能力之间的关系。

Method: DV-Lens 框架包含三个主要步骤：首先，在参考提示集上估计注意力 Q/K/V/O 投影和 FFN 的模块特定下游雅可比矩阵，建立原生参数方向与下游词汇响应之间的映射。其次，利用这些映射将原生参数列投影到最终词汇空间，得到带符号的读出表示，用以刻画参数列的局部平均输出响应。第三，根据词汇读出对参数列进行分组，并引入下游词汇复杂度（DV-Complexity），通过原始权重的归一化重构残差量化组内结构变化。该方法用于指导参数消融、引导和交换，并在多个模型上验证了读出的非随机性和预测能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "坐标方向一致性", "value": "98.0%", "setting": "720 个案例，来自 9 个模型"}, {"metric": "Spearman 相关系数", "value": "0.904", "setting": "48 个语言模型，DV-Complexity 联合参数得分与基准能力排名"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 DV-Lens 框架，通过模块特定的下游雅可比矩阵将原生参数列投影到最终词汇空间，获得带符号的读出表示。；引入下游词汇复杂度（DV-Complexity），基于原始权重的归一化重构残差量化组内结构变化。；通过随机对照和有限差分测试验证读出的非随机性，并展示其在参数消融、引导和交换中的干预效果。；发现 DV-Complexity 联合参数得分与 48 个语言模型的基准能力排名具有 0.904 的 Spearman 相关性。

Abstract: Understanding parameter functions helps elucidate the internal mechanisms of large language models (LLMs). However, how to connect parameters from different modules to verifiable output effects and further characterize the relationship between their functional organization and model capability remains to be explored. To this end, we introduce the downstream vocabulary lens (DV-Lens), a parameter-level interpretability framework that links native parameter directions to their downstream vocabulary responses. Specifically, we first estimate module-specific downstream Jacobians over a reference prompt set for attention query, key, value, and output (Q/K/V/O) projections and feed-forward networks (FFNs). Second, we use these mappings to project native parameter columns into the final vocabulary space, obtaining signed readouts that characterize their average local output responses. Third, we group parameter columns by their vocabulary readouts and introduce downstream vocabulary complexity (DV-Complexity), which quantifies within-group structural variation using normalized reconstruction residuals of the original weights. At the parameter level, randomized controls and finite-difference tests show that DV-Lens readouts capture non-random vocabulary structure and predict local logit changes with 98.0% coordinate-orientation agreement across 720 cases from nine models. These readouts further guide parameter ablation, steering, and swapping across 21 models, shifting target-token probabilities in the predicted directions under controlled conditions. At the model level, the joint-parameter score of DV-Complexity achieves a Spearman correlation of 0.904 with benchmark-based capability rankings across 48 language models. Together, these results provide intervention-based evidence for DV-Lens interpretations and reveal an association between DV-Complexity and model capability.

</details>


### [39] [Understanding and Mitigating Hallucination Escape in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04409)
*Peigui Qi,Kunsheng Tang,Yide Song,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现工具调用LLM智能体的‘幻觉逃逸’失败模式：现有缓解方法在跨工具配置时效果相互抵消。作者提出训练无关的推理时方法EscapeGuard，通过冲突感知门控与配置衍生注意力增强，在六个基准上降低工具选择幻觉9.0个百分点，跨配置平均值降低23.7个百分点，配对查询净改进89.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具调用型LLM智能体在切换工具配置时存在未被充分关注的‘幻觉逃逸’（Hallucination Escape）失败模式：现有缓解方法在调优配置上减少幻觉，但在其他配置上反而增加幻觉，抵消收益。论文要解决的问题是在不同工具配置下一致地减少工具选择幻觉并抑制幻觉逃逸。

Method: 论文提出的EscapeGuard是训练无关的推理时方法。其整体流程为：在LLM智能体进行工具调用决策时，首先通过冲突感知门控（conflict-aware gating）判断模型内在工具使用倾向与当前工具配置之间是否存在冲突，并据此控制干预强度；随后利用配置衍生注意力增强（configuration-derived attention enhancement）在推理阶段增强与当前工具配置相关的注意力信号，以抑制错误工具选择并防止跨配置幻觉逃逸。方法不涉及训练或参数更新，仅在推理时对注意力与门控进行干预。论文未披露具体的模块输入表示、注意力增强的实现细节、训练目标（因训练无关）或服务部署流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "tool-selection hallucination reduction", "value": "9.0 pp", "setting": "六个基准、多种模型上的评估（未披露具体基准名称、模型与基线）"}, {"metric": "cross-configuration mean reduction", "value": "23.7 pp", "setting": "跨工具配置平均，用于衡量抑制幻觉逃逸的效果"}, {"metric": "paired-query net improvement", "value": "89.1%", "setting": "配对查询评估协议下"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别并定义Hallucination Escape失败模式：工具幻觉缓解方法在跨工具配置时收益被抵消甚至反转。；分析其机制：当模型内在工具使用倾向与当前工具配置冲突时幻觉急剧上升，现有方法强化而非抑制这些倾向。；提出EscapeGuard：训练无关的推理时方法，包含冲突感知门控（conflict-aware gating）与配置衍生注意力增强（configuration-derived attention enhancement）。；提出跨配置评估视角，并用配对查询评估净改进。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as autonomous agents that invoke external tools. However, this capability introduces tool hallucination, selecting incorrect tools or generating invalid calls. Existing mitigation methods report substantial improvements, yet we identify a previously overlooked failure mode that we term Hallucination Escape. These methods reduce hallucination on the tool configuration they are tuned on but increase it on other configurations, canceling out the gain. We further investigate this phenomenon and find that hallucination rises sharply when a model's intrinsic tool-use tendencies conflict with the current tool configuration, and that existing methods reinforce rather than suppress these tendencies, which in turn contributes to hallucination escape. Building on these findings, we propose EscapeGuard, a training-free inference-time method that combines conflict-aware gating with configuration-derived attention enhancement to mitigate tool hallucination while preventing hallucination escape. Across six benchmarks on various models, EscapeGuard reduces tool-selection hallucination by 9.0 pp and suppresses hallucination escape, lowering the cross-configuration mean by 23.7 pp and achieving an 89.1% net improvement in paired-query evaluation. We hope this work can encourage evaluation beyond a single tool configuration and pave the way for more reliable tool-using LLM agents.

</details>


### [40] [GlitchPatch: Repairing Glitch Tokens in Frozen Language Models via Local Retokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04399)
*Kunsheng Tang,Peigui Qi,Yide Song,Peijun Huang,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GlitchPatch提出一种针对冻结语言模型的外部glitch token修复框架，通过离线行为路径优化生成替换规则表，在线仅替换匹配的glitch token ID，不修改模型。在10个模型、6个tokenizer家族上平均修复率达85.10%，将平均glitch率从14.88%降至2.27%，且对未匹配输入零改动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无法访问模型内部或修改模型参数的冻结检查点场景下，如何有效修复glitch tokens，使LLM输出与输入一致，同时避免对正常令牌的附带损害。

Method: GlitchPatch是一个针对冻结语言模型的glitch token修复框架，分为离线和在线两个阶段。输入为标准token序列。离线阶段：对于每个glitch token，使用行为路径优化（Behavioral Path Optimization, BPO）在tokenization空间中搜索行为最优的替换token序列——即通过改变输入的分词方式，寻找能够避免触发故障行为且对正常语义影响最小的替代序列。BPO可能利用模型对替换序列的输出进行评分或验证。验证通过的替换序列被编译成一个规则表，存储glitch token ID到替换token序列的映射。在线阶段：模型推理前，对输入的规范token序列进行扫描，仅当遇到规则表中匹配的glitch token ID时，将其替换为对应的最优替换序列；未匹配的输入保持不变，不修改模型参数或内部状态。因此，该方法完全在模型外部运行，无需访问模型内部。训练目标/优化：BPO在离线阶段优化替换序列，目标函数包括最大化修复效果（使输出与输入一致）和最小化对正常令牌的附带损害。推理流程：输入文本→标准tokenization→扫描匹配规则表→替换glitch token ID→送入冻结LLM→输出。该设计解决了在不修改模型的情况下修复异常词汇条目并保持其他输入不变的问题，同时通过局部替换避免了对共享合并节点的正常令牌的破坏。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均修复率（mean fix rate）", "value": "85.10%", "setting": "在十个模型、六个tokenizer家族上的实验，优于最强基线14.37个百分点"}, {"metric": "平均glitch率（average glitch rate）", "value": "从14.88%降至2.27%", "setting": "同上实验设置"}, {"metric": "规则未匹配输入的修复率（RR）", "value": "0.00%", "setting": "全词汇表评估，表明未匹配输入未被改动"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 通过实验揭示了BPE合并规则删除修复glitch token的局限性：修复部分故障但会破坏共享中间合并节点的正常令牌，导致整体glitch率上升。；提出两阶段GlitchPatch框架：离线阶段使用行为路径优化（BPO）为每个glitch token寻找行为最优的替换令牌序列，并编译验证过的替换为规则表；在线阶段仅替换匹配到的glitch token ID，不修改模型参数或内部状态。；实现完全外部修复，无需模型内部访问，且规则未匹配的输入保持不变，在完整词汇表评估中达到0.00%的修复率（可能指错误率）。

Abstract: Glitch tokens are anomalous vocabulary entries that can cause large language models (LLMs) to produce outputs inconsistent with their inputs. Existing repair methods require access to model internals, making them impractical for frozen checkpoints. We investigate whether glitch tokens can be repaired outside the model by optimizing the input tokenization. An empirical study on BPE merge-rule deletion reveals that (1)deleting a glitch token's merge rule can fix a substantial fraction of failures, yet disrupting normal tokens sharing intermediate merge nodes causes the overall glitch rate to rise, and (2)different decomposition granularities yield non-monotonic fix rates while collateral damage on normal tokens grows monotonically. Motivated by these findings, we propose GlitchPatch, a repair framework for frozen language models based on local retokenization, consisting of two stages: the offline stage uses Behavioral Path Optimization (BPO) to find the behaviorally optimal replacement token sequence for each glitch token and compiles validated replacements into a rule table; the online stage substitutes only the IDs of matched glitch tokens in the canonical token sequence, with no modification to model parameters or internal states. Experiments on ten models spanning six tokenizer families show that GlitchPatch achieves an 85.10% mean fix rate, outperforming the strongest baseline by 14.37 percentage points, and reduces the average glitch rate from 14.88% to 2.27%. GlitchPatch achieves a 0.00% RR in full-vocabulary evaluation and leaves rule-unmatched inputs unchanged by design. We further evaluate the practical impact of repair from the perspectives of time cost, language understanding, and capability, supporting its deployment feasibility. We hope this work provides a practical option for improving tokenizer reliability.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM不确定性量化方法（包括有形式保证的保形预测）在同义改写等保义扰动下预测置信度不稳定，导致预测集大小和覆盖率波动。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预测集大小与经验覆盖率", "value": "在七个多选题QA基准和多个模型家族上产生紧凑预测集，经验覆盖率通常接近名义目标，半改写设置下也如此", "setting": "七个multiple-choice QA基准，normal/fully reworded/semi-reworded三种设置，多个模型家族"}, {"metric": "消融结果", "value": "学习到的代理模型贡献了预测集大小缩减的大部分；释义增强训练和推理时聚合提升改写下的稳定性", "setting": "消融研究"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出释义感知的不确定性量化框架，对语义改写鲁棒；在LLM隐状态上训练轻量代理模型，并在多个改写版本上聚合预测，构造按标签的非一致性分数；在分数可交换性下保留保形校准的边际覆盖保证；在仅测试时改写场景中，额外给出释义管线分布对齐条件以维持保证；设计normal、fully reworded、semi-reworded三种评估设置，并做消融分析代理模型、释义增强训练与推理聚合的贡献

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) for large language models (LLMs) aims to provide reliable measures of predictive confidence, yet current methods are often unstable under meaning-preserving perturbations. Semantically equivalent paraphrases can induce substantial variability in predictive confidence, even for methods with formal guarantees, such as conformal prediction. To address this issue, we propose a paraphrase-aware UQ framework robust to semantic rewordings. Our approach trains a lightweight proxy model on LLM hidden states and aggregates its predictions across paraphrases to construct label-wise nonconformity scores. Under score exchangeability, conformal calibration retains marginal coverage. This guarantee can also hold under test-only rewording, provided that the paraphrase pipeline satisfies an additional distributional alignment condition. We evaluate three settings (normal, fully reworded, and semi-reworded) which apply rewording to neither dataset, both calibration and test datasets, or only the test dataset, respectively. Across seven multiple-choice QA benchmarks and multiple model families, our method produces compact prediction sets with empirical coverage generally near the nominal target, even in the semi-reworded setting. Ablation studies show that the learned proxy accounts for most of the reduction in set size, while paraphrase-augmented training and inference-time aggregation improve stability under rewording. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>

*A. Bochkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文在1.7B级模型、100B token训练预算下，比较可训练输入嵌入表与固定Token码（16位ID码、GF(2)可逆重编码）。固定码模型仍获得可观能力（HellaSwag 52.40%、PIQA 70.51%、LAMBADA 42.75%），但学习表控制组在若干评估上更好。结论是Token特定可训练输入向量并非观测能力的架构必要条件，移除约100.7M参数，表明固定身份输入可作为受控研究设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可训练输入嵌入表是否为语言模型获得实质性能力所必需的Token特定参数化。

Method: 论文比较三种仅解码器（decoder-only）语言模型，它们共享相同分词器、上下文Transformer骨干、未绑定输出头架构和训练配方。输入接口不同：1）可训练输入嵌入表，为每个词表项分配独立可调向量；2）固定规范16位Token ID码；3）基于GF(2)的一个固定可逆重编码。固定码被直接重复以匹配模型宽度，不增加额外的可训练输入投影。模型以目标1000亿预测token从零训练，输出头未绑定。评估包括HellaSwag、PIQA、LAMBADA等。固定码接口移除100.7M可训练参数，得到1.711B参数模型。固定身份接口为研究不可变输入下游的表示学习提供了受控设置，但论文未定位具体能力所在。

Conclusion: 用固定不可训练的Token身份接口替代可训练输入嵌入表，比较三种输入接口：学习表、规范16位Token ID码、GF(2)上固定可逆重编码。；固定码可直接重复到模型宽度，无需额外可训练输入投影。；在1.7B参数级、100B token训练预算下进行受控对比，说明架构必要性与经验效用。

Abstract: A trainable input embedding table assigns each vocabulary item an independently adjustable vector. We investigate whether this token-specific parameterization is required for substantial language-modeling capability, or whether a shared Transformer can learn from fixed token identities. We compare three decoder-only language models trained from scratch with the same tokenizer, contextual backbone, untied output-head architecture, and training recipe, with a target budget of 100 billion prediction tokens per model. Their input interfaces are a learned table, canonical 16-bit token-ID codes, and one fixed invertible recoding over GF(2). The fixed codes are repeated to model width without an additional trainable input projection. Both fixed-code models acquire substantial capabilities: canonical codes achieve 52.40\% HellaSwag normalized accuracy, 70.51\% PIQA accuracy, and 42.75\% LAMBADA accuracy. The learned-input control performs better on several evaluations, including HellaSwag and LAMBADA, so these results establish viability rather than performance parity. The fixed interfaces remove 100.7 million trainable parameters, yielding 1.711B-parameter models, but parameter reduction is not the central result. These single-run experiments distinguish architectural necessity from empirical utility: independently trainable token-specific input vectors are not required for the observed capabilities. A fixed identity interface also provides a controlled setting for studying representation learning downstream of an immutable input, without establishing where particular capabilities are localized.

</details>


### [43] [Periscope: Extending Frozen Language Models Beyond Their Context Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04047)
*Mohamed Eltahir,Anas Obayd,Raed Rashid,Abdulrahman Alghamdi,Abdulrahman Mousa,Abdallah Ahmed,Tanveer Hussain,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Periscope 是一种免训练的长上下文推理方法：把长文本分块排成 K×K 网格，用冻结模型对局部连续跨度与全局跨步跨度探测同一问题，读取单 token log-odds，并由每个块的最佳局部与跨步分数生成零额外成本的证据图，只读图中排名最高的 K 个块。在 LongBench v2 上以 9k token 匹配 32k–1M 窗口的最佳窗口读，在 InfiniteBench（中位 150k token）领先 5 点，并在 BRIGHT 上取得六种方法中最佳 NDCG@10；27B 模型可在单张 80GB GPU 上处理 4.5M token 上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结语言模型的上下文窗口有限，且随着输入长度增加准确率在到达窗口前就下降；能否在有限集合上做决策（哪个文档相关、哪个选项被支持、哪段是证据）时，把一次长文本的二次方前向读取分解为低成本的分块探测。

Method: Periscope 是一条免训练的推理流水线，不更新任何模型参数。输入是一段长文本，按块大小 c 切分为 N 个块，并以 K=ceil(sqrt(N)) 将其排布在 K×K 网格上。对每个预设问题（哪个文档相关、哪个选项被支持、哪段是证据），方法构造两类探测：K 个局部跨度，每个由网格中一行/连续 K 个块构成，覆盖连续局部上下文；K 个跨步跨度，每个跨步采样整个文本的 K 个块，覆盖全局信息。冻结模型对这两类跨度分别回答同一问题，并读取答案在单 token 位置的 log-odds 作为分数。答案的最终分数取其在所有局部跨度中的最佳分与所有跨步跨度中的最佳分之融合。由于每个块同时属于一个局部跨度与一个跨步跨度，对每个块用其两个所属跨度的分数即可零额外成本地生成全文本的证据图，图中峰值即为答案背后的块。推理时只读取证据图排名最高的 K 个块作为最终上下文；每次模型调用只缓存一个探测的 KV 缓存，因此 27B 模型可在单张 80GB GPU 上处理 4.5M token 上下文，而一次完整长读需要 296GB 缓存。整体计算复杂度为 O(s^1.5)，有效覆盖长度约 W^2/c（窗口 W、块大小 c）。训练目标不适用：该方法训练-free，仅依赖冻结模型对同一问题的读出与分数聚合。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "LongBench v2", "value": "读取证据图排名最高的 K 个块（9k token）可匹配同一模型在 32k 至 1M 窗口下的最佳窗口读取", "setting": "LongBench v2 基准，冻结模型，仅读取排名最高的 K 个块（约 9k token）"}, {"metric": "InfiniteBench", "value": "领先最佳窗口读取 5 个点", "setting": "InfiniteBench 基准，中位上下文 150k token"}, {"metric": "NDCG@10", "value": "六种方法中最佳", "setting": "BRIGHT 长文档语料排序"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 免训练推理方法：将 N 个块排列在 K=ceil(sqrt(N)) 的 K×K 网格上，用冻结模型对 K 个局部连续跨度与 K 个跨步全局采样跨度提出同一问题，并读取答案的单 token log-odds。；双跨度打分聚合：每个答案取其最佳局部分数与最佳跨步分数组合，从而在 O(s^1.5) 代价下覆盖 W^2/c 的有效长度。；零额外成本的证据图：对每个块用其两个所属跨度打分生成证据图，峰值对应答案背后的块。；以证据图替代长读：只读取证据图排名最高的 K 个块（9k token）即可匹配相同模型在 32k 至 1M 窗口下的最佳窗口读取。；显存高效推理：每次调用只缓存一个探测，使 27B 模型在单张 80GB GPU 上处理 4.5M token 上下文，而单次完整前向需要 296GB 缓存。

Abstract: A language model reads long text in one quadratic forward pass, stops at the context window, and loses accuracy with length before reaching it. We ask whether the read can be factorized when deciding over a finite set: which document is relevant, which option is supported, which passage is the evidence. Periscope, a training-free inference method, arranges the $N$ chunks of a text on a $K{\times}K$ grid with $K{=}\lceil\sqrt{N}\rceil$ and asks a frozen model the same question about $K$ local spans of consecutive chunks and $K$ strided spans that sample the whole text, reading the log-odds of every answer at one token. Each answer takes its best local and strided score, and scoring every chunk by its two spans gives an evidence map at no further cost, whose peak is the chunk behind the answer. Every probe is about $\sqrt{sc}$ tokens for a text of $s$ tokens and chunk size $c$, so a window of $W$ tokens reaches $W^{2}/c$ tokens at $s^{1.5}$ cost. The map replaces the long read. On LongBench v2, reading only the $K$ chunks the map ranks highest, 9k tokens, matches the same model's best window read across windows from 32k to 1M tokens, and on InfiniteBench, where the median context is 150k tokens, it leads the best window read by 5 points. The same map ranks BRIGHT's long-document corpora with the best NDCG@10 of six methods. Each call caches only one probe, so a 27B model reads 4.5M-token contexts on one 80GB GPU, where a single pass would need 296GB of cache. A long read then needs a GPU that holds the model, not one that holds the text.

</details>


### [44] [General Decision Models: Benchmarking and Insights Beyond Jev](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03935)
*Feiyu Duan,Jiayu Lin,Jia Wang,Jun Xiang,Jialiang Wu,Xinnong Zhang,Hanqi Yan,Siyuan Wang,Zhongyu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出双语基准JEVal（11257实例、36数据集、10领域），评估25种通用决策模型与生成式LLM。发现决策模型在有证据可解析时竞争力强，但专家知识与不确定性估计弱，长时程多步系统中局部决策优势被系统级可靠性失败抵消。提出InnerJev-4B/27B，通过推理到读出自我蒸馏实现单步首token决策，27B在JEVal上与Jev持平且延迟约0.1s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用决策模型（如Jev）在结构化判断与选择任务上相比生成式LLM的可靠性边界，以及当局部决策被组合成长时程、多步系统时其优势与失效模式。

Method: 论文提出InnerJev系列模型，其核心架构为单步首token决策：输入结构化判断或选择任务，模型不生成完整的推理链，而是直接输出决策结果对应的首token。训练采用Reasoning-to-Readout Self-Distillation：先让开放权重的LLM生成自身的推理过程，再将推理结果蒸馏到单次前向传播中，使得模型在推理时无需逐步生成即可完成决策。推理路径上，InnerJev-27B以约0.1s的延迟回答典型查询，并在JEVal基准上达到与Jev相当的性能。整体评测部分则通过JEVal基准（11257实例、36数据集、10领域）和25种模型配置，对比通用决策模型与生成式LLM在证据可解析性、专家知识、不确定性估计、长时程交互及大规模社交模拟中的表现。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "JEVal性能", "value": "InnerJev-27B与Jev相当", "setting": "在JEVal基准上对比"}, {"metric": "典型查询响应时间", "value": "约0.1s", "setting": "InnerJev-27B推理"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出JEVal双语基准：11257个实例、36个数据集、10个应用领域，覆盖多种决策场景。；系统评测25种模型配置（通用决策模型与生成式LLM），从证据可解析性、专家知识、不确定性估计、长时程交互、大规模社交模拟等维度给出对比结论。；提出InnerJev-4B与InnerJev-27B，通过Reasoning-to-Readout Self-Distillation将开放权重LLM自身的推理内化为单步首token决策。；报告InnerJev-27B在JEVal上性能与Jev相当，且典型查询响应时间约0.1s。

Abstract: General decision models, such as Jev, have recently emerged as efficient alternatives to LLMs for structured judgment and selection. But what kinds of decisions can these models reliably make, and how does their behavior change when individual decisions are composed into larger systems? To study this, we introduce JEVal, a bilingual benchmark comprising 11,257 instances from 36 datasets across 10 application domains, and evaluate 25 model configurations spanning general decision models and generative LLMs. Our results show that (1) general decision models are most competitive when decisions can be resolved from available evidence, but weaken when they require specialist knowledge or faithful uncertainty estimation: they can often identify the most likely outcome while substantially overstating its probability. (2) In more dynamic and realistic systems involving long-horizon, multi-step interactions, the advantages of fast local decision making are offset by reliability failures at the system level. on $\tau$-bench, faster local decisions reduce median episode time but lower task success as decision errors accumulate over long trajectories. (3) In large-scale social simulation, decision models approach strong generative LLMs on individual response prediction at substantially lower inference cost, yet remain weaker in user profiling and exhibit larger aggregate estimation errors and systematic bias. Finally, we propose InnerJev-4B and InnerJev-27B, which internalize an open-weight LLM's own reasoning into a single-pass first-token decision through Reasoning-to-Readout Self-Distillation, with InnerJev-27B performing on par with Jev on JEVal while answering a typical query in about 0.1 s.

</details>

*Yifeng Zhao,Hongjun Yu,Shibo Wang,Yunjiao Zhou,Zixiao Zhu,Zhipeng Ning,Kezhi Mao,Junlang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SynLat 提出句法对齐单元（SAU）作为压缩边界，通过答案条件化教师构造渐进式 KEEP/LATENT 目标，训练单一压缩条件学生模型。在 Qwen3-8B/14B 上跨压缩级别均优于最强基线，HIGH 压缩下增益达 7.0/5.5 点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链式思维（CoT）轨迹导致输出token成本过高；在压缩预算受限时，压缩边界的位置至关重要，token级或固定长度边界会割裂短语、公式和局部推导等连贯片段，而step级边界又可能把需要不同压缩动作的内容绑在一起。

Method: SynLat 是一个文本-潜在混合的 CoT 压缩框架。输入为问题和请求的压缩级别，压缩边界先通过句法结构划分为非重叠的 Syntax-Aligned Units (SAUs)，以避免 token 级或固定长度边界割裂短语、公式和局部推导。训练阶段使用一个答案条件化的教师模型，为每个 SAU 构造渐进式的 KEEP/LATENT 目标：KEEP 表示保留原始文本 token，LATENT 表示替换为潜在表示。单一的学生模型以压缩级别为条件进行训练，学习在给定问题与压缩级别时生成混合推理序列。推理时，学生模型仅接收问题和压缩级别，即可直接输出包含文本与潜在表示的混合推理结果，从而在受限预算下保留答案关键信息。训练目标为教师提供的 KEEP/LATENT 序列监督，优化学生模型在不同压缩级别下的生成质量。具体硬件、数据规模和延迟未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "总体增益（MEDIUM压缩级别）", "value": "3.6/2.6 点", "setting": "Qwen3-8B/14B学生模型"}, {"metric": "总体增益（HIGH压缩级别）", "value": "7.0/5.5 点", "setting": "Qwen3-8B/14B学生模型"}, {"metric": "12个任务组聚合中匹配或超过最强评估基线", "value": "全部12个；其中11个严格领先", "setting": "在报告的实际压缩率（achieved-CR）选择协议下，跨两个Qwen3学生规模、Standard-CoT与Long-CoT组、三个压缩级别"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出与句法结构对齐的非重叠Syntax-Aligned Units (SAUs)作为压缩边界；使用答案条件化的教师模型为单一压缩条件学生模型构造渐进式KEEP/LATENT目标；推理时学生模型仅根据问题和请求的压缩级别即可生成混合推理（文本与潜在表示）

Abstract: Long chain-of-thought (CoT) traces impose substantial output-token costs. Under constrained budgets, compression must preserve answer-critical information, making boundary placement central. Token-level and fixed-length boundaries can fragment coherent spans such as phrases, formulas, and local derivations, whereas step-level boundaries can bind content requiring different compression actions. We introduce SynLat, a text-latent CoT framework that aligns compression boundaries with syntactic structure through non-overlapping Syntax-Aligned Units (SAUs). An answer-conditioned Teacher constructs progressive KEEP/LATENT targets for a single compression-conditioned Student, which generates mixed reasoning from only the question and requested compression level at inference. Across two Qwen3 Student scales, Standard-CoT and Long-CoT groups, and three compression levels, SynLat matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated baseline in all 12 task-group aggregates and strictly leads in 11 under the reported achieved-CR selection protocol. Overall gains reach 3.6/2.6 points at MEDIUM and 7.0/5.5 points at HIGH for Qwen3-8B/14B, with larger advantages under stronger compression, particularly on Long-CoT groups.

</details>

(460 additional authors not shown)*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Llama 3 是 Meta 发布的大规模基础语言模型 herd，最大 405B 参数、128K 上下文，原生支持多语言、编码、推理与工具调用，并公开预训练/后训练模型及 Llama Guard 3 安全模型，论文详细披露了训练数据、后训练对齐、多模态组合扩展与广泛评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建并公开一个 405B 参数、支持多语言、编码、推理与工具调用的密集型基础语言模型 herd，并系统评估其质量、安全性与多模态扩展能力。

Method: Llama 3 是一组密集 Transformer 语言模型，最大 405B 参数，支持最多 128K tokens 上下文。模型原生支持多语言、编码、推理和工具使用。训练流程包括：大规模多语言/代码/推理数据预训练；后训练阶段包含监督微调（SFT）、拒绝采样、直接偏好优化（DPO）等对齐技术；同时训练 Llama Guard 3 用于输入输出安全过滤。多模态能力通过组合式方法集成：将预训练的图像/视频/语音编码器与 Llama 3 语言模型连接，在冻结或微调部分模块的条件下进行对齐，使模型在图像、视频和语音识别任务上达到竞争性表现。论文详细描述了数据配比、课程学习、基础设施、并行训练策略、评测协议及安全评估。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MMLU (5-shot)", "value": "与 GPT-4 相当（论文称 comparable）", "setting": "405B 模型，5-shot 评测"}, {"metric": "多模态图像/视频/语音识别任务", "value": "与 state-of-the-art 竞争（论文称 competitively）", "setting": "组合式多模态扩展实验，未广泛发布"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 405B 密集 Transformer 的规模扩展与 128K 上下文窗口训练；多语言、编码、推理、工具使用能力的一体化后训练 pipeline；组合式方法集成图像、视频、语音多模态能力；Llama Guard 3 输入输出安全模型的联合发布；大规模预训练数据配比、课程与基础设施工程细节公开

Abstract: Modern artificial intelligence (AI) systems are powered by foundation models. This paper presents a new set of foundation models, called Llama 3. It is a herd of language models that natively support multilinguality, coding, reasoning, and tool usage. Our largest model is a dense Transformer with 405B parameters and a context window of up to 128K tokens. This paper presents an extensive empirical evaluation of Llama 3. We find that Llama 3 delivers comparable quality to leading language models such as GPT-4 on a plethora of tasks. We publicly release Llama 3, including pre-trained and post-trained versions of the 405B parameter language model and our Llama Guard 3 model for input and output safety. The paper also presents the results of experiments in which we integrate image, video, and speech capabilities into Llama 3 via a compositional approach. We observe this approach performs competitively with the state-of-the-art on image, video, and speech recognition tasks. The resulting models are not yet being broadly released as they are still under development.

</details>


### [47] [Can Agent Harnesses and Inference Engines Hear Each Other? The HEAR Protocol for Agentic LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06597)
*Jiaqi Zhao,Haodong Chen,Jitai Hao,Wei Zhao,Jinghao Pang,Qiang Huang,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HEAR 协议连接智能体 harness 与推理引擎，统一工作流意图与运行时状态，支持缓存感知调度和执行模式选择；在多个智能体基准上实现 1.61× 批处理加速、SCBench 中位 TTFT 降低 2.23×，BrowseComp-Plus 和 DeepResearchBench 端到端加速 1.23×/2.45×，且未观察到任务质量下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有接口无法系统性地连接智能体 harness 的工作流意图与推理引擎的运行时状态（队列、KV 缓存、资源压力），导致工作负载感知的执行受限，服务效率不高。

Method: HEAR 是一个双向 Harness–Engine 配对协议。Harness 侧负责表达智能体工作流的意图与执行需求（如工作流依赖、上下文生命周期、执行目标），Engine 侧则返回运行时状态、能力与执行结果（如请求队列、KV-cache 状态、资源压力、执行能力）。协议将语义定义与优化策略分离，使得不同的协调策略可以在不修改工作流或模型语义的前提下接入。论文实例化了两种策略：在线缓存感知运行时协调（利用 harness 的工作流意图与引擎的 KV 缓存状态进行协同调度）和工作负载感知执行模式选择（根据智能体角色选择执行模式）。推理路径为：harness 通过 HEAR 发送工作流意图与需求，引擎接收后结合当前运行时状态返回能力与结果，并据此进行调度与执行决策。论文未披露具体的模块实现、训练目标或更细的推理流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "batch speedup", "value": "1.61×", "setting": "四个对话与研究智能体基准，内存受限并发服务"}, {"metric": "median time-to-first-token reduction", "value": "2.23×", "setting": "SCBench"}, {"metric": "end-to-end speedup", "value": "1.23×", "setting": "BrowseComp-Plus，工作负载特定配置，无观察到的任务质量下降"}, {"metric": "end-to-end speedup", "value": "2.45×", "setting": "DeepResearchBench，工作负载特定配置，无观察到的任务质量下降"}]}

Conclusion: 提出 HEAR：智能体 LLM 服务的双向 Harness–Engine 配对协议，标准化 harness 表达工作流意图/执行需求与引擎返回运行时状态/能力/结果。；将协议语义与优化策略解耦，在不改变工作流或模型语义的前提下支持多种协调策略（如在线缓存感知协调、工作负载感知执行模式选择）。；面向智能体角色进行运行时协调和执行模式选择，并在内存受限并发服务下验证有效性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly execute complex workflows involving multi-turn reasoning, tool use, and parallel agents. Efficient serving requires decisions that span two layers with complementary information: the agent harness understands workflow dependencies, context lifecycles, and execution objectives, whereas the inference engine observes request queues, KV-cache state, resource pressure, and execution capabilities. Existing interfaces do not systematically connect these views, limiting workflow-aware execution. HEAR, a bidirectional Harness--Engine Pairing protocol for agentic LLM serving. HEAR standardizes how the harness communicates workflow intent and execution requirements and how the engine returns runtime state, capabilities, and outcomes. By separating protocol semantics from optimization policies, HEAR supports diverse coordination strategies without changing workflow or model semantics. We instantiate HEAR for online cache-aware runtime coordination and workload-aware execution-mode selection for agent roles. Across four conversational and research-agent benchmarks under memory-constrained, concurrent serving, HEAR achieves a $1.61\times$ batch speedup and reduces median time-to-first-token by $2.23\times$ on SCBench. Mooncake shows that workflow intent and live engine state provide complementary benefits across load regimes. On BrowseComp-Plus and DeepResearchBench, workload-specific configurations yield $1.23\times$ and $2.45\times$ end-to-end speedups, respectively, without observed task-quality degradation. These results establish HEAR as a reusable coordination substrate for efficient agentic LLM serving.

</details>


### [48] [HERA: Harness-Environment Co-Evolution for Reliable Agentic Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06563)
*Han Luo,Bingbing Wen,Guang Yang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Pan Lu,Lucy Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HERA 提出 harness-environment 共演化框架，通过自动构造可验证的可行/不可行任务对并让失败驱动 harness 与环境共同演化，显著提升 LLM agent 的拒答准确率与可行任务完成率，且 harness 可跨 19 个模型迁移并大幅降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 在复杂工具使用环境中经常无法识别任务不可行、不存在有效解的情况；现有方法针对固定任务集优化模型或 agent harness，泛化到未见失败模式的能力有限。

Method: HERA 由两个核心组件构成。第一，自动构造可验证的可行/不可行任务对：通过受控环境变异，将原本可解的任务转化为需要拒答的不可行任务，从而得到带有可验证监督信号的配对样本。第二，共演化过程：agent 在先前任务上的性能失败被用来驱动 harness 适配，同时生成针对先前弱点的新执行环境与任务，形成 harness 与环境的持续共同演化。评估时，演化得到的 harness 在 held-out 任务上提升 abstention 准确率与可行任务完成率；该 harness 还能迁移到 19 个其他 LLM，无需模型特定优化，并使较小模型以更低估计成本达到更强模型的表现。摘要未披露具体的模型架构、训练目标、优化算法、推理硬件或延迟信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "abstention accuracy", "value": "61.7% → 83.3%", "setting": "held-out evaluation tasks，HERA 演化出的 harness 对比基线"}, {"metric": "feasible-task completion", "value": "68.3% → 76.7%", "setting": "held-out evaluation tasks，HERA 演化出的 harness 对比基线"}, {"metric": "abstention accuracy", "value": "平均提升 15.3 个百分点", "setting": "跨 19 个其他 LLM 的迁移，无模型特定优化"}, {"metric": "estimated cost", "value": "降低约 85%", "setting": "使较小模型达到更强模型性能的估计成本"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 自动构造可验证的可行/不可行任务对：通过受控环境变异将可解任务转化为需要拒答的情形；harness-environment 共演化：利用先前任务上的性能失败驱动 harness 适配，并生成针对先前弱点的新的执行环境与任务；演化出的 harness 可跨 19 个其他 LLM 迁移，无需任何模型特定优化；在 abstention 与可行任务完成之间取得同时提升，并实现显著成本降低

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly capable of acting in complex tool-use environments, yet they often fail to recognize when tasks are infeasible and no valid solution exists. Recent work has formalized this reliability gap as the problem of agentic abstention, and existing approaches typically optimize a model or agent harness against a fixed set of tasks, leading to limited generalization to unseen failure modes. We introduce HERA, a framework for harness-environment co-evolution for agentic abstention. HERA consists of (i) a pipeline to automatically construct verifiable pairs of feasible and infeasible tasks by applying controlled environment mutations that transform solvable tasks into cases requiring abstention, and (ii) a co-evolution procedure in which performance failures on previous tasks are used to drive harness adaptation and generate new execution environments and tasks geared towards previous weaknesses. On held-out evaluation tasks, an evolved harness from HERA improves abstention accuracy from 61.7% to 83.3% while improving feasible-task completion from 68.3% to 76.7%, achieving the highest abstention and feasible-task completion among the compared methods. The resulting best harness transfers across 19 other LLMs, improving abstention accuracy by 15.3 percentage points on average without any model-specific optimization, and enabling smaller models to match the performance of more powerful models at an estimated 85% lower cost.

</details>


### [49] [DGA-Muon: Decoupled Geometry-Aligned Adaptive Scaling for Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06578)
*Wenpeng Zhang,Runsheng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 系统分析 NorMuon 自适应缩放的几何本质，提出解耦几何对齐的 DGA-Muon 优化器，为大规模预训练优化器设计提供理论洞察与改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NorMuon 的行级自适应缩放机制来源不明且可能存在几何错配，需要澄清其自适应性的几何本质并设计更合理的 Muon 自适应缩放机制。

Method: 论文针对 Muon 优化器及其变体 NorMuon 进行分析与改进。NorMuon 在 Muon 的正交化更新基础上增加行级自适应缩放，但论文指出该自适应机制在精确正交化下对方阵和宽矩阵退化为单一全局标量；对高矩阵，其变化仅来自正交化后行能量的非均匀分布。近似正交化时正交性残差引入额外变化，形成‘正交化-自适应性悖论’：正交化越准确，自适应性越弱。论文进一步指出 NorMuon 的刚性行缩放与高矩阵的单边正交结构在几何上错配。基于此提出 DGA-Muon：将自适应缩放与正交化解耦，缩放因子直接由原始梯度计算；根据极因子形状依赖的正交结构进行几何对齐，宽矩阵用行缩放、高矩阵用列缩放；并加入基于和的二阶矩估计、偏差校正以及缩放因子自适应裁剪。论文给出 DGA-Muon 的收敛性保证，并通过实验验证 NorMuon 缩放退化理论和 DGA-Muon 优于 NorMuon。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次系统分析 NorMuon 的自适应性，揭示其源于正交化诱导的几何而非真实优化信息，并提出正交化-自适应性悖论；提出解耦几何对齐的自适应缩放原则：缩放因子直接由原始梯度计算，且宽矩阵使用行缩放、高矩阵使用列缩放；结合基于和的二阶矩估计、偏差校正和缩放因子自适应裁剪，提出 DGA-Muon 优化器并给出收敛保证

Abstract: While NorMuon has achieved strong empirical performance in large-scale pretraining by enhancing Muon with row-wise adaptive scaling, its underlying adaptive mechanism remains poorly understood. In this work, we provide the first systematic analysis of NorMuon's adaptivity, revealing that it originates primarily from orthogonalization-induced geometry rather than genuine optimization-relevant information. Under exact orthogonalization, the adaptive scaling factors degenerate into a single global scalar for square and wide matrices, while for tall matrices their variation arises from the non-uniform distribution of row energy after orthogonalization. Under approximate orthogonalization, the orthogonality residual introduces additional variation into the scaling factors, leading to the \textit{Orthogonalization--Adaptivity Paradox}: more accurate orthogonalization weakens adaptivity. We further show that NorMuon's rigid row-wise scaling is geometrically misaligned with the one-sided orthogonal structure of tall matrices. Based on the analysis of these limitations, we propose two core design principles that a desirable adaptive mechanism for Muon should satisfy. First, adaptive scaling should be decoupled from orthogonalization, with the scaling factors computed directly from raw gradients. Second, adaptive scaling should be aligned with the shape-dependent orthogonal structure of the polar factor, using row-wise scaling for wide matrices and column-wise scaling for tall matrices. By incorporating several other design considerations, including sum-based second-moment estimates, bias correction, and adaptive clipping of scaling factors, we obtain the Decoupled Geometry-Aligned Muon (DGA-Muon) optimizer. We establish convergence guarantees for DGA-Muon and empirically validate both our theoretical characterization of NorMuon's scaling degeneration and the superiority of DGA-Muon over NorMuon.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多轮对话中将已提及但已被拒绝或被取代的内容错误地当作仍然生效的需求，导致任务执行偏离用户最终意图，即“提及即生效”混淆。

Conclusion: Intent-Eval：覆盖工具调用、代码、数据库、数学的受控基准，系统研究演化意图下模型行为，揭示拒绝提议与被取代需求的脆弱性；Intent-OPSD：决策条件化的策略内自蒸馏框架，Teacher与学生同一初始化，冻结Teacher基于匹配用户决策的完整任务提供有效意图监督，学生基于完整对话训练

Abstract: When a large language model handles a multi-turn task and a user proposes a change but ultimately rejects it, the model should continue as if nothing changed. We find a surprising failure: merely mentioning a rejected change can derail task execution, even when the user's final intent remains unchanged. To systematically study language model behavior under evolving user intent, we introduce Intent-Eval, a controlled benchmark spanning tool actions, code, databases, and mathematics. Across diverse tasks, models are vulnerable to both rejected proposals and superseded requirements, consistent with mentioned-as-in-effect confusion: conversational content is treated as active requirements even after it has been rejected or replaced. Accuracy degradation can deepen or persist as interaction continues, highlighting the need to distinguish what has been mentioned from what remains in effect. Building on this insight, we propose Intent-OPSD, a decision-conditioned on-policy self-distillation framework with Teacher and Student initialized from the same model. The frozen Teacher provides active-intent supervision from the complete task matching the user's decision, training the Student on the full dialogue to follow active requirements reflecting user intent.

</details>

*Shouju Wang,Haopeng Zhang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 隐私基准主要基于模拟轨迹和结果导向指标，无法捕捉多步 agent 执行过程中产生的隐私风险，尤其是超出最终响应泄漏之外的不必要信息访问。

Method: AgentPrivArena 是一个用于评估真实 LLM Agent 工作流隐私风险的框架。它整合真实 MCP 工具与自托管服务，构成可复现的执行环境，使 agent 能够在接近实际的工具调用与服务交互中执行多步任务。在此基础上，论文提出轨迹级隐私度量，记录并量化 agent 执行轨迹中不必要的信息访问，而不仅仅分析最终响应是否泄漏隐私。进一步，AgentPrivAudit 作为运行时审计方法，在 agent 执行过程中监控隐私违规。实验部分对最先进的 LLM Agent 进行广泛测试，揭示现有评估范式所忽略的显著隐私风险。由于摘要未披露具体模型结构、训练目标、推理硬件或服务延迟等细节，无法进一步补充。

Conclusion: AgentPrivArena：集成真实 MCP 工具与自托管服务的可复现执行环境，用于评估现实 LLM Agent 工作流中的隐私风险。；轨迹级隐私度量：量化 agent 执行过程中不必要的信息访问，而不仅是最终响应泄漏。；AgentPrivAudit：运行时审计方法，在 agent 执行过程中监控隐私违规行为。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of LLM agents has enabled systems to autonomously perform complex tasks through external tools, but their growing access to personal data introduces significant privacy risks. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate LLM agent privacy through simulated trajectories and outcome-based metrics, limiting their ability to capture privacy risks arising during multi-step agent execution. In this work, we introduce AgentPrivArena, a framework for evaluating privacy risks in realistic LLM agent workflows. AgentPrivArena integrates authentic MCP tools and self-hosted services within a reproducible execution environment. We further propose trajectory-level privacy metrics that quantify unnecessary information access beyond final response leakage. Building on this framework, we introduce AgentPrivAudit, a runtime auditing approach for monitoring privacy violations during agent execution. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art LLM agents reveal substantial privacy risks overlooked by existing evaluation paradigms, highlighting the importance of trajectory-level auditing for trustworthy agent deployment.

</details>


### [52] [Capability-Driven Self-Evolution of Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06361)
*Yaoqi Chen,Yuru Feng,Qianxi Zhang,Baotong Lu,Jianan Lu,Zhirui Wang,Shusen Xu,Zewen Jin,Zengzhong Li,Cheng Li,Qi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PrisMem 提出能力驱动的智能体记忆自演化：将搜索指导从整体性能细化到各能力维度，通过依赖感知的能力选择、历史引导诊断与 trace 引导整合，合并互补的程序修改。在 BEAM-1M 与 LongMemEval-M 上分别超过最强基线 10.54 与 7.83 个百分点，验证了百万 token 历史下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆自演化采用整体式（holistic）进化，将混合反馈汇总为统一修改方向并以整体性能判断进展，导致优化方向被掩盖、能力维度的局部收益被他处回退抵消，有潜力的方向未被充分探索。

Method: PrisMem 以可执行记忆程序为核心对象：这类程序负责从历史交互中存储与检索信息，并通过任务反馈迭代改进。整体流程为：(1) 能力维度分解与选择——不做单一整体性能信号，而是按能力维度评估与维护候选记忆程序，并用依赖感知的能力选择机制挑选与其它能力存在依赖关系、可能产生跨能力收益的目标作为下一轮演化对象；(2) 历史引导诊断——针对被选中的能力专家程序，利用历史演化记录与任务反馈进行诊断，生成细化的修改方向；(3) trace 引导整合——在成对差分案例（paired differential cases）上比较已评估的候选程序，依据两者在同一输入上的行为差异判断各自带来的互补能力增益，进而把互补改进合并成一个统一的记忆程序，同时避免合并后相互抵消。检索/存储路径由被演化出的记忆程序在推理时执行，作用于百万 token 级历史；训练与优化信号来自任务反馈而非梯度式预训练，属于基于搜索与评估的自我演化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对最强基线的提升", "value": "10.54 个百分点", "setting": "BEAM-1M 基准，百万 token 历史"}, {"metric": "相对最强基线的提升", "value": "7.83 个百分点", "setting": "LongMemEval-M 基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 能力驱动演化（capability-driven evolution）：把搜索指导从整体性能扩展到单个能力维度，保留有潜力的修改并扩展超出整体演化边界的探索空间；依赖感知的能力选择（dependency-aware capability selection）：优先选择可能带来跨能力收益的目标；历史引导的能力专家诊断（history-guided diagnosis）：对能力专用程序做精细化改进；trace 引导整合（trace-guided integration）：在成对差分样本上比较已评估程序，利用行为差异将互补收益合并为统一记忆程序

Abstract: Memory self-evolution uses task feedback to iteratively improve executable memory programs that store and retrieve information from past interactions. Existing approaches typically adopt holistic evolution, deriving revision directions from mixed feedback and judging progress by overall performance. This can obscure optimization directions and hide capability-specific gains offset by regressions elsewhere, leaving promising directions underexplored. We introduce capability-driven evolution, which extends search guidance from overall performance to individual capability dimensions, preserving promising revisions and expanding exploration beyond the boundaries of holistic evolution. We propose PrisMem, which uses dependency-aware capability selection to prioritize targets with potential cross-capability benefits and history-guided diagnosis to refine capability specialists. Trace-guided integration compares evaluated programs on paired differential cases, using their behavioral differences to consolidate complementary gains into a unified memory program. Experiments show that PrisMem outperforms the strongest baselines by 10.54 and 7.83 percentage points on BEAM-1M and LongMemEval-M, respectively, demonstrating its effectiveness on million-token histories.

</details>


### [53] [ImproveAnyTask: An Autonomous Post-Training Harness for Iterative Model Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06347)
*Xingbo Yao,Xiaoman Wang,Zhengwu Lei,Tinghui Luo,YiLin Zhang,Yuefeng Wu,Yijie Xu,Tianfu Wang,Qingyuan Zhan,Ye Guo,Daoxin Zhang,Zhe Xu,Jian Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ImproveAnyTask，一个自主后训练 harness，通过错误归因、策略选择、可执行训练更新三步闭环，在有限预算下自动提升 LLM 在特定任务上的表现。在 11 个任务上，Base 模型平均提升 18.29 个百分点，Instruct 模型平均提升 11.97 个百分点，最高提升 41.96 个百分点，预算为 24 小时、8 张 H20 GPU 等效资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限计算预算下，如何自动化地将通用大语言模型适配到特定任务，并持续迭代改进，同时避免因模型更新导致错误分布变化而需要反复人工调整策略。

Method: ImproveAnyTask 是一个自主后训练 harness，受梯度参数优化启发，将适配过程组织为三个迭代阶段：(1) 错误归因：对当前模型在目标任务上的表现进行指标级和案例级分析，识别出需要优先解决的焦点问题；(2) 更新方向选择：搜索有研究支持的策略，比较其报告的性能增益和复现难度，选择最适合当前焦点问题且易于复现的策略；(3) 可执行模型更新：将选定的策略转化为训练数据和训练配置，先执行小规模训练检查以验证可行性，再运行完整的后训练。后续评估用于指导模型选择并触发下一轮适应。经过验证的策略和脚本会被保存以便复用。该闭环在有限计算预算下不断迭代，从而应对模型更新后错误分布变化的问题，实现持续自我改进。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均性能提升", "value": "18.29 个百分点", "setting": "Base 模型，11 个任务，24 小时预算，8 张 H20 GPU 等效资源"}, {"metric": "平均性能提升", "value": "11.97 个百分点", "setting": "Instruct 模型，11 个任务，24 小时预算，8 张 H20 GPU 等效资源"}, {"metric": "最大性能提升", "value": "41.96 个百分点", "setting": "11 个任务中单个任务，24 小时预算，8 张 H20 GPU 等效资源"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 受梯度优化启发的自动化后训练框架，组织为错误归因、更新方向选择、可执行模型更新三个阶段。；结合指标级与案例级分析自动定位焦点问题。；自动调研研究支持的策略，并比较其报告增益与复现难度以选择更新方向。；将选定策略转化为训练数据与训练配置，先小规模执行检查再进行完整后训练。；保存已验证的策略和脚本以供后续复用，形成持续改进闭环。

Abstract: Adapting general-purpose large language models to specific tasks requires substantial human effort in designing data and training strategies. Sustaining improvement is especially challenging because model updates change the error distribution, requiring strategies to be continually refined. We introduce ImproveAnyTask, an autonomous post-training harness that improves task performance under a limited compute budget. Drawing inspiration from gradient-based parameter optimization, the harness organizes adaptation into error attribution, update-direction selection, and executable model updates. It combines metric-level and case-level analysis to identify a focal problem, then investigates research-backed strategies and compares their reported gains and reproduction difficulty. The selected strategy is translated into training data and a training configuration, with small-scale execution checks preceding full post-training. Subsequent evaluation guides model selection and further adaptation, while validated strategies and scripts are retained for reuse. Across 11 tasks, ImproveAnyTask achieves mean gains of 18.29 and 11.97 percentage points on the Base and Instruct models, respectively, with a maximum gain of 41.96 points, under a 24-hour budget with resources equivalent to eight H20 GPUs.

</details>


### [54] [Evolving in Thought Space: Training a Small Model at Test Time Unlocks Better Discoveries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06269)
*Chonghe Jiang,Ao Qu,Siyuan Liu,Ruoyun Ma,Zijian Zhou,Dingyi Zhuang,Bo Liu,Han Zheng,Hanfei Yu,Baichuan Mo,Jinhua Zhao,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Guidance-TTT 把测试时训练的职责拆开：小型引导模型只学高层策略修改并用自适应组相对强化学习在线更新，冻结的强执行模型负责把策略落地成可执行解。无需网络访问即在组合优化、启发式编程、机器学习与 GPU 内核优化四类任务上超过先前最佳解，并与公开排行榜 SOTA 保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的开放式发现系统在测试时训练时，若执行模型很大，则需维护梯度、优化器状态与策略统计并反复生成结构化长输出，代价高；同时结果级验证反馈难以同时归因于高层策略与底层实现。

Method: Guidance-TTT 将测试时训练系统拆分为两个角色。输入是目标问题的初始解群体与验证器反馈。小型引导模型（guidance model）以短策略决策为输出，负责提出对某个已发现解的高层战略修改；冻结的执行模型（execution model）接收该战略修改，生成完整可执行解。系统每一步先从先前已发现的解中选择一个较有希望的候选，由引导模型提出变更，再交由执行模型实现为完整可执行方案，然后执行并用验证器评估。只有引导模型使用自适应组相对强化学习目标进行更新：组内相对比较来自验证器结果级反馈，从而把信用分配给短高层决策而非长实现细节。执行模型始终冻结，保留其强实现能力，避免大量长结构化输出反传带来的梯度、优化器状态与策略统计开销。推理/测试循环即上述选择—提议—实现—执行—验证—更新过程，直到在四个领域任务上产出解。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "解质量", "value": "在 Polyomino Packing、AHC058、Lasso、TriMul 四个任务上优于先前工作报告的最佳解，并与公开在线排行榜上的 SOTA 结果保持竞争力", "setting": "无网络访问条件下，测试时训练求解四个领域任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将测试时训练中的高层策略决策与底层可执行实现解耦：小型引导模型负责提出战略变更，冻结的强执行模型负责实现完整可执行方案；仅对小型引导模型使用自适应组相对强化学习目标进行测试时更新，聚焦短策略决策，避免对强执行模型进行昂贵适配；每步从先前发现的解中选择有希望的候选、提出变更、执行验证并更新引导模型，形成闭环的测试时发现流程

Abstract: Open-ended scientific discovery often requires repeatedly proposing and evaluating candidate solutions. LLM-based systems can support this process by generating and refining executable solutions from verifier feedback. Methods such as TTT-Discover use test-time training (TTT) to update the solution-generating LLM from verifier feedback, adapting its generation policy to improve subsequent proposals on the target problem. However, this becomes expensive when reliable execution requires a large model, since training must maintain gradients, optimizer states, and policy statistics while repeatedly generating long, structured outputs. It also complicates credit assignment: outcome-level verifier feedback must jointly evaluate the high-level strategy and its low-level implementation. In this work, we introduce Guidance-TTT, which separates these roles. A compact guidance model is trained at test time to propose high-level strategic changes, while a frozen execution model implements them as complete executable solutions. At each step, the system selects a promising previously discovered solution, proposes a change, executes and verifies it, and updates only the guidance model using an adaptive group-relative RL objective. This concentrates test-time learning on short strategic decisions while retaining the implementation capability of a substantially stronger model without adapting it. Without web access, Guidance-TTT produces strong solutions across four distinct domains: combinatorial optimization (Polyomino Packing), heuristic programming (AHC058), machine learning (Lasso), and GPU kernel optimization (TriMul). Across these tasks, it outperforms the best solutions reported in prior work while remaining competitive with state-of-the-art results on public online leaderboards. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [55] [VERA: Scaling Verifiable Environments for Agentic co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05923)
*Junqi Liu,Yongyang Pan,Zhuosong Jiang,Dongbai Li,Bo Zhang,Xitong Ling,Sheng Wang,Hanrong Ye,Yufan He,Can Zhao,Pengfei Guo,Dong Yang,Andriy Myronenko,Yuyin Zhou,Tianyu Liu,Daguang Xu,Yucheng Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VERA introduces a scalable method to build verifiable environments for long-horizon agents, enabling co-evolution of model and harness via rubric rewards and a verifier that gates updates. It releases a 9K+ environment corpus and shows strong gains on AutoCoWorkBench and AutoMedBench, with transfer to unseen workflows.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Long-horizon agent training lacks precise, verifiable environments that can be resumed and evolved from observable evidence; existing environments only score outcomes, making stable training difficult.

Method: VERA constructs verifiable environments from initial trajectories. An agent writes rubrics and executable checks for each environment; a judge verifies the sandbox, and only those that pass enter the training bank. The training loop alternates between two updates: (1) train the model with rubric rewards, and (2) edit the harness skills. A separate verifier gates model checkpoints and harness edits using explicit development-set acceptance criteria, enabling attribution of performance changes to either the model or the harness. The final system consists of an open-source corpus of 9,000+ long-horizon verifiable environments, a model (9B or 27B), and a co-evolved agent harness. Evaluation is performed on AutoCoWorkBench and AutoMedBench, with transfer to unseen workflows and retention of general capabilities.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "score improvement over strongest baseline", "value": "10.3 points", "setting": "9B model paired with co-evolved agent, domain 1 (unspecified)"}, {"metric": "score improvement over strongest baseline", "value": "13.0 points", "setting": "9B model paired with co-evolved agent, domain 2 (unspecified)"}, {"metric": "AutoCoWorkBench score", "value": "71.6", "setting": "27B model, surpasses baseline"}, {"metric": "AutoMedBench score", "value": "80.7", "setting": "27B model, surpasses baseline"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Scalable construction of verifiable environments: agent writes rubrics, executable checks, judge verifies each sandbox, only passing sandboxes enter training bank.；Co-evolution training that alternates between training the model with rubric rewards and editing harness skills.；A verifier that gates model checkpoints and harness edits using explicit development-set acceptance criteria, enabling attribution of updates to cause vs. outcome.；Open-source corpus of 9,000+ long-horizon verifiable environments.；Demonstrated transfer to unseen workflows while retaining general capabilities.

Abstract: Competent agents need precise and verifiable environments, such as sandboxes that are resumable at any stage and evolve from observable evidence. However, most long-horizon work exposes how rare these are: for example, an agent in medical research must ground a finding, classify it, and write a report over dozens of dependent steps, yet recent environments score only the outcome. To address the challenges in stable training, we present VERA, which builds such environments at scale and lets agents evolve on them. VERA builds these environments from initial trajectories: an agent writes rubrics, executable checks, a judge verifies each sandbox, and only those that pass enter the training bank. On these environments, VERA alternates between two updates: train the model with rubric rewards, or edit the harness skills. We also create a verifier which gates model checkpoints and harness edits using explicit development-set acceptance criteria. This attribution distinguishes VERA's co-evolution from single-axis baselines: its updates target not only the cause but the outcome. With an open-source corpus of 9,000+ long-horizon verifiable environments, a 9B model paired with its co-evolved agent beats the strongest baseline by 10.3 and 13.0 points in the two domains. At 27B, it surpasses the baseline on AutoCoWorkBench (71.6) and AutoMedBench (80.7), transfers to unseen workflows, and retains general capabilities.

</details>


### [56] [A Testable Theory of Atomic Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05794)
*Kenny Peng,Jon Kleinberg,Nikhil Garg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出原子特征理论并验证稀疏字典随规模增加恢复特征的前缀性质，支持SAE缩放的有效性，对LLM可解释性有理论和实践意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型表示中是否存在原子特征，以及稀疏字典（如SAE）如何随规模增加恢复这些特征。

Method: 论文提出原子特征理论：假设语言模型表示由原子特征线性组合构成。稀疏字典（如SAE）在不同规模下训练，其恢复的特征会形成嵌套序列，即较小SAE恢复的特征是较大SAE恢复特征的子集。理论推导得出恢复原则：随着字典规模增加，SAE能恢复更多最普遍的原子。为了验证，作者训练了从512到131072不同规模的SAE，使用两个大型嵌入模型，并测试了共享特征、跨数据泛化和父子特征恢复等预测。实验表明这些预测与理论一致，支持原子特征理论的有效性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出原子特征理论，推导出稀疏字典恢复最普遍原子的前缀特性（恢复原则）；给出三个可测试预测：小SAE特征被大SAE共享、跨数据SAE共享共有特征、大SAE恢复父子特征；在512到131072规模的SAE上验证预测，挑战了SAE特征不稳定和分裂的传统观点

Abstract: We develop and test a theory of language model representations in which there exist atomic features. Our main theoretical insight is that in such a model, sparse dictionaries (e.g., SAEs) of increasing size recover an increasing prefix of the most prevalent atoms in the training data. This "recovery principle" yields three testable predictions: many features in small SAEs are shared by all larger SAEs, SAEs trained on different data share features prevalent in both, and sufficiently large SAEs recover both parent and child features. In contrast to conventional wisdom that SAE features are unstable and "split" as size increases, we find that these predictions hold on SAEs of sizes ranging from 512 to 131,072 trained on two large embedding models. From a theoretical perspective, our results suggest the promise of a scientific theory of representations based on atomic features. Practically, our results suggest the promise of scaling SAEs.

</details>


### [57] [From Token-Max to Outcome-Max: How You Use AI Determines Its Productivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05697)
*Chen Xu,Mengqiao Liu,Beibei Li,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出outcome-max替代token-max，以单位成本下独立验证的任务完成作为奖励，并构建三级模拟框架验证其能提升AI生产效率而不显著牺牲任务表现；进一步提出OutcomeShare激励机制，理论显示可为员工、公司和LLM提供商带来共享收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型中，token消耗被当作有效努力会导致过度计算，损害单位成本的生产率，需要设计奖励‘独立验证的任务完成/单位成本’的目标以诱导合理停止。

Method: 论文未公开具体模型架构或系统实现细节，主要贡献为概念目标（token-max vs. outcome-max）、三级模拟框架（即时交互、长期行为适应、组织协作）和激励机制（OutcomeShare）的理论与模拟分析。三级模拟框架的输入为AI使用交互，主体模块依次模拟个体即时决策、长期策略适应和组织内协作；优化目标为最大化独立验证的任务完成/单位成本，诱导停止规则；推理/服务流程未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AI辅助生产效率", "value": "在三个模拟层级中均提升，同时基本保持验证任务性能", "setting": "三级模拟框架"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出outcome-max目标：奖励单位成本下独立验证的任务完成，并诱导原理性停止规则；构建三级模拟框架：即时交互、长期行为适应、组织协作；提出OutcomeShare激励机制，理论与模拟显示可诱导参与并为员工、公司、LLM提供商带来共享收益

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) models can perform increasingly complex tasks, yet greater AI usage does not necessarily translate into proportional productivity gains. We identify token-max as one source of this inefficiency: when token consumption is treated as productive effort, agents are encouraged to over-exert and expend computation beyond what is necessary. We instead propose outcome-max, which rewards independently verified task completion per unit cost and induces a principled stopping rule. Then, to study these objectives, we develop a three-level simulation framework spanning immediate interaction, long-run behavioral adaptation, and organizational collaboration. Across all three levels, outcome-max improves the efficiency of AI-assisted production while largely preserving verified task performance. To further align these incentives with outcome-max, we introduce OutcomeShare, an incentive mechanism. Theory and simulation show that OutcomeShare can induce participation while generating shared gains for employees, firms, and LLM providers. Together, our results suggest that AI productivity not only depends on model capability, but also on how to construct the objectives governing AI use.

</details>


### [58] [How Should a Prompt Optimizer Spend a Tight Budget? BudgetAPO with Noise-Adaptive Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05671)
*Haoyue Liu,Zhichao Wang,Huanyu Yan,Xiaoying Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BudgetAPO针对紧预算自动提示优化提出噪声自适应评估切片、固定切片配对比较和联合反思算子，在七个基准和五个模型上全面优于GEPA和OPRO，显著降低有偏API调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧预算（250次以下subject-model调用）和支付/限速API限制下，现有自动提示优化方法（GEPA、OPRO）失败：多阶段流水线消耗预算后返回原始种子提示，单阶段方法使用固定大小的小批量评估忽略任务噪声，导致优化无效。

Method: BudgetAPO是一个单阶段提示优化器，专为紧预算场景设计。其架构包含三个核心组件：首先，噪声自适应规则通过短探针对当前任务进行噪声测量，并据此动态确定评估切片的大小，以平衡评估准确性与预算消耗；其次，固定切片将每个接受/拒绝决策转化为配对比较，即在同一固定数据切片上比较候选提示与当前提示，从而降低比较方差并保证决策的可重复性；最后，反思算子同时重写推理策略和输出格式，生成新的候选提示。整个优化过程为迭代式：每一次迭代中，系统先基于当前提示在固定切片上进行评估，并根据噪声自适应规则调整评估样本量，然后通过反思算子生成候选提示，再以配对比较方式决定是否接受。训练目标隐含为最大化预算约束下的最终任务性能，无需更新模型权重。推理路径为直接使用优化后的提示调用subject模型，不增加额外推理开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "返回种子提示的比例", "value": "250次调用时 BudgetAPO 13%，GEPA 86%", "setting": "七个基准和五个subject模型，紧预算设定"}, {"metric": "达到BudgetAPO 100次调用分数所需的调用次数", "value": "GEPA需要4.5倍调用次数（即450次调用）", "setting": "GPT-OSS-20B模型，七个基准"}, {"metric": "排名第一", "value": "在每个subject模型上均排名第一，并在Holm校正的配对检验中击败所有基线", "setting": "七个基准和五个subject模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 噪声自适应规则：通过短探针测量任务噪声，动态调整评估切片大小；固定切片设计：将每个接受/拒绝决策转化为配对比较，减少方差；反思算子：联合重写推理策略和输出格式，而非仅修改提示文本

Abstract: Automatic prompt optimization (APO) has been widely employed to adapt large language models without updating their weights, yielding promising results. However, existing methods such as GEPA and OPRO assume hundreds to thousands of subject-model calls, far more than is practical behind paid, rate-limited APIs. Under tight budgets they fail in two ways: multi-stage pipelines can exhaust the budget and return the seed prompt unchanged, while single-stage methods compare candidates on fixed-size minibatches, regardless of each task's noise. As a remedy, we introduce BudgetAPO, a single-stage optimizer for the tight-budget regime. BudgetAPO incorporates (1) a noise-adaptive rule that sizes the evaluation slice to each task's noise, measured by a short probe; (2) a fixed slice that turns every accept/reject decision into a paired comparison; and (3) a reflective operator that rewrites reasoning strategy and output format jointly. Extensive results across seven benchmarks and five subject models demonstrate that BudgetAPO ranks first on every subject and beats every baseline under Holm-corrected paired tests, while returning the seed in 13% of runs at 250 calls against 86% for GEPA. On GPT-OSS-20B, GEPA needs 4.5 times as many calls to match BudgetAPO's 100-call score.

</details>


### [59] [Visual Grounding Safety in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05637)
*Erfan Shayegani,Kundan Krishna,Yue Dong,Nael Abu-Ghazaleh,Leon Gatys,Shruti Palaskar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文系统分析了视觉语言模型在结构化输出通道（点、边界框）上的安全对齐缺陷，发现模型对有害请求以问答形式能拒绝，但要求接地输出时会遵从。提出了结合拒绝数据、能力数据和自蒸馏良性数据的微调方法，在两个模型上大幅提升接地拒绝率并保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在结构化输出通道（点、边界框）上的安全对齐不足：模型对有害请求以问答形式能拒绝，但同一请求要求接地输出时却会遵从。

Method: 论文研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在生成结构化输出（如点、边界框）时的安全性。通过改造三个安全基准构建了15,401对匹配的有害请求，每对请求仅输出形式不同：自由文本答案（VQA）或接地输出（点或边界框）。测试了五个VLM，发现模型对VQA形式的有害请求拒绝率显著高于接地形式，平均差距为31-59个百分点，且安全系统提示无法弥补。提出的微调方法结合了三类数据：接地形式的拒绝数据、能力接地数据（以保持模型接地能力）和自蒸馏的良性数据（以对抗过度拒绝）。在Qwen3-VL-8B和VisionReasoner-7B上，该方法在VLSU和BBQ-V上提升了接地拒绝率77-95个百分点，在留出的Asimov-2.0领域提升了64-85个百分点，同时提高了VQA拒绝率，保持了接地能力，并限制了过度拒绝。表征分析表明微调使有害请求在表示空间中向模型的拒绝方向移动，对接地输出最为明显，而良性请求仍接近无害参考。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "接地拒绝率提升", "value": "77-95个百分点", "setting": "VLSU和BBQ-V基准，Qwen3-VL-8B和VisionReasoner-7B模型"}, {"metric": "接地拒绝率提升", "value": "64-85个百分点", "setting": "留出的Asimov-2.0领域"}, {"metric": "问答拒绝率", "value": "提升（具体数值未披露）", "setting": "微调后"}, {"metric": "接地能力", "value": "保持", "setting": "微调后"}, {"metric": "过度拒绝", "value": "有限", "setting": "微调后"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将三个安全基准（VLSU, BBQ-V, Asimov-2.0）改造为15,401对匹配的有害请求，仅输出形式（自由文本问答 vs 点/边界框接地）不同；提出一种微调方法，结合接地形式拒绝数据、能力接地数据和自蒸馏良性数据，以缓解过度拒绝；通过表征分析揭示微调使有害请求向拒绝方向移动，对接地输出尤为显著

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly trained to generate structured outputs like points and bounding boxes that downstream interfaces, agents, and robots can act on, yet safety alignment of this output channel has not been systematically analyzed. We study visual grounding safety by repurposing three safety benchmarks spanning direct harm (VLSU), social bias (BBQ-V), and situational safety (Asimov-2.0) into 15,401 matched pairs of harmful requests that differ only in the requested output: a free-text answer (VQA) or a grounding (point or bounding box). Across five VLMs, models that refuse a harmful request posed as a question often comply when the same request asks for a grounding: averaged over models, grounding refusal trails VQA refusal by 31-59 percentage points, depending on the domain, and safety system prompts do not close this gap. We propose a fine-tuning approach that combines grounding-form refusals with capability grounding data and self-distilled benign data to counter over-refusal. For Qwen3-VL-8B and VisionReasoner-7B, it improves grounding refusal by 77-95 percentage points on VLSU and BBQ-V and by 64-85 points on the held-out Asimov-2.0 domain, while also improving VQA refusal, preserving grounding capability, and keeping over-refusal limited. Representation analysis shows that fine-tuning moves harmful requests toward each model's refusal direction, most strongly for grounding, while leaving benign requests near the harmless reference.

</details>


### [60] [SkillGATE: Gate-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search for Skill Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05489)
*Rongchen Zhao,Yu Chen,Yanming Yang,Shijia Xu,Juyuan Wang,Jin Xu,Zibin Zheng,Jingping Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillGATE 面向大规模外部技能库的技能检索（SR），提出图引导层次索引与 Gate-Aware MCTS，将区域导航与技能选择统一为自适应信息觅食过程，在六个 SR 基准上使多种检索/重排骨干持续提升，总体 R@1 较最强 retriever-based baseline 提升 16.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着外部技能库规模与多样性增长，Skill Retrieval 需要从大规模技能库中识别最相关技能；现有方法要么独立排序技能，要么依赖预定义图传播和层次路由，容易受到语义干扰、局部最优和早期路由错误影响。

Method: SkillGATE 将技能检索建模为自适应信息觅食过程，核心是在区域级导航与技能级选择之间进行协调。首先构建一个保留图结构的层次索引，将技能库组织为可导航的层次化区域，同时保留技能之间的图关系。检索时运行 Gate-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search：selection 步骤使用 G-PUCT 根据搜索统计与门控信息选择动作；expansion 步骤探索新的区域以扩大搜索范围；simulation 步骤对候选技能进行评估；backpropagation 步骤将评估结果回传并更新搜索统计。通过这种选择-扩展-模拟-回传循环，SkillGATE 能够根据过程中观察到的效用与不确定性自适应地调整探索与利用，从而缓解语义干扰、局部陷阱和早期路由错误。实验在六个 SR 基准上进行，验证其对多种检索和重排骨干的提升，总体 R@1 较最强 retriever-based baseline 提升 16.3%。更细的输入表示、训练目标、门控机制实现及具体损失函数在摘要中未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "overall R@1", "value": "提升 16.3%", "setting": "六个 SR 基准上，与最强 retriever-based baseline 相比"}, {"metric": "diverse retrieval and reranking backbones 的提升", "value": "持续提升", "setting": "六个 SR 基准上的多种检索与重排骨干"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将 Skill Retrieval 建模为自适应信息觅食过程，根据搜索过程中观察到的效用和不确定性协调区域级导航与技能级选择。；提出 SkillGATE，一种图引导的层次化检索框架，构建保留图结构的层次索引。；引入 Gate-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search（MCTS），通过 selection、expansion、simulation、backpropagation 进行自适应检索。；提出 G-PUCT 指导动作选择，并设计扩展、模拟与回传模块分别用于探索新区域、评估候选技能和更新搜索统计。

Abstract: Skill Retrieval (SR) aims to identify the most relevant skills from external skill libraries, and becomes increasingly challenging as libraries grow in scale and diversity. Existing methods either rank skills independently or rely on predefined graph propagation and hierarchical routing, making them vulnerable to semantic distractors, local trapping, and early routing errors. We formulate SR as an adaptive information-foraging process that coordinates region-level navigation with skill-level selection according to the utility and uncertainty observed during search. Based on this formulation, we propose SkillGATE, a graph-guided hierarchical retrieval framework with Gate-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). SkillGATE constructs a graph-preserving hierarchical index and performs adaptive retrieval through selection, expansion, simulation, and backpropagation. G-PUCT guides action selection, expansion explores new regions, simulation evaluates candidate skills, and backpropagation updates search statistics. Experiments on six SR benchmarks show that SkillGATE consistently improves diverse retrieval and reranking backbones, achieving a 16.3\% improvement in overall R@1 over the strongest retriever-based baseline. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [61] [Hallucination Across the Reasoning Lifecycle: Interface Visibility, Causal Evidence, and Release Control in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05472)
*Zhe Yu,Mohan Li,Lei Yu,Ka-Ho Chow,Chengwei Qin,Xingyu Wu,Wenpeng Xing,Shuguang Xiong,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一篇关于推理型LLM幻觉的系统综述，提出UIPCA分类框架，综合312篇论文与58个来源，强调干预措施在提升推理与保持事实可靠性之间的证据缺口，并将诊断连接到验证、修复与选择性释放控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何观测、诊断并控制推理型大语言模型在推理生命周期中传播的幻觉，将诊断证据连接到验证、修复与选择性释放等纠正措施。

Method: 该论文为综述，不提出具体模型架构。其核心贡献是UIPCA分类体系：U（无依据前提）记录缺乏证据支持的推理前提；I（无效推理）标识逻辑上不成立的推理步骤；P（依赖复用）追踪错误在被后续决策或记忆复用时产生的传播；C（可见一致性）评估最终答案与推理轨迹在可见层面是否一致；A（行动策略失败）捕获因推理错误导致的工具调用或行动策略失效。综述围绕三个问题组织证据：哪些现象可观测、哪些研究设计能确立因果关系、证据支持哪些纠正行动；并将诊断输出连接到验证、修复、选择性释放以及记忆、工具使用与训练反馈回路中的持久状态控制。全文不涉及具体的模型参数、训练数据、硬件或推理延迟等部署细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出UIPCA分类框架，统一记录无依据前提、无效推理、依赖复用、答案-推理链可见一致性、行动策略失败五类推理幻觉证据；系统梳理312篇论文与一手机报告，围绕可观测证据、研究设计效力与纠正行动证据三条主线进行综合；指出在58个受审查来源中，没有任何对比能证明特定干预在提升推理能力的同时降低事实可靠性（匹配条件下）；将幻觉诊断与验证、修复、选择性释放及记忆/工具/训练反馈中的持久状态控制相连接

Abstract: Reasoning errors can propagate into later decisions and memory.
