  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定语言模型的情况下，生产级编码智能体 harness（系统提示、工具集、上下文管理）对任务通过率和推理成本的影响到底有多大，以及现有基准测试能否可靠地检测这些差异。

Method: 论文将编码智能体定义为语言模型加 harness，harness 包含系统提示、工具集和上下文管理。实验流程：在 SWE-bench Verified 上，将五个固定模型分别放入三个生产 harness（Claude Code、mini-SWE-agent、OpenCode）中运行。每个 harness 在每一步向模型发送固定的系统提示和工具 schema 前缀，然后根据模型输出执行工具调用并更新上下文。通过重复运行相同配置来估计分数噪声。分析对比不同 harness 的通过率和每任务成本，成本拆分为初始调用开销（系统提示和工具 schema token 数）、每步增长和每次工具调用的输出差异。没有训练过程，也没有部署在线系统。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 对三个生产 harness 和五个模型在 SWE-bench Verified 上进行受控对比实验，并重复运行以校准噪声。；发现 harness 对通过率的影响大多与重复运行的噪声相当，唯一显著的 harness 效应是 OpenCode 的负向差距（最多 9 分）。；量化 harness 对成本的影响：相同模型和任务下，单任务成本可相差最多 3 倍，主要由系统提示和工具 schema 的初始 token 数决定。；评估了 harness 对比所需的统计功效：45 个任务仅能以 50% 概率检测到 13 分差距，447 个任务才能分辨 5 分差距。

Abstract: A coding agent is a language model wrapped in a harness: the system prompt, the tool set, and the context management that turn a chat model into something that can work inside a repository. Production harnesses ship releases daily, vendors advertise pass-rate gains from harness changes, and leaderboards mix harnesses freely. What is rarely measured is how much the harness itself moves the score when the model is held fixed. We run five models through three production harnesses, Claude Code, mini-SWE-agent, and OpenCode, on SWE-bench Verified, and rerun the same configurations to calibrate how much a score moves when nothing changes but the run. On 447 tasks and the two models we ran there, Claude Code and mini-SWE-agent, the heaviest and the lightest harness, are equivalent within five points. On a 45-task hard subset and five models, swapping the harness flips as many tasks as rerunning the same harness, 13% in both cases, and the tasks a harness wins in one run are not the tasks it wins in the next. The one harness effect that clears the noise is a loss, not a gain: OpenCode trails by up to 9 points on the large pool, and on one model half of that gap sits in runs its output cap cut short. What the harness does decide is the bill. With the same model, the same tasks, and one price list, cost per task differs by up to 3x across harnesses. The gap is set at the first call, by the preamble of system prompt and tool schemas each harness sends with every step, and scaled by the number of steps; per-step growth and per-call tool output differ far less. The provider's price for cached input scales the bill and does not reorder it. The rerun data also give the resolution a harness comparison needs: at the discordance we observe, 45 tasks catch a 13-point gap only half the time and no gap with 80% power, and 447 tasks resolve 5 points, still coarser than the gains many harness changes claim.

</details>


### [89] [Asking Earns Nothing: Scoring the Decision to Act in BFCL Multi-Turn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04429)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计 BFCL 多轮基准的评分器：该基准有两个类别本应奖励“缺信息时先提问”，但 gold 轨迹在该轮为空且被 checker 跳过，导致提问不得分、乱猜不受罚、问两次反而失分。作者利用基准自带的 should-ask/base 配对（同一轮一次完整一次缺失）构造 223 对二元决策，直接从存储轨迹判读“是否发起世界改变型调用”，无需 LLM judge，并让“总是行动/总是提问”两种退化策略都得 50 分。结果显示 gpt-5.4 决策准确率最高（80.7%），官方分却排第六；一句“不要提问”的提示让官方分涨 13.5–23.5 分而决策准确率无可检测变化，说明榜单分数跟随的是行动倾向而非决策质量。作者发布配对数据、无需 API key 的轮级评分器与 31 条人工核验坏样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BFCL 多轮基准的官方评分器在“本应提问”的轮次上不检查模型行为（gold trajectory 为空、checker 跳过该轮、脚本用户无法回答），导致提问无收益、该轮乱猜无代价、重复提问反而失分，评出的榜单分数无法反映模型“是否该行动”的关键决策能力。

Method: 本文不是新模型架构论文，而是对 BFCL multi-turn 评测流程的评分架构审计与替代评分器设计。输入侧：以 BFCL 基准的 should-ask 项与 base 项构成配对，配对定义为“同一请求、同一轮索引，一次信息完整、一次被抽掉一条信息”。参考轨迹与模型输出轨迹均从 BFCL 输出目录读取。核心模块一为配对构造器：利用基准自带的对照实验结构，把 base 项及其信息删减孪生项配成一对，共 223 对，每对对应一个二元决策点。核心模块二为轮级决策判别器：不调用 LLM judge，直接解析存储轨迹，判定模型在该轮是否尝试发起“世界改变型调用”；对完整轮记“应行动”，对不完整轮记“应提问”，每对仅评一个决策，从而把“总是行动”和“总是提问”两种退化策略都锚定在 50 分。核心模块三为对照干预分析：在提示词层面加入“不要提问”（推模型行动）或“先提问”（推模型先问）的单行指令，比较决策准确率与官方 BFCL 整体分数两条曲线的走向。训练目标/优化：本文不训练模型，其“优化目标”是构造一个对行动偏好不可作弊、无 API 依赖、可复现的评测指标。推理/服务流程：评分器可直接在任意 BFCL 输出目录上离线运行，不需要 API key、不需要重新调用被测模型，用于揭示官方 scorer 与真实决策质量之间的错位，并附带 31 条人工核验的坏样本以说明官方评分的失效实例。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "决策准确率（decision accuracy）", "value": "gpt-5.4 达 80.7%，七模型中最高", "setting": "223 对 should-ask/base 配对项上，从存储轨迹直接判读，无 LLM judge"}, {"metric": "gpt-5.4 在完整轮发起调用的比例", "value": "83.4%", "setting": "同一配对中的完整信息轮（应执行改变世界的调用）"}, {"metric": "gpt-5.4 在不完整轮克制（未发起调用）的比例", "value": "78.0%", "setting": "同一配对中的缺失信息轮（应提问）"}, {"metric": "官方 BFCL 分数变化", "value": "+13.5 至 +23.5 分", "setting": "给 gpt-5.4 添加一行“不要提问”提示后，在两个 should-ask 类别及 base 孪生项上"}, {"metric": "gemma-4-31B-it 决策准确率变化", "value": "+4.5 分", "setting": "添加“先提问”提示后，决策准确率提升但官方分数无增益"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 指出 BFCL multi-turn 四个类别中两个围绕“应提问轮”构建，但该轮 gold 为空且被 checker 跳过，形成“问了没分、乱猜不罚、问两次扣分”的评分缺陷；利用基准自身已有的对照实验结构：should-ask 项是 base 项抽掉一条信息后的配对，同一请求在同一轮索引出现两次（完整/不完整），从而构造同一决策的配对判别任务；提出每对只评一个决策：模型在该轮是否尝试发起世界改变型调用，直接从存储的轨迹读取判定，无需 LLM judge；并指出“总是行动”和“总是提问”策略均得 50 分；跨 7 个模型量化决策准确率与官方分数的错位（gpt-5.4 决策准确率最高 80.7%，官方分却排第六；官方第一的模型在该决策上居中）；通过提示词干预实验证明官方分数随“被推向行动”而非随决策质量的迁移：给 gpt-5.4 加一句“不要提问”后决策准确率无可检测变化，但两个 should-ask 类别和 base 孪生项官方分上涨 13.5–23.5 分；发布配对数据、可在任意 BFCL 输出目录上运行且无需 API key 的轮级评分器，以及 31 条人工核验的坏样本

Abstract: An agent that lacks the information it needs should ask rather than act, and the task definitions of agent leaderboards say so. BFCL multi-turn builds two of its four categories around a turn on which the model is supposed to ask, and its scorer never looks at that turn: the gold trajectory there is empty, the checker skips it, and the scripted user cannot answer, so asking earns nothing, guessing costs nothing on that turn, and asking twice loses the item. The benchmark also contains the control experiment for that decision. A should-ask item is a base item with one piece of information removed from one turn, so the same request appears twice at the same turn index, once complete and once not: on the first the model should make the call that changes the world, on the second it should ask. We score one decision per pair, whether the model attempted a world-changing call on that turn, read off the stored trajectories with no LLM judge; acting always and asking always both score 50. On the 223 pairs that pose this decision, gpt-5.4 attempts the call on 83.4% of the complete turns and holds back on 78.0% of the incomplete ones, the best decision accuracy of seven models at 80.7%; on the same items the official score ranks it sixth and puts first a model that lands in the middle here. One added line telling gpt-5.4 not to ask pushes it toward acting on both sides of the pair, so its decision accuracy shows no detectable change, while its official score rises by 13.5 to 23.5 points on the two should-ask categories and on the base twins; the opposite line, telling gemma-4-31B-it to ask first, improves its decision by 4.5 points and gains no official score. The score moves with the push toward action, not with the decision. We release the pairs, a turn-level scorer that runs on any BFCL output directory without an API key, and 31 manually verified bad items.

</details>


### [90] [AgentPersonaBench: Benchmarking Persona-Driven User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04379)
*Jintao Huang,Yifan Wang,Hongyu Shen,Yi Daniel Lu,Shirley Huang,Minsik Oh,Yewen Wang,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Zhen Xu,Yilan Fan,Zichen Yuan,Ahsan Bilal,Zibu Wei,Sankalp Jajee,Henry Gagnier,Saksham Kapoor,Jicheng Wang,Qianfeng Wen,Yixuan He,Steven Dillmann,Jiashu He,Yucheng Lu,Linqiang Guo,Danyang Zhang,Shi Bo,Raunak Mondal,Haixiang Tang,Weihang Xiao,Allen Nie,Jing Tang,Yueying Li,Yifan Simon Liu,Jianheng Hou,Dianzhuo Wang,Qianyu Zhu,Zhixu Silvia Tao,Zhejian Peng,Zihan Wang,Ishan Gupta,Jinxuan Fan,Wanting Jiang,Shushu Liang,Chenxi Qiu,Yijun Wang,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APB 是一个评估人格条件是否忠实驱动 LLM 智能体行为的基准，通过 survey、chat、web、app 四类递增真实性交互面，从可观察动作验证潜在特质遵循度。基准含 2,460 个任务、867 个特质，经自动审计与专家评审验证。对 20 个前沿模型臂的评测显示，领先模型无提示下完整通过率最高达 84.7%，但跨四交互面全通过率仅 37.9%-64.3%，多属性需求会降低保持率，不同模型家族行为分化明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多评估对话风格或自我报告，无法验证人格条件是否真实地、潜在性地驱动智能体在可观察动作层面的行为；APB 要解决 persona-driven user simulation 的行为保真度评测问题。

Method: APB 的评测流程为：输入是一个完整合成用户画像，其中嵌入至少一个目标人格特质，但目标特质不被显式命名，测试目的也不被披露；智能体需要在四种交互面中执行任务，依次为 survey（问卷式交互）、chat（对话式交互）、web（交互式网页环境）和 app（桌面软件环境）。每个交互面产生可观察动作轨迹，评测端仅依据这些可观察行为而非自我报告来验证目标特质是否被忠实执行。基准包含 2,460 个任务、867 个特质，真值经过自动审计和专家评审双重验证。评测时对 20 个前沿模型臂在无提示条件下运行，统计完整通过率、跨四交互面的全通过率、多属性需求下的保持率，并比较不同模型家族的行为差异。论文未披露模型训练、推理服务、参数规模或硬件资源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "full-pass adherence", "value": "最高 84.7%", "setting": "20 个前沿模型臂，无提示且不披露测试的条件下，领先模型的完整通过遵循率"}, {"metric": "four-surface full-pass adherence", "value": "37.9%-64.3%", "setting": "同时通过 survey、chat、web、app 四个交互面的完整通过率范围"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将每个目标人格特质嵌入完整合成画像中，不显式命名目标特质且不透露测试意图，以评估潜在人格遵循度。；通过四个递增真实性的交互面（survey、chat、web 交互环境、app 桌面软件环境）从可观察行为严格验证遵循度。；构建 2,460 个任务、覆盖 867 个特质的基准，并通过自动审计与专家评审验证。；系统评测 20 个前沿模型臂，揭示跨交互模态的遵循度下降、多属性需求下的保持退化以及模型家族间的行为分化。

Abstract: We introduce AgentPersonaBench (APB), a benchmark evaluating whether persona conditioning faithfully steers downstream agent behavior. While language models are increasingly deployed for persona-driven user simulation, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate conversational styling or self-reports rather than authentic behavioral fidelity. APB evaluates latent persona adherence one trait at a time, embedding each target trait within a complete synthetic profile without explicitly naming the trait or disclosing the test. Ground-truth adherence is verified strictly from observable actions across four interaction surfaces of increasing realism: survey, chat, web (interactive web environments), and app (desktop software environments). APB comprises 2,460 tasks spanning 867 traits, verified through automated audits and expert review. Our evaluation of 20 frontier model arms demonstrates that high-fidelity user simulation is already attainable: leading models achieve up to 84.7% full-pass adherence under unprompted conditions. At the same time, APB identifies clear behavioral boundaries: adherence drops across interaction modalities (only 37.9-64.3% pass all four surfaces), multi-attribute demands degrade retention, and competing model families exhibit pronounced behavioral divergence.

</details>


### [91] [Do Tool Calls Execute as Intended? Measuring and Repairing Intent-Execution Correspondence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04375)
*Boyang Yang,Zhenhao Li,Ziyao Yang,Kanghui Jia,Xin Yin,Mingmou Liu,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文定义并度量 LLM Agent 工具调用的 intent-execution correspondence（IEC）：调用在到达执行程序前经过多个 hop，任一 hop 都可能在无提示下改变调用内容（Claude Code Bash 工具改变 12.0% 携带代码/转义/长文本的调用，10/10 被测 harness 均会改变调用），且 80.7% 的反斜杠变更会在无报错情况下执行错误动作。作者提出逐跳观测协议并用各 hop 自身解析器定位首个变更点，构建 IEC-Bench，并发布 IntAct：以该 hop 无法篡改的形式投递或拒绝调用，在商业产品中部署恢复 79.2% 的失败调用；同时指出轨迹式归因把 95.1% 的生产失败错误归咎于 LLM，路径使每通过任务的 token 成本上升 2.4 倍（最高 12.3 倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 发出的工具调用在到达执行程序前需经过多个 hop，任一 hop（如 harness 的 Bash 工具、shell 解析器）可能在无提示情况下改变调用内容，导致实际执行的动作与调用意图不符；现有基准与轨迹式失败分析看不到中间 hop 的变更，错误地把失败归因给 LLM。

Method: 系统由观测协议、修复组件 IntAct 与基准 IEC-Bench 三部分构成。观测协议层面：对 Agent 发出的每一个工具调用，不实际执行该调用，而是在调用链的每个 hop 处记录该 hop 实际接收到的字节/文本表示；对每一 hop 使用其自身的解析器（receiver's own parser）对收到的内容进行解析，并与上游发出的调用在工具契约（tool contract）下做语义一致性比对，从而判定『执行动作是否等于发出调用所指称的动作』，并定位第一个改变调用的 hop。IntAct 修复路径：在识别出会改变调用的 hop 后，将调用重新编码为该 hop 无法改动（如规避其转义/截断/重写行为）的形式再投递；若无法保证不被改动，则直接拒绝该调用，从而保证『正确执行或明确拒绝』。数据流：LLM 生成调用 → 观测点在每一 hop 采样接收内容 → 与意图比对/定位首个变更 hop → IntAct 重写或拒绝 → 执行或中止。评测层面：IEC-Bench 由真实使用中观测到的变更样本构建，包含依赖调用链，并在 4 种广泛使用 harness 的执行路径下评估，衡量任务通过所需的 token 成本与失败发现顺序（揭示后续 hop 的隐藏变更），同时用轨迹式归因与逐跳归因做对照分析。论文未披露具体训练目标或模型训练流程，Methods 主要面向工程系统的检测/修复与基准评测。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "每次通过任务的 token 成本倍数", "value": "2.4x（最高 12.3x）", "setting": "IEC-Bench，路径引入变更 vs 无变更基线"}, {"metric": "Claude Code Bash 工具改变携带代码/转义序列/长文本的调用比例", "value": "12.0%", "setting": "47,828 条生产会话中的 shell 调用"}, {"metric": "反斜杠被改变后实际执行错误动作且无任何报错的比例", "value": "80.7%", "setting": "上述被改变反斜杠的调用集合"}, {"metric": "被测量 harness 中会改变调用的数量", "value": "10/10", "setting": "10 个被测 harness"}, {"metric": "轨迹式判断把生产失败归因于 LLM 的比例", "value": "95.1%（实际路径导致超过一半）", "setting": "生产失败归因分析"}, {"metric": "仅在修复首个 hop 后才显现的失败比例", "value": "55.1%", "setting": "某一执行路径"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "失败恢复率（调用已被改变的情形）", "value": "79.2%", "setting": "IntAct 部署于某商业产品中的实际运行"}]}

Conclusion: 定义 intent-execution correspondence (IEC)：执行动作须与发出的调用在工具契约下语义一致；提出不改动调用、不实际执行即可观察每个 hop 收到内容的逐跳观测协议，并由接收方自身解析器定位第一个改变调用的 hop；IntAct：将调用以该 hop 无法更改的形式投递，或直接拒绝该调用；构建 IEC-Bench：基于真实使用中观测到的变更，包含 4 种广泛使用 harness 执行路径下的依赖调用链；发现 hop 变更会隐藏其后续 hop 的变更（55.1% 失败仅在修复首个 hop 后显现），并量化轨迹式归因将 95.1% 生产失败错误归因于 LLM；量化路径对成本的影响：IEC-Bench 上每通过任务的 token 成本上升 2.4 倍，最高 12.3 倍

Abstract: Agents built on large language models (LLMs) build and run software through tool calls. A call reaches its program through several hops, and any hop can change the call without notice. When the changed call fails, the agent retries a correct call, which costs users time and money. Benchmarks and failure analyses do not see the change, because they read the call and its result but not what a hop received. We define intent-execution correspondence (IEC) as the property that the executed action matches the action the emitted call denotes under the tool contract. Our protocol observes what each hop received without executing the call, and names the first hop that changed it by the receiver's own parser. IntAct then delivers the call in a form that this hop cannot alter, or refuses the call. We build IEC-Bench from the changes observed in real-world use, with chains of dependent calls under the execution paths of 4 widely-used harnesses. In 47,828 shell calls within production sessions, Claude Code's Bash tool changes 12.0% of the calls that carry code, escape sequences, or long text. For 80.7% of the calls whose backslashes are changed, the wrong action runs without any reported error. All 10 measured harnesses change a call. Trajectory-based judgment attributes 95.1% of the production failures to the LLM, although the path caused more than half of them. On IEC-Bench, the path raises the token cost per passed task 2.4 times (up to 12.3 times). A hop that changes a call also hides the changes after it, so 55.1% of the failures on one path appear only after its first hop is repaired. IntAct, deployed in a commercial product, recovers 79.2% of the failures with a changed call. Harnesses should therefore be designed and tested hop-by-hop to ensure a correct call executes as intended or is refused.

</details>


### [92] [Safe Context Switching for Agents in the Wild: Mitigating Subspace Interference via Orthogonal Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05219)
*Akash Das,Ishan Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文识别了LLM中顺序任务间的子空间干扰现象，并提出AURA谱正则化框架，通过正交约束推理更新以保护安全对齐，实验显示可恢复23.0%的性能并保持>0.98安全余弦保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序任务（如逻辑推理与安全对齐）在LLM内部表征中发生几何干扰，导致微调逻辑任务时安全性能下降，现有适配方法无法有效解耦。

Method: AURA是一种谱正则化框架，用于在微调过程中保持推理与安全目标的解耦。其核心是估计对齐流形的零空间，并将推理任务（如多步数学和代码生成）的梯度更新约束在该零空间的正交补上，从而避免对安全相关表征的破坏。论文通过实验表明，该方法能够在不牺牲安全性的前提下提升逻辑推理能力，余弦保真度大于0.98。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "干扰惩罚", "value": "23.3%", "setting": "标准微调逻辑任务（多步数学和代码生成）后在对齐基准上的性能下降"}, {"metric": "性能恢复", "value": "23.0%", "setting": "应用AURA后恢复的逻辑任务性能提升"}, {"metric": "余弦保真度", "value": ">0.98", "setting": "与安全状态之间的余弦相似度"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 定义并量化了Sequential Subspace Interference现象；提出AURA（Adaptive Unique Residual Allocation）谱正则化框架；通过估计对齐流形的零空间并将推理更新约束在其正交补上，实现推理与安全性的谱独立

Abstract: Most Large Language Models exhibit a fundamental tension between two sequential tasks, such as logical reasoning and safety alignment. The high-variance internal states required for sophisticated Chain-of-Thought (CoT) deduction can geometrically interfere with latent representations encoding safety constraints. We identify this phenomenon as Sequential Subspace Interference, showing that standard fine-tuning on logical tasks such as multi-step mathematics and code generation can result in a 23.3% interference penalty on alignment benchmarks, substantially weakening the model's safety priors. This Reasoning Drift is not adequately captured by current adaptation methods because gradients for logical tasks are rarely orthogonal to safety objectives. To address this issue, we propose AURA (Adaptive Unique Residual Allocation), a spectral regularization framework that enforces Spectral Independence between reasoning and safety. By explicitly estimating the null space of the alignment manifold and constraining reasoning updates to its orthogonal complement, AURA enables models to improve logical reasoning without compromising safety. Empirically, AURA recovers 23.0% of the lost performance while preserving greater than 0.98 cosine fidelity to the safe state, demonstrating that reasoning and alignment can be effectively decoupled through geometric regularization.

</details>


### [93] [SpecFold: Folding Multi-Branch Redundancy for Faster Speculative Decoding in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04875)
*Chung-En Ho,Weiyu Sun,Cheng-Jhih Shih,He Li,Yong Liu,Yingyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpecFold 针对扩散语言模型多分支推测解码中的多分支计算冗余，提出 token 级残差门控与折叠注意力/FFN 的算法复用，配合 Triton 稀疏多分支执行，在两种 DLLM 系列、五个模型、五个基准上取得最高 1.64x（对 Spiffy）与 1.99x（对 vanilla）吞吐提升，且任务性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多分支推测解码在扩散语言模型中进行验证时存在多分支计算冗余：各草稿分支继承父分支的大部分 token，导致隐藏状态高度相似，仍然重复计算注意力与 FFN。

Method: SpecFold 针对扩散语言模型的多分支推测解码流程做算法-系统协同设计。输入为 DLLM 迭代式分块去噪（block denoising）过程中产生的多个推测分支：主分支与若干草稿分支，草稿分支由父分支继承大部分 token 并额外解掩码少量位置。由于共享大部分 token，各分支的隐藏状态高度相似。算法层：对 token 做残差门控（residual gating），判定哪些 token 的计算可从父分支复用；通过折叠注意力与 FFN（folded attention / FFN）复用父分支计算，同时保留各分支自身的残差隐藏状态，保证输出等价性不被破坏。系统层：用 Triton 实现稀疏多分支执行，将 token 级细粒度复用映射为高效 kernel，减少冗余计算并转化为端到端吞吐提升。该方案与跨去噪步的时间缓存正交，因此可与现有 DLLM 推测策略叠加使用。训练目标、推理硬件与延迟细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "吞吐提升", "value": "最高 1.64x", "setting": "相对 Spiffy，在两种 DLLM 系列、五个模型、五个标准基准上"}, {"metric": "吞吐提升", "value": "最高 1.99x", "setting": "相对 vanilla decoding（普通解码），同上设置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别出推测验证步内的“多分支冗余”这一与时间冗余正交的冗余轴；token 级残差门控（token-level residual gating），选择性复用父分支计算；折叠注意力与 FFN（folded attention and FFN），同时保留残差隐藏状态；Triton 核实现，将细粒度复用转化为端到端吞吐收益的稀疏多分支执行；与现有时间缓存及 DLLM 推测策略正交兼容

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (DLLMs) generate text through iterative block denoising, and multi-branch speculative decoding accelerates this process by verifying a main branch together with multiple draft branches in a single forward pass. While prior DLLM acceleration methods primarily exploit temporal redundancy across denoising steps, we identify a complementary redundancy axis within each speculative verification step: multi-branch computational redundancy. During speculative verification, draft branches inherit most tokens from their parents while unmasking a small set of additional positions, causing large portions of hidden states to remain highly similar across branches. We propose SpecFold, an algorithm-system co-design that exploits this multi-branch redundancy to reduce the cost of multi-branch speculative verification. Algorithmically, SpecFold performs token-level residual gating and selectively reuses parent computation through folded attention and FFN while preserving residual hidden states. Systemically, a Triton kernel implementation translates this fine-grained reuse into end-to-end throughput gains through efficient sparse multi-branch execution. SpecFold is orthogonal to temporal caching and compatible with existing DLLM speculation strategies. Across two DLLM families, five models, and five standard benchmarks, SpecFold achieves up to 1.64x throughput over Spiffy and up to 1.99x over vanilla decoding, while maintaining comparable task performance.

</details>


### [94] [Agent Behavior as Code: Efficient and Robust LLM Agents with Programmatic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04824)
*Peng Qi,Chunliang Lyu,Gang Li,Fabian Chan,Cheng Chang,Ignacio Cases,Will Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ABCAgent 主张用符号程序（可嵌入神经函数的 Python 代码）在运行时完整指定 LLM Agent 的行为，并由 FM Agent 在需要时编辑程序以保持灵活性。由于行为规约不提前绑定输入、执行确定，同一程序可在不同输入上复用，从而在长控制流与参数化任务族上更鲁棒且更省成本。在六个 Agent 基准上，其准确率在 GSM-Symbolic 上达 98.3%（对比 97.3%）、τ²-bench telecom 的 Pass^4 达 71.9%（对比 47.4%），并在复用程序时实现最高 5.2× 更低延迟与 7.0× 更低成本，但论文未披露公司、团队、模型规模与训练资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于基础模型的Agent在实践中存在三类问题：(a) 语义相似任务上行为偏差大，错误会灾难性传播；(b) 每当任务以不同输入重复出现都要重新进行完整FM调用，成本与延迟高；(c) FM上下文与指令遵循能力有限，难以管理不断增长的执行上下文并遵循复杂计划。论文希望用符号程序在运行时完整指定Agent行为，实现不提前绑定变量、执行确定性，同时保留FM编辑程序带来的灵活性。

Method: ABCAgent 的核心思路是把 Agent 的运行时行为从一个由基础模型在每一步临场决策的神经策略，转变为一个符号程序（例如 Python 代码，其中可以嵌入神经函数作为可调用组件）。该程序在运行时被用来完整指定 Agent 的行为：程序中的变量不会提前绑定到具体任务输入，因此同一程序可以以不同输入反复执行，执行路径由程序逻辑确定性决定，避免了神经 Agent 在语义相似任务上出现的剧烈行为偏差和错误级联。为了保持对未见任务或任务变体的灵活性，系统引入一个强大的 FM Agent 负责编写与编辑该行为程序；当任务族变化或需要新能力时，FM 在程序层面进行修改，而不是在每次执行时重新规划。执行阶段由符号解释器按程序控制流驱动，按需调用程序内嵌的神经函数（即仅在程序显式调用处发生 FM 调用），从而把 FM 调用集中在真正需要语义/生成能力的少数节点上，其余流程（循环、条件、状态传递、文件/记录写入等）由确定性代码完成。论文通过六个 Agent 基准评估该架构，其中包括为测试派生程序对任务变体泛化能力而构造的两个基准（augmented GAIA 变体、控制流增强的 WorkArena），并与模型匹配的神经 Agent 在准确率、Pass^4、延迟和成本上对比，验证了在鲁棒性、长控制流任务以及参数化任务族复用效率上的优势。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "GSM-Symbolic 准确率", "value": "98.3% vs 模型匹配神经Agent的97.3%，p = 0.001", "setting": "GSM-Symbolic 基准对比实验"}, {"metric": "τ²-bench telecom 域 Pass^4", "value": "71.9% vs 47.4%，p = 0.0001", "setting": "τ²-bench telecom 域对比实验"}, {"metric": "控制流增强 WorkArena 正确写入记录数", "value": "在每个循环长度上均优于模型匹配神经Agent", "setting": "自建 control-flow-augmented WorkArena 基准"}, {"metric": "GAIA 与 augmented GAIA", "value": "与模型匹配的神经Agent持平", "setting": "GAIA 与 augmented GAIA 基准"}, {"metric": "GSM-Symbolic 无新程序复用解决率", "value": "92.6%", "setting": "不重新编写程序、直接复用已有程序"}, {"metric": "augmented GAIA 变体无新程序复用解决率", "value": "20.1%", "setting": "不重新编写程序、直接复用已有程序"}, {"metric": "GSM-Symbolic 延迟降低", "value": "5.2× 更低延迟", "setting": "复用程序执行场景"}, {"metric": "GSM-Symbolic 成本降低", "value": "7.0× 更低成本", "setting": "复用程序执行场景"}, {"metric": "augmented GAIA 成本降低", "value": "19% 更低成本", "setting": "复用程序执行场景"}, {"metric": "τ²-telecom Agent 延迟降低", "value": "9.5× 更低延迟", "setting": "复用程序执行场景"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将Agent行为完全用符号程序（如带潜在神经函数的Python代码）在运行时指定，行为规约与运行时输入解耦（without premature variable binding），执行确定性；引入强大的FM Agent在运行时编辑该行为程序，从而在不牺牲灵活性的前提下获得可复用、可泛化到任务变体的行为代码；面向参数化任务族的高效复用：同一程序在不同输入上重复执行，避免每次重新完整调用FM，从而降低延迟与成本；构造控制流增强的WorkArena基准与augmented GAIA变体，专门测试派生程序对任务变体的泛化与长控制流下的鲁棒性；在六个Agent基准上与model-matched神经Agent对比，覆盖准确率、Pass^4、延迟与成本多维度评估

Abstract: AI agents based on foundation models (FMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities to perform complex open-ended tasks. However, they face some common challenges in practice: (a) agent behavior can deviate drastically even for semantically similar tasks, leading to catastrophically propagated errors; (b) high cost and latency due to FM calls, repeated in full whenever a task recurs with different inputs; (c) FMs' limited context and instruction following capability confine how well agents manage the ever-growing execution context and follow complex plans. We introduce $\textbf{A}$gent $\textbf{B}$ehavior as $\textbf{C}$ode $\textbf{Agent}$ (ABCAgent), which uses a symbolic program (e.g., Python code with potential neural functions) to fully specify the agent's behavior at runtime, with a powerful FM agent editing that program for flexibility. Behavior is thus specified without premature variable binding, and its execution is deterministic. We evaluate ABCAgent on six agent benchmarks, two of which we construct to test how well a derived program generalizes to variants of the task it was written for. ABCAgent matches a model-matched neural agent on GAIA and augmented GAIA, and surpasses it where robustness and long control flows matter: 98.3% against 97.3% on GSM-Symbolic ($p = 0.001$), 71.9% against 47.4% $\mathrm{Pass}^4$ on the telecom domain of $\tau^2$-bench ($p = 0.0001$), and more records written correctly at every loop length on our control-flow-augmented WorkArena benchmark. For more parametric task families, ABCAgent is also significantly superior in efficiency. Without authoring a new program, ABCAgent solves 92.6% of GSM-Symbolic instances and 20.1% of augmented GAIA variants, which yields $5.2\times$ lower latency and $7.0\times$ lower cost on GSM-Symbolic, 19% lower cost on augmented GAIA, and $9.5\times$ lower agent latency on $\tau^2$-telecom.

</details>


### [95] [Bidirectional Preference Synthesis: Learning Prompt-Conditioned Preferences from Boundary Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04328)
*Junbo Wang,Lidong Lu,Zhuoqun Li,Guiping Jiang,Xiangyu Wu,Tinghai Zhang,Tong Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BPS 通过为已验证的边界失败构造正反两向偏好对（原提示下为 rejected、合成达成提示下为 chosen），在不改变 DPO 目标的前提下显式建模提示依赖。在 Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 上，达成侧排序准确率从 6.8% 提升至 62.3%，原侧排序保持，并在多语言多轮指令遵循等下游评估中与 Forward-DPO 形成区分。方法完全离线、易复现，适合关注 LLM 后训练与偏好数据工程的读者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正式离线偏好流程通常只把模型失败当作原提示下的 rejected 响应，忽略了边界失败（违反给定指令却连贯满足邻近意图/约束）中隐含的提示依赖关系，导致偏好监督不完整。

Method: BPS 是一种数据构造方法，作用于标准 DPO 训练流程之前。输入为经过验证的边界失败样本，即响应违反给定指令但连贯满足邻近意图或约束。对于每个边界失败，BPS 保留常规前向偏好对：原提示 + 该响应为 rejected。同时构造反向偏好对：合成一个“达成提示”（achieved prompt），在该提示下同一响应被视为 chosen。这样同一响应在错误条件下被拒绝、在正确条件下被选择，使提示依赖显式化。训练时仍使用标准 DPO 目标，不引入奖励模型，也不需要在线采样。推理/评估阶段：在 Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 上检查原侧与达成侧的成对排序准确率，并在 held-out crossed anchors 上报告达成侧排序准确率从 6.8% 提升到 62.3%；在 Kimi-K2.6 交叉教师探针下观察到类似偏移；盲人人工审计验证反向偏好方向；下游任务评估覆盖多语言多轮指令遵循、agentic、tool-use、code 检查，用于观察与 Forward-DPO 的区分及能力保留。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "achieved-side ranking accuracy", "value": "从 6.8% 提升至 62.3%", "setting": "Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507，held-out crossed anchors"}, {"metric": "achieved-side ranking shift", "value": "类似偏移", "setting": "Kimi-K2.6 交叉教师探针"}, {"metric": "下游评估表现", "value": "在多语言多轮指令遵循上最清晰地区分于 Forward-DPO，并在 agentic、tool-use、code 检查中保持一致的能力保留模式", "setting": "未披露具体数值与基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Bidirectional Preference Synthesis (BPS)：对每个验证过的边界失败，同时保留原提示下的前向偏好对，并新增在合成达成提示下的反向偏好对。；让同一响应在错误处被拒绝、在正确处被选择，显式建模缺失的提示依赖，而不改变 DPO 目标、不训练奖励模型、不需要在线采样。；在 Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507 上验证：保持原侧成对排序，同时将达成侧排序准确率从 6.8% 提升至 62.3%（held-out crossed anchors）。；使用 Kimi-K2.6 交叉教师探针显示类似偏移，并通过盲人人工审计支持反向偏好方向。；下游评估在多语言多轮指令遵循上最清晰地区分于 Forward-DPO，并在 agentic、tool-use、code 检查中保持能力保留模式。

Abstract: Correction-based offline preference pipelines commonly treat model failures only as rejected responses under the original prompt. This supervision is incomplete for boundary failures: responses that violate the given instruction yet coherently satisfy a nearby intent or constraint setting. We introduce Bidirectional Preference Synthesis (BPS), a data-construction method for standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) that makes this missing prompt dependence explicit. For each validated boundary failure, BPS keeps the conventional forward pair under the original prompt and adds a reverse pair under a synthesized achieved prompt, so the same response is rejected where it is wrong and chosen where it is right, without changing the DPO objective, training a reward model, or requiring online sampling. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, BPS preserves original-side pairwise ranking while raising achieved-side ranking accuracy from 6.8% to 62.3% on held-out crossed anchors, with a similar shift under a Kimi-K2.6 cross-teacher probe. A blind human audit supports the intended reverse preference direction, and downstream evaluations show the clearest separation from Forward-DPO in multilingual multi-turn instruction following, with consistent capability-retention patterns on agentic, tool-use, and code checks.

</details>


### [96] [Suppressing Pressure, Amplifying Evidence: Self-Guided Attention Steering to Mitigate Sycophancy and Stubbornness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04329)
*Yinghao He,Mengyu Xu,Haixiang Sun,Donghan Li,Yibo Wang,Lixu Wang,Kezhen Chen,Chi Li,Chunwei Liu,Bharat Bhargava,Chongyang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出CoPE-Bench评测LLM在用户压力与上下文证据冲突下的谄媚与固执权衡，并给出无需训练的注意力引导方法SPAE，通过抑制压力token、放大证据token来改善模型行为。在五个骨干模型上平均降低压力跟随18.8个百分点，提升联合条件更新5.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在无支持的用户压力下表现出谄媚，而在上下文信息表明需要修正时又表现出固执，且分别缓解一种失败可能加剧另一种失败，如何在不训练的情况下同时抑制用户压力并放大上下文证据的影响。

Method: SPAE是一个无需训练的推理时框架。输入包括用户查询、模型的中性回答（或模型自身判断）以及可能存在的用户压力声明和/或上下文信息。首先，模型利用自身判断（self-judgments）识别与压力或上下文相关的token。然后，在注意力计算中对这些token进行注意力引导：对表示用户压力的token施加抑制，对表示上下文信息的token进行放大。该过程在推理时逐token修改注意力权重，不更新模型参数。训练目标不适用（无需训练）。推理路径为：给定输入，先获取模型自身的相关token判断，然后应用token级注意力操控，最后生成修正后的回答。该设计旨在平衡谄媚与固执：抑制压力可减少不合理的顺从，放大证据可促进基于上下文的合理更新。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "压力跟随降低", "value": "18.8个百分点", "setting": "在五个骨干模型上平均，相对于主对比中最强基线"}, {"metric": "联合条件更新提升", "value": "5.5个百分点", "setting": "在五个骨干模型上平均，相对于主对比中最强基线"}, {"metric": "联合条件更新提升（两轮对话）", "value": "13.2个百分点", "setting": "两轮对话设置，相对于最强提示基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出CoPE-Bench，每个问题包含六种条件（中性基线、正确/错误用户压力、与中性答案一致/冲突的上下文信息、以及错误声明与冲突上下文联合条件），用于量化谄媚与固执之间的权衡。；提出SPAE（Suppressing Pressure, Amplifying Evidence），一种无需训练、基于模型自身判断识别相关token，并通过token级注意力引导抑制用户压力、放大上下文信息的框架。；在五个骨干模型上验证SPAE平均降低压力跟随18.8个百分点，提升联合条件更新5.5个百分点；在两轮对话中相比最强提示基线提升联合条件更新13.2个百分点。

Abstract: Reliable language models should resist unsupported user pressure while effectively using objective contextual information. However, models may exhibit sycophancy by yielding to unsupported user pressure or contextual stubbornness by failing to update their answers when relevant contextual information warrants revision. Evaluating interventions for these failures separately can obscure whether mitigating one failure exacerbates the other. To assess this trade-off, we introduce CoPE-Bench with six conditions per question: a neutral baseline, correct or incorrect user pressure, contextual information consistent with or conflicting with the neutral answer, and a joint condition combining incorrect claims with conflicting contextual information. To regulate the influence of user pressure and contextual information, we propose SPAE (Suppressing Pressure, Amplifying Evidence), a training-free framework that uses the model's own judgments to identify relevant tokens, suppressing user pressure and amplifying contextual information through token-level attention steering. On average across five backbones, SPAE reduces pressure following by 18.8 percentage points and increases joint-condition updating by 5.5 percentage points relative to the strongest baseline in the main comparison. In two-turn dialogue, it improves joint-condition updating by an average of 13.2 percentage points over the strongest prompting baseline. The source data and codes can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [97] [On the Steering Dimensionality of Refusal in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04245)
*Han Wang,Erik Miehling,Dennis Wei,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究语言模型中拒绝行为的激活引导维度，发现安全对齐触发的拒绝是1维可引导，而一般语境的拒绝需要更高维度子空间，揭示了激活几何的上下文依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法假设高层概念可由单一方向中介，但拒绝等行为可能占据更丰富的激活几何，需要确定可靠控制行为所需的最小子空间维度（steering dimensionality）。

Method: 论文未提出端到端系统架构，而是对语言模型激活空间进行干预分析。输入为语言模型的内部激活表示；方法包括加性引导（向激活添加方向向量以诱导目标行为）和方向消融（移除特定方向以抑制行为）。核心是学习或识别一个子空间，其维度即为steering dimensionality。通过在子空间维度递增时观察目标行为控制效果（单调提升至饱和）来表征充分子空间。训练目标并非传统模型训练，而是通过优化或搜索找到最小充分子空间。推理时应用该子空间进行干预。具体模块细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出steering dimensionality概念，定义为可靠控制行为所需的最小子空间维度；通过加性引导和方向消融两种互补干预，表征充分引导子空间；实验发现安全对齐触发的拒绝是1维可引导的，而一般语境下的拒绝即使5维子空间也无法可靠捕获其全部可引导变化

Abstract: Existing activation steering methods often assume that a high-level concept can be mediated by a single steering direction. To support this, two complementary interventions should be achieved: additive steering should induce the target behavior, while directional ablation should suppress it. Yet behaviors may occupy richer activation geometries beyond a single direction, and semantically similar behaviors may be represented by distinct directions. In this work, we study how many directions can reliably control two different types of refusal behaviors: refusal triggered by the safety alignment and refusal in general contexts. Given the limited expressive capability of a single steering vector, we study the general setting of steering subspaces and introduce the notion of steering dimensionality as the minimum subspace dimensionality required to reliably control a behavior. We characterize sufficient steering subspaces that cover the full extent of the target behavior through both the (monotonic) improvement before the sufficient dimensionality, and the saturation beyond it. Empirically, we find that refusal triggered by safety alignment is 1-dim steerable, while multiple distinct steering directions can achieve comparable control. In contrast, refusal in general contexts exhibits substantially richer activation geometry where even 5-dim steering subspaces fail to reliably capture its full steerable variation. Our results reveal that the activation geometry underlying refusal is highly context-dependent and can be substantially more complex than a single linear steering direction.

</details>


### [98] [Asynchronous Is Nearly Free for Evolution Strategies on Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04196)
*William Hoy,Jingxuan Fan,Nurcin Celik,Xu Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次在长时序终端 agentic 编码任务上系统比较同步与异步演化策略（ES）后训练。使用 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 在 Endless Terminals 上验证：自然异步 Async-1 与同步 ES 持平（25.9% vs 25.4%），延迟 10% 队列 4/8 个更新仅下降 1.6/3.1 个百分点，表明 ES 对中等策略滞后有较好容忍度；GRPO 仍更优（29.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 长时序 agentic 后训练中 rollout 生成成为瓶颈：轨迹多轮、完成时间差异大，同步更新屏障导致快 worker 等待慢 worker，而异步 RL 虽可消费先到轨迹却引入 policy lag 与 off-policy 优化问题。

Method: 论文采用演化策略（ES）作为无反向传播的 LLM 后训练方法，以 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 为基座模型，在 Endless Terminals 多轮终端风格 agentic 编码任务上进行训练与评估。训练流程为：模型生成多条 rollout 轨迹（每条轨迹包含多轮交互与环境反馈），ES 基于群体奖励信号更新参数，不依赖反向传播。标准同步 ES 每次更新必须等待整个 cohort 的所有 rollout 完成，因此受 straggler 拖累。本文提出的 bounded-staleness 异步 ES 允许按照 rollout 到达顺序立即消费轨迹进行参数更新，同时将每个轨迹所对应的策略版本与当前版本之差限制在给定上界内（bounded staleness），从而在减少等待时间的同时控制 policy lag。受控实验通过人为延迟每个更新 cohort 中 10% 的轨迹（延迟 4 或 8 个策略更新）来模拟并量化策略滞后，且不使用显式 off-policy 修正。在推理/评估阶段，以 held-out 任务成功率作为指标，在三个随机种子上比较同步 ES、自然异步 Async-1 以及受控延迟调度的表现，并与 GRPO 基线对比。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "held-out success", "value": "25.9% (Async-1) vs 25.4% (sync ES)", "setting": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Endless Terminals 基准, 3 个评估种子"}, {"metric": "held-out success 下降", "value": "1.6 个百分点", "setting": "延迟 10% 更新队列 4 个策略更新，无显式 off-policy 修正"}, {"metric": "held-out success 下降", "value": "3.1 个百分点", "setting": "延迟 10% 更新队列 8 个策略更新，无显式 off-policy 修正"}, {"metric": "held-out success", "value": "29.0% (GRPO)", "setting": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Endless Terminals 基准, 作为对比基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 bounded-staleness 异步演化策略（ES）后训练方法，允许按到达顺序消费轨迹并限制策略滞后；在 Endless Terminals 多轮终端 agentic 编码任务上首次系统对比同步与异步 ES；通过受控调度实验（延迟 10% 更新队列 4 或 8 个策略更新）量化 ES 对策略滞后的容忍度，且不使用显式 off-policy 修正

Abstract: LLM-based long-horizon agentic post-training is often bottlenecked by rollout generation: trajectories span many interaction turns, completion times vary substantially, and synchronous update barriers leave faster workers waiting for stragglers. Asynchronous reinforcement learning which has been adopted in LLM post-training addresses this inefficiency by consuming trajectories as they arrive, but introduces policy lag and off-policy optimization. Evolution strategies (ES) offer a backpropagation-free alternative for LLM post-training, yet it relies on a larger number of rollouts and existing practices have remained largely synchronous. In this short-form paper, we introduce bounded-staleness asynchronous ES and demonstrate it on Endless Terminals benchmark using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. Across three evaluation seeds, natural Async-1 matches synchronous ES, achieving 25.9\% versus 25.4\% held-out success. Controlled schedules that delay 10\% of each update cohort by four or eight policy updates reduce success by only 1.6 and 3.1 percentage points, respectively, without explicit off-policy correction. GRPO performs better overall, reaching 29.0\% held-out success, but importantly our results show that ES tolerates moderate policy staleness with limited degradation, opening possibilities for future improvement of ES-based post-training with asynchronous algorithms. To the best of our knowledge, we are the first to demonstrate the effectiveness of sync and async ES on a multi-turn terminal style agentic coding task.

</details>


### [99] [ALoDLM: Adaptively Looped Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04198)
*Liancheng Fang,Zhuowei Li,Youngeun Kim,Tianchen Zhao,Rajat Koner,Jiaye Wu,Linghan Xu,Xuanbai Chen,Xiang Xu,Zheng Zhang,Jakub Zablocki,Nishant Sankaran,Yifan Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ALoDLM提出token自适应的潜在循环机制，按预测难度为每个未知token分配计算深度，并以条件NELBO联合学习token预测与计算调度；在1.7B和8B规模、11个基准上优于评估的DLM与AR基线，同时保留并行解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）对所有未知token施加统一计算深度，导致与同规模自回归模型相比存在质量差距，即计算难度不匹配问题。

Method: ALoDLM以扩散语言模型为基础，在部分观测的序列上逐步去噪生成。与传统DLM对每个未知位置均匀施加相同计算深度不同，ALoDLM在每个去噪步骤执行token自适应的潜在循环：它迭代地精炼各未知token的潜在表示，并根据预测难度动态分配计算量。当某个token的表示足够确定、可以提交时，该token被反馈为离散上下文，参与后续去噪步骤的条件；尚未解决的token则保留其潜在状态并经过额外的循环传递继续精炼，而不是立即离散化。为联合学习token预测和计算分配，论文将每个token的计算调度（即需要多少次循环精炼）视为潜变量，并推导出条件负证据下界（NELBO）作为训练目标，从而在训练中同时优化预测质量与计算资源分配。推理时，该机制允许简单token尽早提交、困难token获得更多循环计算，同时保留并行解码能力，从而在优化推理引擎下取得质量与效率的折中。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均基准得分", "value": "在11个基准上优于所有评估的DLM与对应AR基线（1.7B和8B两档）", "setting": "1.7B和8B参数规模，11个基准，优化推理引擎下"}, {"metric": "质量-效率权衡", "value": "保留快速并行解码，在评估的AR与扩散模型中取得强权衡", "setting": "优化推理引擎"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用token自适应的潜在循环替代统一计算：在每个去噪步骤迭代精炼潜在表示，并按token难度分配计算量。；已可提交的token反馈为离散上下文，未解决的token保留并继续精炼潜在状态，经过额外循环传递。；将token级计算调度建模为潜变量，推导条件负证据下界（NELBO），联合学习token预测与计算分配。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) enable fast generation by predicting multiple tokens in parallel, but their practical adoption remains limited by a persistent quality gap relative to comparably sized autoregressive (AR) models. We attribute this gap to a computation-difficulty mismatch: within a partially observed sequence, some unknown tokens are easy to predict, while others require substantially more computation. Existing DLMs nevertheless apply uniform computational depth to all unknown positions at each denoising step. We introduce ALoDLM, which replaces uniform computation with token-adaptive latent recurrence. At each denoising step, ALoDLM iteratively refines latent representations and allocates computation according to token difficulty. Tokens ready to commit are fed back as discrete context, while unresolved tokens retain and further refine their latent states through additional recurrent passes. To learn token prediction and computation allocation jointly, we formulate token-wise computation schedules as latent variables and derive a conditional negative evidence lower bound (NELBO). We train ALoDLM at 1.7B and 8B parameter scales. Across eleven benchmarks, ALoDLM outperforms all evaluated DLMs and the corresponding AR baselines in average benchmark score at both scales. ALoDLM also retains fast parallel decoding, yielding a strong quality-efficiency trade-off among evaluated autoregressive and diffusion models under optimized inference engines.

</details>


### [100] [Language Model Activations Inhabit Privileged Error-Correcting Basins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04183)
*Matthew Finlayson,Francisco Pernice,Eric Todd,Amir Zur,Daniel Wurgaft,Fenil R. Doshi,Vasudev Shyam,Matt Feiszli,Satchel Grant,Lucius Bushnaq,Tal Haklay,Usha Bhalla,Matthew Kowal,Thomas Fel,Jack Merullo,Atticus Geiger,Xiang Ren,Owen Lewis,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文探索语言模型激活空间的几何结构，发现激活聚集于多个吸引盆且引导操作可在盆间移动激活。通过自适应调制引导强度跨越吸引盆边界，显著提升跨语言引导效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在激活被扰动（如线性引导）后仍能保持连贯输出，其鲁棒性的内在机制尚不明确；本文旨在探究激活空间中是否存在被动的误差修正机制将激活导向合理区域。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是对现有语言模型的激活空间几何进行分析。输入为语言模型前向传播中的隐藏层激活，通过观察模型层在低维曲线上的作用来探测激活空间的几何结构。分析发现自然激活聚集于多个不同的吸引盆中，这些盆地能够从几何上将自然激活与分布相似的合成激活区分开。在语义引导方向上识别出特征特异性的吸引盆，并观察到线性引导将激活从一个盆移动到另一个盆。基于此，提出自适应调制引导强度的方法，在引导过程中动态调整强度以促使激活跨越盆地边界，从而提升跨语言引导中目标语言token的采样概率。整个流程不涉及训练目标修改或端到端架构变更，而是对激活空间的几何分析和干预策略的优化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "目标语言token采样概率", "value": "显著提升（相对固定强度引导）", "setting": "跨语言引导任务，通过自适应调制引导强度实现"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出模型激活位于多个吸引盆中的几何假设，并区分自然激活与分布相似的合成激活；发现语义引导方向上的特征特异性吸引盆，引导操作在盆间移动激活；设计自适应引导强度方法，通过跨越吸引盆优化跨语言引导效果

Abstract: Language models exhibit remarkable robustness, continuing to produce coherent text even when their activations are perturbed by interventions like linear steering. We hypothesize that this robustness is a result of passive dynamics, i.e., constraining mechanisms in the forward pass that funnel activations toward "good" regions that produce coherent outputs. To investigate these hypothesized error-correcting mechanisms, we probe the geometry of language model activation space by observing the action of model layers on low-dimensional curves. In doing so, we discover that model activations occur within a cluster of distinct attracting basins, which differentiate natural activations geometrically from distributionally similar synthetic activations. Applying this lens to language model steering, we observe feature-specific basins along semantic steering directions, and find that steering moves activations between these basins. To demonstrate the active role of this geometry in neural computation, we show that adaptively modulating steering strength to transport activations across basins improves inter-language steering, significantly increasing the probability of sampling tokens from the target language compared to fixed-strength steering. Our findings establish analysis of activation space geometry as a promising approach to interpreting and controlling language models.

</details>


### [101] [EvalResearchBench: Can AI Agents Design Their Own Evaluations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04184)
*Yaolun Zhang,Tianyi Xu,Yujie Zhao,Jishen Zhao,Qingyun Wu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出 EvalResearchBench（ERB），研究 AI agent 能否在固定时间与 API 预算下自动设计评测：选择/合成任务、实现并在试点中修正 grader，最终冻结可执行评测器。实验覆盖 9 个 researcher、13 个候选模型与 14 个目标基准，最优评测器成对一致性约 75%，低于目标基准间分歧给出的 91% 上限；无单一方法全面领先，开发目标最优者未必在封存目标上最优，人工设计基线仍然很强，且成本最低的评测器一致性最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复运行复杂基准评测成本高、拖慢递归自我改进（RSI）迭代；能否让 AI agent 自动完成评测设计——即在固定时间与 API 预算下选择或合成任务、实现 grader 并在试点测试中修正，最终冻结出可执行评测器，替代人工挑选基准子集或设计紧凑评测套件。

Method: ERB 设定一个自主评测研究流程：输入包括目标材料（target materials）、开发参考（development references）、候选 API 集合，以及固定的时间预算与 API 预算。agent（称为 researcher）在该预算约束下执行四步：(1) 从候选任务中选择或合成新任务；(2) 为所选任务实现 grader（评测打分器）；(3) 在试点测试（pilot tests）中运行并依据反馈修正任务与 grader；(4) 修正后冻结（freeze）一个可执行评测器。冻结后的评测器针对 coding、co-work、reasoning 三类能力输出评测结果。评测协议将每个冻结评测器与 14 个目标基准（target benchmarks）比较，使用分数一致性（score concordance）与成对一致性（pairwise agreement）评估其能否复现目标基准的排序与打分；实验同时区分开发目标（development targets）与对 researcher 隐藏的封存目标（sealed targets）。实验规模为 9 个 researcher 智能体与 13 个候选模型，并与人工设计的公开任务样本基线对比。论文未披露具体模型参数规模、训练硬件、训练数据、延迟或吞吐量等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "pairwise agreement（最优评测器排序候选对的比例）", "value": "约 75%", "setting": "冻结评测器与 14 个目标基准的成对一致性对比，9 个 researcher、13 个候选模型"}, {"metric": "pairwise agreement 上限（目标基准间分歧导致的封顶）", "value": "91%", "setting": "作为目标基准彼此不一致造成的上限"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 EvalResearchBench (ERB)：面向自主评测研究的基准，给定目标材料、开发参考、候选 API 及固定时间与 API 预算，由 researcher agent 选择/合成任务、实现 grader、试点修正后冻结可执行评测器；覆盖 coding、co-work、reasoning 三类场景，系统评测 9 个 researcher 与 13 个候选模型；使用分数一致性与成对一致性（pairwise agreement）两类指标，将冻结评测器与 14 个目标基准对比，并区分开发目标与封存（sealed）目标；揭示若干实证发现：最优评测器约排对 75% 候选对，低于目标基准间分歧造成的 91% 上限；无单一 researcher 全面领先；开发目标最优者未必在封存目标上最优；人工设计的公开任务样本仍是强基线；执行成本最低的评测器成对一致性最高；归纳 agent 修正后仍存在的失败模式：截断回答、耗尽评测预算、少数问题主导某一领域分数

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) relies on evaluation feedback to assess progress and guide further research, yet repeatedly running complex benchmarks is costly and slows iteration. Human experts reduce this cost by selecting benchmark subsets or designing compact suites. We ask whether AI agents can automate this design process and introduce EvalResearchBench (ERB), a benchmark for autonomous evaluation research. Given target materials, development references, candidate APIs, and fixed time and API budgets, an agent called the researcher selects or synthesizes tasks, implements graders, and revises them in pilot tests before freezing an executable evaluator for coding, co-work, and reasoning. We study 9 researchers and 13 candidate models and compare each frozen evaluator with 14 target benchmarks on score concordance and pairwise agreement. The best evaluators order about 75\% of candidate pairs as the targets do, below the 91\% ceiling set by disagreements among the targets. No researcher leads on every metric, and the best evaluator on development targets is not the best on sealed targets hidden from the researcher. A human-designed sample of public tasks remains a strong baseline, and the evaluator with the lowest recorded execution cost attains the highest pairwise agreement. Agents repair tasks and graders through pilot feedback, yet their evaluators can still truncate answers, exhaust the evaluation budget, or let a few questions dominate a domain score.

</details>


### [102] [SHarP: Saliency-based Pruning of Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04178)
*Xinyi Gao,Qiucheng Wu,Kaizhi Qian,Handong Zhao,Shiyu Chang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 把神经网络剪枝的思路用到 agent harness 上：通过单模块消融估计 tools/instructions/支撑机制的显著性，剪掉低贡献高开销模块。实验发现多数 harness 高度冗余，大幅剪枝后性能与 token 效率仍可比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agent harness 在迭代修补中不断膨胀，导致大量 token 开销，但不清楚哪些 harness 模块是冗余的，需要在任务性能与 token 成本之间取得更好平衡。

Method: SHarP 把 agent harness 视为由可独立禁用的模块组成的系统，模块类别包括工具（tools）、指令（instructions）以及支撑机制。流程为：首先枚举可单独关闭的 harness 组件；然后对每个模块执行单模块消融（只禁用它、保留其余全部模块），记录消融后的任务性能与 token 成本；再以全部单模块消融结果作为参照基准，估计每个模块相对于整体的显著性——对性能贡献小或带来较大 token 开销的模块显著性低。最后按显著性排序剪除低显著/高开销模块，得到更精简的 harness。该方法不需要训练或微调模型，是推理/部署阶段对 agent 工作流的模块级压缩，目标是在 held-out 验证集上保持任务性能的同时降低 token 成本。论文未披露具体的 harness 实现细节、模型、工具数量或消融规模。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将神经网络剪枝思想迁移到 agent harness：把 tools、instructions 及支撑机制视为可独立关闭的组件。；提出基于显著性的剪枝策略 SHarP：对每个模块单独消融，并以全部单模块消融结果为参照，估计其对任务性能与 token 开销的贡献，剪掉性能贡献最小或开销最大的模块。；在多个 harness 的 held-out 验证集上给出经验发现：多数 harness 高度冗余，剪掉相当比例模块后仍能维持可比性能与效率。

Abstract: Agent harnesses are systems that coordinate model calls, tool use, and task execution to help large language models complete complex tasks. To meet task requirements and address failures, these systems are often iteratively refined by amending and patching their instructions, tools, and workflows, continuously increasing harness complexity. It is therefore unclear whether some resulting harness modules are redundant, introducing substantial token overhead with little, if any, performance gain. Inspired by neural network pruning, in this paper, we study harness pruning as a means of striking a better balance between task performance and token cost. We propose SHarP (Saliency-based Harness Pruning), a simple yet effective pruning strategy based on the saliency of each harness module with respect to performance and efficiency. Specifically, we first identify tools, instructions, and supporting mechanisms as components that can be individually disabled. We then estimate the saliency of each module by ablating it and assessing its task performance and token cost relative to the full set of single-module ablations. Modules with the smallest contribution to performance or largest computational overhead are subsequently pruned. Our evaluation across various harnesses on held-out validation sets reveals a surprising finding: most harnesses that we studied are highly redundant and can maintain comparable performance and efficiency even after a substantial portion of their modules are pruned. Our pruning approach and empirical findings provide new perspectives on agent harness design and optimization.

</details>


### [103] [MemLeak: Cross-User Semantic Leakage in Multi-Tenant AI Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04195)
*Priyanka Mudgal,Kai Zhao,Guilin Zhang,Andy Olsen,Ezekiel Miller,Xu Chu,Aletta Johanna Blanken*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示多租户AI Agent共享向量记忆库中的跨用户语义泄漏问题，非对抗性泄漏率达70-100%，对抗性记忆top-k放置率90-100%，端到端回复污染达5.00/5，且污染回复常被评为更有用；仅硬性检索后所有权门控能恢复干净基线，延迟开销约1.4ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多租户AI Agent共享向量记忆库中，普通余弦相似度检索即可导致跨用户语义相邻记忆的偶然泄漏（非对抗性泄漏率70-100%），并进而污染端到端生成回复，而现有基于关键词的攻击者基线与常见缓解措施不足以解决该问题。

Method: 系统基于多租户共享向量库的长期记忆检索：用户查询经编码后（稀疏TF-IDF或密集MiniLM-L6-v2）在共享嵌入空间中进行余弦相似度检索，返回top-k语义相邻记忆。实验评估跨用户可接纳性失败，包括非对抗偶然泄漏、对抗性记忆top-k放置、端到端生成回复污染。缓解措施在检索后阶段进行，包括硬性所有权门控（仅返回属于查询用户的记忆），在生成之前过滤检索结果；其他两种架构缓解未具体命名。生成模型包括生产检索路径与Claude Sonnet 4.5。训练目标与优化未涉及。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "非对抗性偶然泄漏率", "value": "70-100%", "setting": "池化{同队}检索，稀疏(TF-IDF)与密集(MiniLM-L6-v2)检索下"}, {"metric": "对抗性构造记忆的top-k放置率", "value": "90-100%", "setting": "超过较弱关键词攻击者基线，生产级密集检索(Config B)"}, {"metric": "密集检索分数提升", "value": "+0.416 到 +0.511", "setting": "对抗性构造记忆 vs. 基线，生产级密集检索(Config B)"}, {"metric": "端到端回复污染评分", "value": "5.00/5", "setting": "生产检索路径"}, {"metric": "端到端回复污染评分", "value": "4.67/5", "setting": "Claude Sonnet 4.5 生成"}, {"metric": "污染回复与干净回复的有用性差距", "value": "污染回复常与干净回复一样有用或更有用，经人工判断验证", "setting": "人类评估"}, {"metric": "硬性检索后所有权门控恢复干净基线", "value": "1.00/5", "setting": "跨两个生成模型一致"}, {"metric": "延迟开销", "value": "约1.4 ms/查询", "setting": "硬性检索后所有权门控"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化定义跨用户可接纳性失败（cross-user admissibility failure）作为非对抗性语义泄漏问题；在稀疏（TF-IDF）与生产级密集（MiniLM-L6-v2）检索下系统量化泄漏率，并设计对抗性记忆以测试top-k放置；提出并对比三种架构缓解措施，发现只有检索后的硬性所有权门控能一致恢复干净基线（1.00/5），并测量约1.4ms/查询的延迟开销

Abstract: Personal AI agents in enterprise multi-tenant deployments share a common vector store for long-term memory. Shared embedding spaces create a surface for cross-user memory leakage: a user's query can retrieve semantically adjacent memories belonging to another user through ordinary cosine-similarity retrieval, without any exploit. We formalize this as cross-user admissibility failure and evaluate it across six experiments, plus follow-up ablations, under both sparse (TF-IDF) and production-faithful (MiniLM-L6-v2) retrieval. Non-adversarial, incidental leakage reaches 70--100\% under pooled {same-team} retrieval; adversarially crafted memories achieve 90--100\% top-$k$ placement, exceeding weaker keyword-based attacker baselines, with score lifts of $+0.416$ to $+0.511$ under production-faithful dense retrieval (Config B); and end-to-end response contamination reaches 5.00/5 under a production retrieval path and 4.67/5 with Claude Sonnet~4.5, with contaminated responses often scoring as helpful or more helpful than clean ones, a gap validated against human judgment. Among three architectural mitigations, only hard post-retrieval ownership gating consistently restores the clean baseline (1.00/5) across {two generation models, at a measured latency overhead of roughly 1.4~ms per query.

</details>


### [104] [Agentic Cognitive Depth: Operational Criteria for Evaluating LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04168)
*Nijesh Upreti,Chris Sypherd,Vaishak Belle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文针对LLM Agent系统提出“agentic cognitive depth”轨迹级评估框架，用上下文敏感性、时间连续性、多模态协调、自适应交互、元认知监控五项操作标准刻画Agent运行质量，并为每项给出代理指标与扰动方法，可作为GAIA、SWE-bench、WebArena、TRIP-Bench等基准的补充诊断层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic LLM系统评估主要依赖端到端任务成功率，缺乏对Agent在轨迹层面运行机制（规划、记忆、工具、控制流的协同使用及全局监控）的细粒度评估方法。

Method: 论文以“LLM在循环中(LLM in a loop)配合规划(Planning)、记忆(Memory)、工具(Tools)、控制流(Control Flow)”作为Agentic LLM系统的核心架构视图。在此视图上，论文提出agentic cognitive depth概念：整个系统的运行被建模为一条轨迹(trajectory)，并沿五个操作标准生成轨迹级画像。其中前四项标准分别对应控制流、记忆、工具、规划在整条轨迹中的使用质量：上下文敏感性(C)衡量控制流如何随环境上下文调整，时间连续性(T)衡量记忆在时间维度上的保持与利用，多模态协调(M)衡量工具在多模态输入输出间的协调，自适应交互(A)衡量规划对交互的适应能力；第五项元认知监控(Mc)衡量系统是否对整段运行进行监控与调节。论文为每项标准给出操作化代理指标与扰动过程，用于在轨迹层面施加受控扰动并观测系统表现，从而将画像与Agent的世界模型联系起来。评估层面，论文提出在GAIA、SWE-bench、WebArena、TRIP-Bench等既有端到端基准上附加逐项诊断，而非替代原有任务成功率指标。构建层面，论文建议以符号验证器、结构化记忆、规划器耦合、工具约束作为实现和测试上述能力的具体手段。论文未披露具体的模型结构、训练目标、数据流实现细节或推理服务信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 定义agentic cognitive depth为跨五项操作标准的轨迹级画像：上下文敏感性(C)、时间连续性(T)、多模态协调(M)、自适应交互(A)、元认知监控(Mc)；为每项标准提供操作化代理指标和扰动过程(perturbation procedure)；将认知深度画像与Agent的世界模型相连接；提出将GAIA、SWE-bench、WebArena、TRIP-Bench等基准扩展为带逐项诊断的评估结构；建议通过符号验证器、结构化记忆、规划器耦合、工具约束来构建和测试这些能力

Abstract: Agentic large language model (LLM) systems are commonly implemented as an LLM in a loop with Planning, Memory, Tools, and Control Flow. This application-focused view connects agentic LLM research with deployable systems and leaves open how such systems should be evaluated beyond end-to-end task success. Building on this view, we define agentic cognitive depth as a trajectory-level profile across five operational criteria. The profile contains context sensitivity ($C$), temporal continuity ($T$), multimodal coordination ($M$), adaptive interaction ($A$), and metacognitive monitoring ($Mc$). The first four criteria measure how well Control Flow, Memory, Tools, and Planning are used across a trajectory. The fifth measures whether the system monitors and regulates the full run. For each criterion, we give operational proxies and a perturbation procedure, then connect the profile to the agent's world model. We provide the structure needed to extend benchmarks such as GAIA, SWE-bench, WebArena, and TRIP-Bench with per-criterion diagnostics. Symbolic verifiers, structured memory, planner coupling, and tool constraints provide practical ways to build and test these capacities.

</details>


### [105] [Exploration-Preserving Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04011)
*Hangzhan jin,Mohammad Hamdaqa,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ExPPO 提出一种轻量级优势塑形方法，通过 prompt 相对长度归一化 surprisal 和通过率重新分配信用，以保持探索性并提升推理覆盖与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可验证奖励的强化学习后训练中，组相对目标对同等奖励的响应分配相同优势，使信用与采样模式频率成比例，导致正确模式的探索覆盖受限；需要一种保持探索性的策略优化方法。

Method: ExPPO 是一种用于 LLM 后训练的策略优化方法。输入为 prompt 与经过验证器评分的响应，采用组相对优势估计。核心模块是一种优势塑形规则：对每个响应计算 prompt 相对、长度归一化的 surprisal，并结合 prompt 通过率重新分配信用，同时使用有界塑形与共享归一化，保持验证器极性并近似维持每个 prompt 组的绝对序列优势质量。训练目标为策略优化，通过推导局部条件来分析熵和正确模式发现的增益。推理流程与常规 RL 后训练一致，未披露具体模型、数据、硬件或推理资源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "in-domain and out-of-domain reasoning coverage", "value": "improved", "setting": "实验设置未在摘要中具体说明"}, {"metric": "aggregate response accuracy", "value": "higher", "setting": "实验设置未在摘要中具体说明"}, {"metric": "coverage at large sampling budgets", "value": "strong coverage", "setting": "大采样预算"}, {"metric": "correct-mode yield", "value": "increased", "setting": "controlled multi-answer evaluation"}, {"metric": "diversity among verified-correct responses", "value": "gains", "setting": "controlled multi-answer evaluation"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 ExPPO 轻量级优势塑形规则，使用 prompt 相对、长度归一化的响应 surprisal 和 prompt 通过率重新分配信用；将有界塑形与共享归一化结合，保持验证器极性并近似维持每个 prompt 组的绝对序列优势质量；分析响应级信用分配与采样模式更新，推导熵和正确模式发现增益的局部条件；在域内和域外推理覆盖、聚合准确率、大采样预算覆盖以及多答案评估中展示改进

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning, while the allocation of learning signal shapes which solutions remain accessible under repeated sampling. Group-relative objectives assign equal advantages to equally rewarded responses, making aggregate credit proportional to sampled mode frequency. We introduce Exploration-Preserving Policy Optimization (ExPPO), a lightweight advantage-shaping rule that redistributes credit using prompt-relative, length-normalized response surprisal and prompt pass rate. ExPPO combines bounded shaping with shared normalization to preserve verifier polarity and approximately maintain each prompt group's total absolute sequence-advantage mass. Our analysis characterizes response-level credit allocation alongside sampled mode updates, deriving local conditions for gains in entropy and correct-mode discovery. Experiments show improved in-domain and out-of-domain reasoning coverage, higher aggregate response accuracy, and strong coverage at large sampling budgets. A controlled multi-answer evaluation further demonstrates increased correct-mode yield and gains in diversity among verified-correct responses. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [106] [SkillScriptBench: Benchmarking Self-Evolution of Executable Agent Skill Packages Beyond Markdown](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04008)
*Yuxuan Liu,Haoran Li,Yuhao Zhang,Jiahe Guo,Hongyu Luo,Wenbin Hu,Huihao Jing,Kawai Chung,Junle Chen,Changxuan Fan,Qing Zong,Lingyun Xie,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillScriptBench 是一个评估LLM智能体可执行技能包自演化的基准，包含150个修复任务和200个四状态受控任务，并提出AST引导的技能修订方法，显著提升脚本故障修复成功率和多次运行一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法系统区分文档修复、脚本修复与正确行为保留三种能力，导致LLM Agent技能包自演化评估不完整。

Method: SkillScriptBench 是一个用于评估可执行智能体技能包自演化的基准。输入是包含自然语言指令和脚本的可执行技能包，以及维护请求和必需行为的可执行检查。基准从35,000多个GitHub托管的技能根目录中选取100个技能包，构建150个修复任务；每个任务将注入脚本故障的技能包与维护请求配对，并通过可执行检查验证所需行为。补充的受控轨道包含来自50个技能包的200个任务，同一维护请求在四种状态下评估：干净、文档故障、脚本故障、两者均有。评估针对四种LLM，比较编辑文档和脚本的方法在文档修复、脚本修复和保留正确行为方面的表现。提出的AST-Guided Skill Revision方法使用抽象语法树和调用关系，将维护需求映射到相关代码位置；它将脚本编辑限制在这些位置，并更新文档以匹配修订后的脚本。该方法在故障包上跨模型平均实现修复成功率的绝对提升，并提高三次运行均成功任务的比例，表明重复运行下修复更一致。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "修复成功率绝对提升", "value": "21.9%", "setting": "Raw Package，跨模型平均，注入脚本故障的技能包"}, {"metric": "修复成功率绝对提升", "value": "27.7%", "setting": "CoEvoSkills，跨模型平均，注入脚本故障的技能包"}, {"metric": "三次运行均成功任务比例绝对提升", "value": "20.8%", "setting": "Raw Package，跨模型平均"}, {"metric": "三次运行均成功任务比例绝对提升", "value": "31.5%", "setting": "CoEvoSkills，跨模型平均"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: SkillScriptBench：来自35,000+ GitHub技能根目录调查，选取100个技能包构建150个修复任务，并设置200个四状态受控任务（干净、文档故障、脚本故障、两者均有）。；AST-Guided Skill Revision：使用抽象语法树与调用关系将维护需求链接到相关代码位置，限制脚本编辑范围，并同步更新文档以匹配修改后的脚本。

Abstract: Executable Agent Skills combine natural-language instructions and scripts into reusable packages for LLM agents, and revising them requires fixing errors without breaking correct behavior. Existing benchmarks do not systematically distinguish documentation repair, script repair, and preservation when evaluating skill self-evolution. We introduce SkillScriptBench, a 350-task benchmark designed to evaluate these capabilities separately. From a survey of over 35,000 GitHub-hosted Skill roots, we select 100 packages and construct 150 repair tasks. Each task pairs a package containing injected script faults with a maintenance request and executable checks of the required behavior. A complementary controlled track contains 200 tasks from 50 packages, each evaluated under the same maintenance request in four states: clean, documentation faults, script faults, and faults in both. Across four LLMs, methods that edit both documentation and scripts can repair script faults but do not consistently outperform Markdown-only revision on documentation repair or preservation. We therefore introduce AST-Guided Skill Revision, which uses abstract syntax trees and calling relationships to link maintenance requirements to relevant code locations. It restricts script edits to these locations and updates the documentation to match the revised scripts. Averaged across models, this revision stage yields absolute gains in repair success of 21.9% for Raw Package and 27.7% for CoEvoSkills on faulty packages. Absolute gains in the proportion of tasks solved in all three runs reach 20.8% and 31.5%, respectively, indicating more consistent repair success across repeated runs.

</details>


### [107] [Proxy Confidence: Auditing Black-Box LLM Agents with a Surrogate's Log-Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03894)
*Yikai Zhao,Saurabh Pandey,Pradeep Kumar Misra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出Proxy Confidence方法：用低成本开源替代模型并行读取相同上下文和候选动作，通过teacher forcing、request-PMI、判别式verdict和tool-choice competition等无需训练的readout，从对数概率中提取工具调用错误检测信号。在困难编码任务上达到AUROC 0.825（actor自述置信度仅0.598），并支持实时门控与置信度反馈两种部署模式，在live-execution基准上提升任务成功率最高+0.137。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM智能体在工具调用、查询和代码上可能静默出错，而API隐藏token概率、智能体自述置信度接近随机、重采样因模型高度重复而无帮助，因此需要在不访问智能体内部且低成本的前提下恢复可靠的错误信号。

Method: 系统包含被审计的LLM智能体和一个低成本开源替代模型。替代模型与智能体并行运行，读取完全相同的上下文、工具schema以及智能体提出的候选动作（工具调用、查询或代码）。替代模型对候选动作进行一次prefill pass，从其token对数概率中通过一组互补的readout提取审计信号：(1) teacher forcing：在给定上下文的条件下评估候选动作中每个参数值的生成似然；(2) request-PMI：衡量参数值与请求上下文的点互信息，以判断参数值是否由请求恰当导出；(3) 判别式verdict：将整个候选调用作为输入，输出一个整体正确/错误的判别判断；(4) tool-choice competition：将目标函数与同批次其他可用函数进行竞争比较，判断函数选择是否合理。核心原则是：生成式似然（teacher forcing/request-PMI）擅长定位错误的参数值，而判别式verdict擅长捕捉整体错误的调用；当错误类型未知时，集成多个readout作为低后悔默认。整个readout过程training-free，不访问智能体内部，仅增加一次prefill开销。得到的置信度分数驱动两种部署模式：实时门控模式在分数低于阈值时将调用升级人工审查；置信度反馈模式将工具执行结果连同置信度分数一起返回给智能体，使智能体在下一步调整行为。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AUROC", "value": "0.825", "setting": "困难编码任务上，替代模型readout对错误工具调用的检测，对照actor自述置信度0.598"}, {"metric": "AUROC提升", "value": "+0.07 至 +0.28", "setting": "生成式readout相比actor自述置信度，在另外三个actor上"}, {"metric": "AUROC提升", "value": "+0.14 至 +0.19", "setting": "相比self-consistency，在近确定性actor上，成本为1/K"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "accepted-action accuracy", "value": "+0.05 至 +0.30", "setting": "实时门控部署模式，50%覆盖率下接受动作的准确率提升"}, {"metric": "task success", "value": "+0.119 和 +0.137 (p <= 1e-4)", "setting": "置信度反馈部署模式，在live-execution基准上的任务成功率提升"}, {"metric": "task success", "value": "+0.078 (p = 0.003)", "setting": "置信度反馈相比随机值控制，在步骤错误静默场景下的任务成功率提升"}]}

Conclusion: 用低成本开源替代模型并行读取相同上下文、schema和候选动作，从自身对数概率中提取审计信号，无需访问被审计模型内部。；提出互补的readout家族：teacher forcing与request-PMI评估每个参数值的似然，判别式verdict整体判断调用，tool-choice competition将函数与兄弟函数竞争，并给出原则：生成式似然定位错误参数值，verdict捕捉整体错误调用，未知错误类型时集成是低后悔默认。；readout无需训练，仅需一次prefill pass，与工具调用并行执行，成本约为self-consistency的1/K。；两种部署模式：实时门控将最不可信的调用升级人工审查；置信度反馈将工具结果与得分一并返回，使智能体调整下一步。

Abstract: A deployed LLM agent emits tool calls, queries, and code that can be silently wrong -- by the time the error surfaces, the action has run. Frontier chat APIs hide the model's token probabilities; the agent's stated confidence barely beats chance on the mistakes that matter; and resampling does not help, since frontier models are highly repetitive, reproducing the same call across samples. We recover the missing signal from a low-cost open-weight surrogate run in parallel. It reads the same context, schema, and proposed action as the agent, then scores the call from its own log-probabilities through a family of complementary readouts: teacher forcing and request-PMI weigh the likelihood of each argument value, a discriminative verdict judges the call as a whole, and tool-choice competition tests the function against its siblings. One principle says which to trust: a generative likelihood localizes wrong argument values, while the verdict catches holistically wrong calls. When the error type is unknown, an ensemble is the low-regret default. The readout is training-free, needs no access to the agent's internals, and costs one prefill pass alongside the tool call. On difficult coding tasks it reaches AUROC 0.825 where the actor's stated confidence is near chance (0.598), and the generative readouts beat it by +0.07 to +0.28 across three further actors. Against self-consistency it gains +0.14 to +0.19 on near-deterministic actors, at 1/K the cost. The signal drives two deployment modes: a real-time gate escalating the least-trustworthy calls for review (+0.05 to +0.30 accepted-action accuracy at 50% coverage), and confidence feedback, returning the tool result with the score so the agent adapts its next step -- lifting task success on live-execution benchmarks (+0.119 and +0.137, p <= 1e-4) and beating a random-value control where step errors are silent (+0.078, p = 0.003).

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Orchestrating Specialized Agents for Trustworthy Enterprise RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18267)
*Xincheng You,Qi Sun,Neha Bora,Huayi Li,Shubham Goel,Kang Li,Sean Culatana*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Atlassian提出ADORE企业级RAG智能体框架，用结构化Claim-Evidence Graph约束生成、以证据覆盖率驱动迭代检索，并在DeepResearch Bench和DeepConsult上取得领先或最高胜率，适合关注可信RAG与Agent检索的工程团队阅读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一趟式检索-生成管线在企业高要求场景中易产生浅层摘要、 grounding 不一致、缺乏完整性验证与可追溯引用，难以支持需深度综合与严格溯源的企业决策。

Method: ADORE以中央orchestrator协调多个专用agent，将传统线性检索替换为迭代、用户引导的调查流程。系统核心是一个结构化Memory Bank（Claim-Evidence Graph），存储经过筛选的证据并显式链接声明与证据，且限定每个章节可采信的证据集合。生成阶段执行Memory-locked synthesis：报告内容被约束在Memory Bank内，每个声明必须关联具体证据，从而实现可追溯引用与 grounded 摘要。执行流程由证据覆盖率引导：retrieval-reflection循环按章节审计证据覆盖情况，若发现不足则触发针对性的追加检索，直到满足证据驱动的停止准则。为应对上下文长度限制，采用section-packed长上下文 grounding：对每个章节的证据进行打包、剪枝和保留引用的压缩，使长文综合可在有限上下文窗口内完成。系统整体上通过orchestrator调度上述模块，形成迭代检索、验证与生成的闭环。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "DeepResearch Bench得分", "value": "52.65", "setting": "ADORE在DeepResearch Bench评测中排名第一"}, {"metric": "DeepConsult head-to-head偏好胜率", "value": "77.2%", "setting": "与商业系统对比的最高胜率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Memory-locked synthesis：将报告生成约束到结构化Memory Bank（Claim-Evidence Graph），并按章节限定可采信证据，实现可追溯声明与有依据引用。；Evidence-coverage-guided execution：检索-反思循环审计章节级证据覆盖率，触发针对性追加检索，并以证据驱动的停止准则终止。；Section-packed long-context grounding：通过章节级打包、剪枝与保留引用的压缩，使长文综合在上下文限制下可行。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) shows promise for enterprise knowledge work, yet it often underperforms in high-stakes decision settings that require deep synthesis, strict traceability, and recovery from underspecified prompts. One-pass retrieval-and-write pipelines frequently yield shallow summaries, inconsistent grounding, and weak mechanisms for completeness verification. We introduce ADORE (Adaptive Deep Orchestration for Research in Enterprise), an agentic framework that replaces linear retrieval with iterative, user-steered investigation coordinated by a central orchestrator and a set of specialized agents. ADORE's key insight is that a structured Memory Bank (a curated evidence store with explicit claim-evidence linkage and section-level admissible evidence) enables traceable report generation and systematic checks for evidence completeness. Our contributions are threefold: (1) Memory-locked synthesis - report generation is constrained to a structured Memory Bank (Claim-Evidence Graph) with section-level admissible evidence, enabling traceable claims and grounded citations; (2) Evidence-coverage-guided execution - a retrieval-reflection loop audits section-level evidence coverage to trigger targeted follow-up retrieval and terminates via an evidence-driven stopping criterion; (3) Section-packed long-context grounding - section-level packing, pruning, and citation-preserving compression make long-form synthesis feasible under context limits. Across our evaluation suite, ADORE ranks first on DeepResearch Bench (52.65) and achieves the highest head-to-head preference win rate on DeepConsult (77.2%) against commercial systems.

</details>


### [109] [SPRIG: Semantic-ID-enhanced Paths for Knowledge Graph-based Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06590)
*Justin Hangoebl,Marta Moscati,Alessandro B. Melchiorre,Shah Nawaz,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SPRIG 将内容派生的语义ID集成到知识图谱路径生成式推荐中，结合结构化关系与离散语义编码，在电影和音乐数据集上以更少参数和更低计算成本取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐中，基于语义ID（SID）的方法缺乏关系接地，而基于知识图谱（KG）路径的方法使用不透明的离散token，导致参数共享和泛化能力受限；如何融合内容驱动的SID与KG结构化路径，提升生成式推荐的性能与效率。

Method: SPRIG是一种生成式推荐模型，其输入为知识图谱路径，路径由实体和关系组成，其中物品节点使用内容派生的离散语义ID（SID）表示，而非随机初始化的嵌入。模型通过训练信息丰富的KG路径来生成推荐，具体地，路径从用户历史交互出发，在知识图谱中游走，最终终止于表示为离散内容派生token的物品。训练目标是基于这些路径生成下一个物品的语义ID。推理时，给定用户历史，模型生成候选物品的语义ID序列，从而实现推荐。该架构融合了SID的语义泛化能力和KG路径的关系接地能力，避免了大规模嵌入表，减少了参数和计算开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将内容派生的语义ID（SID）集成到知识图谱路径推理中，使路径末端物品表示为离散、内容派生的token。；设计信息丰富的KG路径，结合结构化关系与语义离散编码，统一SID与KG路径生成式推荐的优点。；在电影和音乐推荐数据集上验证，相比多种基线（序列语言模型、KG增强方法、SID方法）取得有竞争力的性能，同时参数更少、计算成本更低。

Abstract: Recommender systems leveraging generative models often generate item identifiers directly, rather than ranking catalog items by a recommendation score. Recent work extends beyond pure sequential interaction signals by incorporating item content and structured relationships among items, with two distinct directions emerging. Semantic IDs (SIDs) enrich item representations by replacing opaque, randomly initialized embeddings with hierarchically quantized discrete codes derived from item content. Knowledge-graph (KG) path reasoning instead generates entity-relation paths that ground recommendations in structured relationships between items, attributes, and external entities, thereby enriching the relational context. These two lines have complementary limitations: SID-based models lack relational grounding, while KG-based generative recommenders still represent items as arbitrary, opaque tokens tied to large embedding tables, limiting parameter sharing and generalization. We propose SPRIG, a generative recommender that integrates content-derived SIDs into KG path reasoning. SPRIG is trained on information-rich KG paths that terminate in items represented as discrete, content-derived tokens, combining the advantages of both approaches. We evaluate SPRIG on movie and music recommendation datasets against baselines spanning sequential language models, KG-augmented methods, and SID-based approaches. Our results show that SPRIG achieves competitive performance over prior generative models while using fewer parameters and a lower compute cost. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [110] [Commercial Intent in Human-AI Conversations: A Corpus Audit and Architecture for Website Sales Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06205)
*Benjamin Tannenbaum*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Aiso 委托的对话语料元数据审计与销售代理参考架构，提出将语料清单、商业相关性与训练资格分离，并设计了业务知识 + 来源对话状态 + 受限动作策略的架构；无模型训练或在线验证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在人工-AI 销售对话中可靠地区分产品咨询与购买承诺，保持显式需求，并让下一步动作以最新业务信息为依据。

Method: 论文提出面向网站销售代理的参考架构，输入为对话记录及其元数据（对话哈希、标签、语言、深度等）。架构包含三个主要模块：1）业务知识模块（business-grounded knowledge），提供当前产品/业务信息以支撑回答；2）带来源标记的对话状态模块（provenance-bearing conversation state），记录并保留显式需求与交互历史；3）受限 next-action 策略（constrained next-action policy），在允许的动作集合内依据当前业务信息选择下一步。数据流上，语料先经过质量规范（来源权利、隐私、标签验证、去重、训练-测试分离）得到可用的对话状态，再驱动受限动作策略。论文未披露具体的模型结构、训练目标、优化方法或推理细节；其核心贡献是元数据审计与架构设计，而非端到端训练或在线验证。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 对 Aiso 提供的 725,219 条对话记录进行聚合元数据普查，披露商业/交易标签占比与批次差异；提出将语料清单、商业相关性、训练资格与业务结果显式分离的参考架构；结合业务知识、带来源标记的对话状态与受限 next-action 策略的架构设计；针对来源权利、隐私、标签验证、去重与训练-测试分离的质量规范

Abstract: Conversational sales agents must distinguish questions about products from purchase commitments, preserve explicit requirements, and ground the next action in current business information. We report an aggregate census of 725,219 records in an accessible conversation table provided by Aiso and develop a reference architecture for this setting. All records have distinct non-null conversation hashes. Existing metadata labels identify 41,800 commercial records (5.76%) and 2,387 transactional records (0.33%); their union contains 44,187 records (6.09%). Within the commercial category, 54.31% are labeled English, 41.59% have recorded depth of at least two, and 13.51% have depth of at least four. Commercial-label prevalence varies from 4.87% to 6.35% across three source batches. These measurements motivate explicit separation of corpus inventory, commercial relevance, training eligibility, and observed business outcomes. The proposed architecture combines business-grounded knowledge, provenance-bearing conversation state, and a constrained next-action policy. A quality specification addresses source rights, privacy, label validation, deduplication, and training-test separation. The study is a metadata audit and technical design, not a validation of label accuracy, model training volume, or sales conversion. No raw conversation text or personal identifiers are released.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond States: Investigating the Effects of Context on User Modeling with Feature-Conditioned Markov Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06060)
*Jana Isabelle Friese,Andreas Konstantin Kruff,Timo Breuer,Philipp Schaer,Norbert Fuhr*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文针对经典马尔可夫用户模型难以融入上下文的问题，提出特征条件马尔可夫用户模型，将转移概率建模为位置、内容和交互特征的函数，并在多数据集、多搜索场景下评估其预测拟合度和行为保真度。结果表明上下文特征能提升模拟真实性，但需针对具体任务和场景进行特征选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典基于状态的用户模型（如马尔可夫模型）难以纳入对决策有用的上下文信息，导致用户行为模拟的预测拟合度和行为保真度受限。

Method: 该论文提出一种特征条件马尔可夫式用户模型。输入包括用户历史交互状态、文档位置特征、内容特征（如查询-文档相关性）以及交互衍生特征（如点击、停留等）；模型将状态转移概率建模为这些特征的函数，从而在保留马尔可夫状态结构的同时引入上下文信息。训练目标为最大化用户行为序列的预测似然，学习特征到转移概率的映射。推理时根据当前状态和可用特征计算下一动作的概率分布，生成模拟用户行为序列。评估采用多级框架，分别在预测拟合度（如似然、预测准确率）和行为保真度（如与真实用户交互分布的相似性）两个层面，在多个数据集、搜索设置和特征配置下进行对比。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出特征条件马尔可夫式用户模型，将转移概率建模为位置、内容与交互衍生特征的函数，实现上下文感知决策，同时保留状态模型的结构简单性和计算效率。；构建多级评估框架，同时衡量预测拟合度与行为保真度，分析不同上下文信息源对用户模拟真实性的贡献。；实证表明上下文特征的有效性依赖搜索场景和建模目标，不存在通用最优特征集，需要进行任务与场景特定的特征选择。

Abstract: User behavior simulation is widely used to evaluate interactive information retrieval systems, but classical state-based approaches (e.g., Markov models) have limited ability to incorporate contextual information relevant for decision-making. We address this limitation by introducing a feature-conditioned Markov-style user model, in which transition probabilities are modeled as functions of positional, content-based, and interaction-derived features, enabling context-aware decision making while preserving the structural simplicity and computational efficiency of state-based models. Applying a multi-level framework that assesses predictive fit and behavioral fidelity, we analyze how different sources of contextual information contribute to realistic user simulation across multiple datasets, search settings, and feature configurations. Our results show that incorporating contextual features improves the models' ability to reproduce key aspects of real user interactions, but that their effectiveness hinges on search scenario and modeling objective. Instead of a one-size-fits-all solution, effective simulation requires task- and setting-specific feature selection. Our framework provides a practical and interpretable basis for making these choices.

</details>


### [112] [MATE: Adaptive Long- and Short-Term User Memory for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06050)
*Yu Hou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MATE提出一种自适应长短期用户记忆框架，利用时间证据评估动态更新记忆，在线推理时仅更新用户记忆，在多个数据集上相比最强基线平均NDCG@10提升7.0%–13.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM增强的推荐系统中，仅靠语义表示无法区分历史行为反映的是持久偏好还是近期兴趣，导致用户理解不完整。

Method: MATE包含三个核心模块：1) 时间证据评估器：对每个新交互，从两个时间视角判断其是否被历史行为重复支持、是否与近期交互一致，生成时间证据信号；2) 双记忆模块：长期记忆保守地保存持久偏好，短期记忆快速适应近期兴趣。时间证据信号控制两个记忆的更新速率；3) 融合与预测：在每个推荐步，近期上下文表示动态决定两个记忆对当前用户表示的贡献强度。训练阶段以下一项预测为主目标，并加入时间监督进行联合优化；在线推理阶段，共享模型参数固定，仅根据新观察到的交互更新两个用户记忆。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean NDCG@10", "value": "提升7.0%–13.2%", "setting": "对比最强外部基线，数据集：MovieLens-10M、Amazon Luxury Beauty、KuaiRec"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出MATE框架，同时维护长期和短期用户记忆，分别捕获持久偏好和近期兴趣；设计时间证据评估机制，从重复支持性和近期一致性两个维度评估新交互对记忆更新的贡献；使用近期上下文表示动态控制长短期记忆的融合权重；离线联合优化下一项预测与时间监督，在线仅更新用户记忆并冻结共享模型

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-enhanced recommender systems leverage rich item semantics to support personalized recommendation. However, semantic representations alone do not determine which historical behaviors reflect persistent preferences and which mainly indicate recent interests, leaving an important aspect of user understanding unresolved. Recent advances in LLM inference show that newly available information can be used to refine the internal state during inference, thereby improving subsequent predictions. Inspired by this principle, we propose MATE (Memory Adaptation with Temporal Evidence), an adaptive user modeling framework for LLM-enhanced sequential recommendation. MATE first evaluates each newly observed interaction from two temporal perspectives: whether it is repeatedly supported by historical behaviors and whether it is consistent with recent interactions. The resulting temporal evidence controls the updates of two user-specific memories, where the long-term memory conservatively preserves persistent preferences while the short-term memory rapidly adapts to recent interests. For each recommendation, a recent-context representation dynamically determines how strongly the two memories contribute to the current user representation. During offline training, next-item prediction is jointly optimized with temporal supervision, while during online adaptation, the shared model remains fixed and only the two user memories are updated from newly observed interactions. Experiments on MovieLens-10M, Amazon Luxury Beauty, and KuaiRec show that MATE improves mean NDCG@10 over the strongest external baseline by 7.0--13.2%. Further analyses support its ability to adapt to recent interests while retaining useful information about recurring earlier preferences.

</details>


### [113] [Constraint-Aware Conversational Job Recommendation in Code-Mixed Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05787)
*Md Arman Hossain,Mubashir Jawad,Fariha Khandaker Moon,Sonia Binte Siraj,Masfiqur Rahaman,Raihan ul Islam,Ahmed Wasif Reza,Nafis Sadeq*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍JobCCC，一个面向孟加拉国低资源、代码混合场景的对话式职位推荐基准，包含22,410条职位和988段多轮对话。提出W-SCAR软约束排序框架，结合BM25、多语言稠密检索和TOPSIS多标准排序，实验证明硬过滤会损害检索，而软约束方法在英语和Banglish上Hit@10分别达到37.37%和38.43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源、代码混合的对话式职位推荐场景中，严格约束过滤会因属性提取不完整或错误而不可逆地删除相关职位，导致检索性能下降。

Method: W-SCAR框架输入为对话历史、职位发布信息以及求职者偏好。首先通过稀疏BM25检索和稠密多语言检索获得候选职位集；然后提取经验、地点、教育、薪资等属性，并将其转化为分级效用值；接着计算词汇相关性和语义相关性得分；最后使用TOPSIS方法综合词汇相关性、语义相关性以及各属性的分级效用进行多标准排序，输出推荐职位列表。该方法旨在避免硬约束过滤导致的不可逆剪枝，从而在低资源、代码混合场景下提高检索和排序的鲁棒性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Hit@10", "value": "37.37%", "setting": "英语条件"}, {"metric": "Hit@10", "value": "38.43%", "setting": "罗马化孟加拉语-英语（Banglish）条件"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出JobCCC基准，包含22,410条结构化职位和988段多轮职业建议对话，并标注了动态求职者偏好和真实职位，提供英语与罗马化孟加拉语-英语两种语义等价的评测变体。；提出加权软约束感知排序框架（W-SCAR），结合词汇相关性、语义相关性和经验、地点、教育、薪资的分级效用，使用TOPSIS进行多标准排序，避免破坏性剪枝。；通过实验证明硬约束过滤在低资源代码混合场景下会损害检索性能，而软约束方法可获得更平衡的表现。

Abstract: Conversational job recommendation requires jointly modeling semantic relevance, user preferences, eligibility requirements, and the noisy language used in real-world career discussions. These challenges are especially pronounced in low-resource, code-mixed settings, where strict constraint matching can incorrectly eliminate otherwise suitable jobs. We introduce JobCCC, a conversational job recommendation benchmark for Bangladesh comprising 22,410 structured job postings and 988 multi-turn career-advice dialogues derived from regional Reddit communities. Each dialogue is annotated with evolving seeker preferences and linked to a ground-truth job, and is evaluated in semantically equivalent English and Romanized Bangla--English variants. We compare sparse BM25 retrieval, multilingual dense retrieval, and their hard-constraint-filtered counterparts against Weighted Soft-Constraint-Aware Ranking (W-SCAR), our multi-criteria ranking framework that combines lexical relevance, semantic relevance, and graded utilities for experience, location, education, and salary using the Technique for Order Preference by Similarity to Ideal Solution (TOPSIS). Experiments reveal that strict filtering consistently degrades retrieval because incomplete extraction and brittle attribute matching irreversibly remove relevant jobs. W-SCAR avoids destructive pruning and achieves more balanced performance across the two language conditions, obtaining 37.37% and 38.43% Hit@10 on English and Banglish, respectively. The code and dataset are publicly available at \href{ this https URL }{GitHub} and \href{ this https URL }{Hugging Face}, respectively.

</details>
