### [114] [SCOUT: Supply-Aware Cold-Start Proactive Query Suggestion for Travel Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05619)
*Hao Li,Shashank Reddy,Kedar Bellare,Ashish Jain,Stephanie Moyerman*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Airbnb 提出 SCOUT，面向旅行搜索的供给感知主动查询建议框架：在无历史查询日志、无种子查询的冷启动条件下，用生产 reranker 的 query-listing 匹配分数作为密集奖励，将搜索引擎视为 RL 环境并以 GRPO 优化策略，以零边际推理成本在实时搜索路径上实现 IMR@18 相对提升 12.3%，同时保持多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行搜索受物理库存约束且传统为分面搜索界面、无自由文本查询日志，导致既无需求侧用户反馈可对齐，又需在请求时无种子查询的情况下主动生成建议；即如何在冷启动与库存受限条件下生成可被供给端满足的查询建议。

Method: SCOUT 的输入是请求时可得的结构化上下文（如目的地、日期、库存/房源属性等）而非历史自由文本查询，因旅行平台传统分面搜索界面缺乏查询日志，形成冷启动。框架将搜索系统作为强化学习环境：策略（由 LLM 驱动）基于结构化上下文生成候选查询建议；环境返回的奖励不来自用户点击或转化（需求侧信号缺失），而来自生产 reranker 对查询与可预订房源列表的匹配分数，构造为密集奖励，从而使建议被物理库存所约束。策略优化采用 GRPO，通过组内相对比较估计优势，避免单独价值网络。推理时直接由策略一次生成建议集合，无需 best-of-8 多次采样，因此在实时搜索路径上增加零边际推理成本。该设计分别解决两个问题：以供给侧反馈解决无历史查询日志的数据缺口，以主动生成解决请求时无种子查询的冷启动，以 reranker 分数奖励解决偏好对齐与库存可得性脱节的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "IMR@18（inventory match rate）", "value": "相对提升 12.3%", "setting": "真实旅行搜索生产环境，SCOUT 供给感知查询建议"}, {"metric": "边际推理成本", "value": "零边际推理成本", "setting": "与 compute-intensive best-of-8 策略效果相当"}]}

Conclusion: 以供给侧系统反馈替代缺失的需求侧用户反馈（supply-side system feedback 替代 demand-side user feedback）的引导式框架；将搜索引擎视为强化学习环境，从生产 reranker 的 query-listing 匹配分数构造密集奖励；使用 GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）优化生成策略；在零边际推理成本下达到与 compute-intensive best-of-8 策略相当的效果，使供给感知建议可部署于实时搜索路径

Abstract: Generative query suggestion, powered by Large Language Models (LLMs), has become increasingly popular in search and conversational systems to reduce user friction and guide intent formulation. Existing approaches align suggestions with user preferences (e.g., clicks or conversions). This works for open-ended applications like chatbots and personal assistants, where the result space is unconstrained or historical user free-text queries are abundant. However, applying these methods to travel search presents two limitations. First, travel search is fundamentally constrained by physical inventory; a query (e.g., "romantic beachfront villa") may yield abundant results in Bali but few in Tokyo, so aligning with user preferences is not by itself grounded in what can be offered. Second, travel platforms traditionally rely on faceted search interfaces with no free-text queries. This creates a cold-start problem: without historical query logs there is no demand-side data for alignment, and without a seed query at request time, suggestions must be generated proactively from structured context alone. To address these challenges, we propose SCOUT, a bootstrapping framework for supply-aware proactive query suggestion. SCOUT overcomes the data gap by substituting missing demand-side user feedback with supply-side system feedback. It treats the search engine as a reinforcement learning environment, deriving a dense reward from the production reranker's query-listing match scores, and optimizes the policy with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). SCOUT improves inventory match rate (IMR@18) by 12.3% while preserving diversity, matching a compute-intensive best-of-8 policy at zero marginal inference cost and making supply-aware suggestion deployable on a real-time travel search path.

</details>


### [115] [Beyond Semantic Similarity: Performance and Costs of Agentic Retrieval for Complex Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05750)
*Reza Esfandiarpoor,Radek Osmulski,Yauhen Babakhin,Gabriel de Souza P. Moreira,Oliver Holworthy,Jie He,Ronay Ak,Jiarui Cai,Ryan Chesler,Bo Liu,Even Oldridge*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了基于 ReAct 循环的 agentic retrieval 在复杂检索任务中的效果与成本。实验表明，在相同嵌入模型下，agentic retrieval 相比标准检索将 nDCG@10 提升 8.7 个点，并在 ViDoRe v3 与 BRIGHT 上展现跨域泛化能力；但查询延迟从 0.67 秒增至 107.4 秒，平均消耗 764.1K 输入 token 和 5.8K 输出 token。研究为大规模部署的检索智能体成本优化提供了动机与基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稠密检索依赖表层语义相似度，难以满足日益复杂的检索任务；需要研究能够结合 LLM 推理与高效语料探索的智能体检索方法，并评估其效果与代价。

Method: 论文研究 agentic retrieval：在 ReAct 智能体循环中，LLM 作为推理与决策核心，检索器负责高效探索大规模语料。输入为用户查询，智能体通过多轮 ReAct 步骤决定检索动作、解析检索结果并迭代推理，最终产出检索结果。实验使用相同嵌入模型对比标准稠密检索，在 ViDoRe v3 和 BRIGHT 基准上评估 nDCG@10，并记录延迟与 token 消耗。论文未披露具体模型规模、训练细节或在线服务架构。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "nDCG@10", "value": "提升 8.7 个点", "setting": "与标准检索对比，使用相同的嵌入模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于 ReAct 智能体循环的 agentic retrieval 管线，将 LLM 推理能力与检索器的语料探索能力结合。；在相同嵌入模型下，相比标准检索将 nDCG@10 提升 8.7 个点。；展示同一管线在 ViDoRe v3 与 BRIGHT 榜单上的跨域泛化能力。；系统量化 agentic retrieval 的延迟与 token 消耗，揭示其相比标准检索显著更高的成本。

Abstract: Modern information systems, including many agentic workflows, use dense retrieval to explore large amounts of unstructured data. However, dense retrieval relies on surface-level semantic similarity, which is insufficient for increasingly complex search applications. Here, we investigate agentic retrieval that combines the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) with the efficient corpus exploration of retrievers in a ReAct agentic loop to solve complex retrieval tasks. In our experiments, we show that agentic retrieval is more effective than standard retrieval, improving nDCG@10 by 8.7 points using the same embedding model. Moreover, while specialized retrieval methods struggle on out-of-domain tasks, agentic retrieval is highly generalizable: the same pipeline achieves competitive results on both the ViDoRe v3 and BRIGHT leaderboards. However, this improvement comes at a cost. On average, agentic retrieval takes 107.4 seconds, compared to 0.67 seconds for standard retrieval, and consumes 764.1K input and 5.8K output tokens per query. In short, our study demonstrates the effectiveness of agentic retrieval in modern data systems and motivates future work on more cost-efficient retrieval agents for large-scale deployment.

</details>


### [116] [Agentic RAG Evaluation: Budget Allocation Across Questions, Trajectories, and Reads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05034)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Yibo Kong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 一篇关于agentic RAG评测预算如何分配于问题、轨迹与重复读取的实证研究，基于HotpotQA与MuSiQue测量标准误、读取效率与成本边界，发现更广问题覆盖优于更多轨迹或重复读取，并检验了分配预测与温度对一致性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在agentic RAG评测中，如何在给定模型token预算下分配资源到问题数量、检索轨迹数量和每个问题的重复读取次数，以最大化评测精度并控制成本。

Method: 论文构建了一个agentic RAG评测预算分配框架。输入为HotpotQA和MuSiQue上的检索反馈对比数据，评测单元包括问题(question)、检索轨迹(trajectory)和重复读取(read)三个维度。方法上采用泛化理论对评测方差进行分解，将总token预算(34.14-34.39M)分配到不同维度，并测量分配精度(standard error)、读取效率与成本边界。实验通过比较问题覆盖广度、轨迹数量(如三条轨迹)与读取次数(如五次读取、一读)对标准误的影响，并利用归档的嵌套预测与Q-only预测来验证预算分配的可预测性。成本方面在记录的模型费用与搜索价格($0-1/1000请求)下比较问题优先与轨迹优先的性价比。一致性方面测试温度为零对答案分歧率的影响。推理/服务路径未涉及模型内部架构，而是围绕评测采样策略与统计估计展开。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "standard error降低", "value": "33%", "setting": "34.14-34.39M model tokens预算下，更广问题覆盖 vs 五次读取"}, {"metric": "standard error降低", "value": "12.6%", "setting": "34.14-34.39M model tokens预算下，更广问题覆盖 vs 三条轨迹"}, {"metric": "分配预测误差", "value": "4.0%", "setting": "归档嵌套预测"}, {"metric": "分配预测误差", "value": "3.5%", "setting": "Q-only预测"}, {"metric": "一读方差惩罚", "value": "0-9.9%", "setting": "相同token预算下相对拟合最优，Pro不确定性较大"}, {"metric": "答案不一致率降低", "value": "14.3%→3.4%", "setting": "温度从默认降至零，比较精度保持相似"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用检索反馈对比实验量化问题覆盖、轨迹数与重复读取在相同token预算下的分配精度；基于泛化理论(generalizability theory)对评测方差进行分解与预测；用归档的嵌套预测与Q-only预测验证预算分配的可预测性(误差4.0%和3.5%)；建立一读方差惩罚与token预算关系曲线(0-9.9%)，并给出温度零对答案一致性的影响(14.3%降至3.4%)；在搜索价格($0-1/1000请求)下比较问题数与轨迹数的成本效率

Abstract: Evaluation budgets in agentic retrieval-augmented generation span questions, search trajectories, and repeated answers. We measure allocation precision, reading efficiency, and cost boundaries using a retrieval-feedback comparison on HotpotQA and MuSiQue. At 34.14--34.39M model tokens, broader question coverage lowers standard error by 33\% versus five reads and 12.6\% versus three trajectories. Archived nested and Q-only forecasts predict these allocations within 4.0\% and 3.5\%, respectively. Depth subsets establish no clear forecasting advantage beyond the two-trajectory audit. One-read variance penalties relative to the fitted optimum at the same token budget are 0--9.9\%, with substantial Pro uncertainty. Under recorded model fees, more questions beat more trajectories at search prices of \$0--1 per 1,000 requests; question-versus-read fee rankings remain unresolved. Temperature zero cuts answer disagreement from 14.3\% to 3.4\% while comparison precision stays similar. \par\medskip\noindent\textbf{Keywords:} Agentic RAG; Evaluation budget; Generalizability theory; Repeated sampling.

</details>


### [117] [From Valid to Useful: Post-Verification Acquisition for Recursive Self-Improving Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04302)
*Tonmoy Hasan,Taylor Foust,Shao Tang,Leonardo Neves,Aman Gupta,Hiroto Udagawa,Helder Dias,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究递归自改进推荐中的验证后数据获取问题，提出 DA-RSIR：限制每个源序列贡献，并用 BALD 分歧分数（MC dropout 估计）排序选择验证序列训练下一轮模型。在四个数据集、三种推荐模型、两个指标共 24 组对比中全部优于 retain-all，单轮效果超过 retain-all 五轮最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在递归自改进推荐系统中，如何决定哪些通过验证的合成序列应该用于训练下一轮模型，以避免因序列长度或生成数量导致的不均衡影响，并提升下一轮模型效果。

Method: DA-RSIR 面向递归自改进顺序推荐流程。输入为用户真实交互序列；生成器产生合成/增强的交互序列；验证步骤检查每个生成序列是否仍能预测用户真实交互，丢弃漂移过大的序列。验证后，DA-RSIR 进行 post-verification acquisition：首先限制每个源序列在下一轮训练中的最大贡献，避免生成数量多或续写更长的源序列主导训练；然后对某个源序列产生的所有通过验证的序列，用当前模型在其增强交互上的预测分歧进行排序，只选择分歧较高的序列参与下一轮模型训练。分歧分数基于 Bayesian Active Learning by Disagreement (BALD)，通过 Monte Carlo dropout 多次前向得到预测分布并估计互信息，无需额外标签、教师模型或质量评分器。选中的验证序列与原始数据混合后训练下一轮推荐模型，形成递归循环。推理时使用训练好的推荐模型正常打分排序，不引入额外模块。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "24 组对比中的胜出次数", "value": "24/24 优于 retain-all", "setting": "四个数据集、三种推荐模型、两个指标"}, {"metric": "24 组整体最高均值次数", "value": "23/24", "setting": "四个数据集、三种推荐模型、两个指标"}, {"metric": "统计显著性", "value": "两个指标上总体提升均统计显著", "setting": "聚合比较"}, {"metric": "单轮 DA-RSIR 对比 retain-all 五轮最佳收益", "value": "单轮 DA-RSIR 超过 retain-all 五轮递归的最佳增益", "setting": "递归轮数对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 post-verification acquisition 作为递归自改进的独立控制点，区分“哪些序列通过验证”与“哪些验证序列用于训练”。；提出 DA-RSIR，为每个源序列的贡献设上限，并根据模型在增强交互上的预测分歧对验证序列排序。；使用基于 BALD 的分歧分数，通过 Monte Carlo dropout 估计，无需额外标签、教师模型或质量评分器。

Abstract: Sequential recommenders can generate synthetic interaction sequences and retrain on the augmented corpus in a recursive self-improvement loop. To limit error accumulation, current methods verify each generated sequence remains predictive of the user's real interactions and discard those that drift away from it. Verification does not, however, determine which verified sequences should train the next model. With every verified sequence used for training, source sequences yielding more verified sequences or longer continuations have more influence, although neither quantity indicates how much those sequences will help the next model. We formulate the decision of which verified sequences are used to train the next model as \emph{post-verification acquisition} and introduce {\bf Disagreement-Aware Recursive Self-Improving Recommendation (DA-RSIR)}. DA-RSIR caps each source sequence's contribution and ranks its verified sequences by how much the model's predictions disagree over their augmented interactions. It uses a score derived from Bayesian Active Learning by Disagreement (BALD) and estimated with Monte Carlo (MC) dropout. DA-RSIR requires no extra labels, teacher model, or quality scorer. Across four datasets, three recommender models, and two metrics, it improves on the retain-all approach in all $24$ comparisons and attains the highest mean in $23$ of $24$ overall; the aggregate improvement is statistically significant on both metrics. A single DA-RSIR round exceeds the retain-all approach's best gain over five recursive rounds. These findings establish post-verification acquisition as a separate control point in recursive self-improvement, separating which sequences pass verification from which verified sequences are used to train the next model.

</details>


### [118] [Query Generation with Direct Preference Optimization for Document Expansion in E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04352)
*Kaihao Li,Feng Liu,Juexin Lin,Xunfan Cai,Zhen Yang,Tony Lee,Ciya Liao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Walmart提出QGDPO，将DPO用于电商搜索文档扩展的查询生成：先用相关性模型构造偏好对训练生成模型，再用相关性模型过滤低质生成内容。方法消除50%不相关预测，过滤额外去除14.61%，并已全流量上线，提升相关性与用户参与度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Doc2Query类序列到序列文档扩展模型容易生成与文档无关的幻觉内容或与文档已有内容重复的查询，难以产生高质量、新颖且相关的token，导致索引质量与检索效果受限。

Method: QGDPO为两阶段文档扩展查询生成管线。第一阶段：基于基础序列到序列模型进行微调，使其能为商品文档生成候选查询。第二阶段：使用相关性模型对微调模型的预测进行打分，基于分数构建胜出（高相关性）与落败（低相关性）预测对，作为相关性偏好数据，对生成模型进行直接偏好优化（DPO）训练，从而抑制与文档无关的幻觉和重复内容。推理/索引阶段：模型为文档生成查询，随后相关性模型对生成内容打分并过滤，仅保留最相关的生成内容用于索引，以提升检索召回质量。该管线已部署至生产全流量，实现相关性与用户参与度提升；摘要未披露模型规模、训练数据、硬件、延迟或吞吐等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "不相关预测比例", "value": "消除50%", "setting": "对比Doc2Query基线，QGDPO方法"}, {"metric": "额外不相关预测比例", "value": "进一步去除14.61%", "setting": "在QGDPO基础上叠加相关性模型过滤"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "相关性与用户参与度", "value": "substantial improvement（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "在Walmart电商搜索全流量生产部署"}]}

Conclusion: 提出QGDPO：用直接偏好优化（DPO）引导文档扩展查询生成，先微调基础seq2seq模型，再用相关性模型对其预测打分，构建胜出/落败预测对作为相关性偏好进行DPO训练。；用相关性模型过滤低质量预测，仅保留最相关的生成内容用于索引，进一步提升索引质量。；在电商搜索全流量生产部署，并提供在线相关性与用户参与度提升证据。

Abstract: Doc2Query, a popular document expansion technique, leverages sequence-to-sequence models to generate relevant queries, effectively addressing the "vocabulary mismatch" problem in information retrieval. However, these models often suffer from generating either hallucinations unrelated to the document or repetitive content already present in the document. Training sequence-to-sequence models to produce high-quality, novel, and relevant tokens remains a significant challenge. To address these issues, we introduce a novel approach, QGDPO, that employs direct preference optimization (DPO) to guide the generation process. We first fine-tune a base sequence-to-sequence model and subsequently utilize a relevance model to score its predictions. Based on these scores, we construct pairs of winning and losing predictions as relevance preferences for the DPO training. Furthermore, we enhance our pipeline by using the relevance model to filter out poor predictions, retaining only the most relevant generated content for indexing. QGDPO effectively eliminates 50% of irrelevant predictions comparing against Doc2Query baselines, while the relevance filter removes an additional 14.61%. This feature has been successfully deployed to production on this http URL for full traffic, with a substantial improvement in relevance and user engagement.

</details>


### [119] [FICO: Find-Then-Compute for Corpus-Level Spreadsheet Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03958)
*Sandarsita Guntupalli,Lu He,Kang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: FiCo提出先检索文档摘要并消歧工作簿、再在完整表上执行约束SQL的两阶段表格问答框架。在DataBench 1,810题上达76.2%准确率，超TableRAG-style基线9.9个百分点，超prefix RAG 63.4个百分点；在MiMoTable 508题上达79.7%。实验还量化了源选择成本：给基线金标准工作簿仅提升10.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在电子表格集合上进行问答时，需要同时解决正确工作簿的选择问题与在完整表格上的精确计算问题。

Method: FiCo采用两阶段端到端流程：第一阶段Find，对电子表格集合中的每个工作簿生成文档摘要作为可检索表示，通过检索文档摘要找到相关候选工作簿，并对相似工作簿进行消歧，以选定最可能包含答案的完整工作簿；第二阶段Compute，将选中的完整表格输入约束SQL生成与执行模块，基于表格模式生成受约束的SQL查询并在完整表上执行，从而完成精确计算与答案抽取。训练与优化目标未在摘要中披露；推理时先检索摘要再生成SQL，依赖语义源选择与基于模式的精确计算结合，以解决表格问答中源选择与完整表计算两个子问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "76.2%", "setting": "DataBench (80 datasets, 1,810 questions)，使用预设评估器"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "66.3%", "setting": "TableRAG-style强基线，相同冻结工作簿选择"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "12.8%", "setting": "prefix RAG基线，DataBench"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "79.7%", "setting": "MiMoTable 508 questions"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "22.2%", "setting": "prefix RAG基线，MiMoTable"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "10.0 points (66.3%→76.3%)", "setting": "给TableRAG-style基线金标准工作簿，固定计算量"}, {"metric": "文档召回率", "value": "95.1%", "setting": "FiCo检索阶段"}, {"metric": "可执行SQL比例", "value": "98.5%", "setting": "FiCo计算阶段"}, {"metric": "金标准工作簿上可执行比例", "value": "81.3%", "setting": "FiCo，仅81.3%题目能在金标准工作簿上执行"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Find-then-Compute (FiCo)两阶段框架：先检索文档摘要并消歧相似工作簿，再对选中完整表执行约束SQL；在DataBench与MiMoTable上系统评估源选择成本：固定计算量下，即使给基线金标准工作簿仅提升10.0个百分点，暴露源选择瓶颈；整合语义源选择与基于模式的精确计算，在相同冻结工作簿选择下比TableRAG-style基线高9.9个百分点

Abstract: Question answering over spreadsheet collections requires finding the correct workbook and computing over complete tables. We introduce Find-then-Compute (FiCo), which retrieves document summaries, disambiguates similar workbooks, and executes constrained Structured Query Language (SQL) over the selected full table. On DataBench (80 datasets, 1,810 questions), FiCo reaches 76.2% accuracy: 9.9 points above a strong TableRAG-style baseline on the same frozen workbook choices (66.3%) under the tracks' prespecified evaluators, and 63.4 points above prefix RAG (12.8%). On 508 MiMoTable questions, FiCo reaches 79.7%, versus 22.2% for prefix RAG. Giving the strong baseline the gold workbook raises it from 66.3% to 76.3%, exposing a 10.0-point source-selection cost under fixed compute. Despite 95.1% document recall and 98.5% executable SQL, only 81.3% of questions execute on the gold workbook. FiCo's advantage comes from integrating semantic source selection with exact, schema-grounded computation.

</details>


### [120] [Learning Robust Personalized Prompts for LLM-Driven Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03923)
*Xiaolin Zheng,Qiyong Zhong,Jiajie Su,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: LRPRec提出了一种用于LLM驱动序列推荐的可学习个性化连续提示框架，通过初始化连续指令提示、加性注入用户偏好嵌入以及语义漂移约束，在三个基准数据集上一致优于强基线，并消除了手动调整背景和任务推理模板的需要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的序列推荐中，提示词微小变动导致性能波动，且连续提示学习面临共享任务级指令缺乏用户特定推理指导与梯度更新导致语义漂移的双重挑战。

Method: LRPRec是一种可学习的提示框架。输入为用户历史交互序列和候选物品，通过离散模板初始化连续指令提示。框架包含两个互补机制：1）个性化提示注入：将用户行为编码为偏好嵌入，并加性注入到共享提示中，实现参数高效的用户级适配；2）语义漂移约束：将共享提示正则化在初始化锚点附近的信任域内，以在优化过程中保持语义有效性。通过约束共享组件同时允许加性个性化，LRPRec将稳定性与表达性解耦。训练目标为下一个物品预测，采用自回归生成，条件于自然语言提示。推理时使用学习到的连续提示进行生成。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "整体推荐性能", "value": "一致优于强基线", "setting": "三个基准数据集（具体指标未提供）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出LRPRec框架，用离散模板初始化连续指令提示，避免手动设计背景与任务推理模板。；个性化提示注入：将用户行为编码为偏好嵌入并加性注入共享提示，实现参数高效的用户级自适应。；语义漂移约束：将共享提示正则化在初始化锚点附近的信任域内，在优化中保持语义有效性。

Abstract: LLM-driven sequential recommendation formulates next-item prediction as autoregressive generation conditioned on natural-language prompts. However, minor wording changes in semantically equivalent prompts can cause substantial performance fluctuations, undermining robustness and requiring costly manual prompt engineering. Continuous prompt learning reduces template dependence but faces two interacting challenges: shared task-level instructions lack user-specific reasoning guidance, while gradient updates can push continuous prompts outside the LLM's effective semantic space. Injecting personalized signals can further amplify this semantic drift. To address these challenges, we propose LRPRec, a learnable prompting framework that initializes continuous instruction prompts from discrete templates and introduces two complementary mechanisms. Personalized prompt injection encodes user behavior into a preference embedding and additively injects it into shared prompts, enabling parameter-efficient user-level adaptation. A semantic drift constraint regularizes the shared prompts within a trust region around their initialization anchors to preserve semantic validity during optimization. By constraining the shared component while allowing additive personalization, LRPRec decouples stability from expressiveness. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines while eliminating the need for manual tuning of background and task inference templates.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Towards Looped Models Done Right, Part II: Rethinking at Fixed Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06833)
*Benhao Huang,Chufan Shi,Junlin Chen,Shicheng Wen,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究循环语言模型的固定点特性，通过改进深度先验学习和正交输入注入，结合截断反向传播、KV共享、蒸馏和RL梯度复用，在100M到1.6B参数规模上降低困惑度并显著提升训练与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型因多次递归导致训练、解码、预填充和强化学习成本高昂，如何通过利用循环状态趋近固定点的特性来降低这些成本并提升模型质量。

Method: 论文针对循环语言模型（如Huginn）的架构进行改进。模型通过多次递归（loop）处理输入，每次递归更新隐藏状态，并注入原始输入。论文利用循环状态趋近固定点的特性，当状态接近固定点时，达到该状态的路径影响变小，因此可以在训练时采用截断反向传播以减少计算；在解码时，由于固定点性质，可以共享终端KV缓存，几乎不损失精度；还可以蒸馏一个学生模型，使其预填充速度提升最多1.79倍；在强化学习中，从保存的rollout状态计算梯度，避免通过重放轨迹反向传播，速度提升2倍。为了改善固定点的形成，论文改进了两个训练组件：深度先验和输入注入。原有的固定深度训练会破坏KV共享，而Huginn的宽深度先验支持共享但在目标深度上的监督被稀释超过共享所需。论文提出从预测反馈中学习深度先验，并加入熵项保持先验的广度，从而在支持KV共享的同时提供更准确的监督。原有的输入注入方案中，状态沿输入方向的分量可能会放大或抵消注入信号，论文提出正交注入，移除该分量，使注入更加稳定。整体流程：训练时，模型通过截断反向传播和学习的深度先验进行优化；推理时，利用固定点进行KV缓存共享以降低解码和预填充成本；RL阶段，利用保存的rollout状态快速计算梯度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "困惑度（perplexity）", "value": "在100M到1.6B参数规模上均低于Huginn的先验和现有注入方案", "setting": "语言模型预训练，多个参数规模"}, {"metric": "下游任务平均性能", "value": "1.6B模型使用学习先验配合3倍小的KV缓存，匹配固定深度训练使用全缓存的下游平均性能", "setting": "1.6B参数模型，下游任务评估"}, {"metric": "预填充速度", "value": "最多1.79倍加速", "setting": "蒸馏学生模型"}, {"metric": "RL梯度计算速度", "value": "比通过重放轨迹反向传播快2倍", "setting": "强化学习更新，从保存的rollout状态计算梯度"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 利用固定点特性实现训练时的截断反向传播；终端KV共享实现解码时几乎无精度损失的加速；蒸馏学生模型使预填充速度提升最多1.79倍；RL更新从保存的rollout状态计算梯度，比通过重放轨迹反向传播快2倍；从预测反馈中学习深度先验并加入熵项保持广度；正交注入移除状态中输入分量以消除放大或抵消效应

Abstract: Every recurrence of a looped language model adds cost in training, decoding, prefill, and reinforcement learning (RL). The closer recurrent states get to fixed points, the less the path to them matters. This enables truncated backpropagation in training; terminal key-value (KV) sharing for decoding with almost no loss in accuracy; a distilled student that prefills up to 1.79x faster; and RL updates that compute gradients from saved rollout states, 2x faster than backpropagating through the replayed trajectory. We therefore improve the two components of training that shape these fixed points: the depth prior and input injection. Fixed-depth training breaks KV sharing, and Huginn's broad depth prior supports sharing but dilutes supervision at the target depth more than sharing requires; we learn the prior from prediction feedback, with an entropy term that keeps it broad. Existing injection schemes let the state's component along the input amplify or cancel the injection; we remove this component with orthogonal injection. From 100M to 1.6B parameters, the learned prior and orthogonal injection lower perplexity at every scale relative to Huginn's prior and existing injection schemes, respectively. At 1.6B, the learned prior with a 3x smaller KV cache matches the downstream average of fixed-depth training with the full cache.

</details>


### [122] [OVAL: Output-Aware Local Page Bases for KV Cache Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06686)
*Ashkan Shahbazi,Chayne Thrash,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OVAL提出一种输出感知的局部页面基，用于KV cache检索：通过从keys与values联合结构中导出页面编码，使检索目标直接对齐value加权注意力的近似误差；方法训练free、推理时无需额外value统计，存储和打分成本与key-only谱表示相当，在长推理、长上下文理解和生成基准上优于key-only谱基线并具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有page稀疏注意力检索方法只估计注意力分数或页面相关性，其目标未直接考虑近似误差如何影响value加权后的注意力输出，导致检索子集与最终输出误差不匹配。

Method: OVAL面向长上下文推理中不断增长的KV cache问题：标准注意力需对所有KV计算，成本随长度增长。页面稀疏注意力将KV cache划分为若干页面，并为每页构造紧凑表示，查询时仅检索与当前query相关的子集。现有方法主要估计注意力分数或页面相关性，其检索目标与最终value加权注意力输出误差不直接对齐。OVAL提出输出感知的页面编码：从keys与values的联合结构中导出一个页面表示，在保留准确检索所需key信息的同时，使表示能够直接反映页面被选中后对value加权输出的近似误差。该编码是训练free的，构建后推理时不需要额外的value相关统计量。其存储表示与key-only谱表示大小相同，解码时的打分成本也相当，因此不增加显著的存储或打分开销。整体流程为：构建阶段将每页的K/V联合结构压缩为固定大小的页面表示；推理时query与页面表示进行打分，检索top页面；对检索到的页面做稀疏注意力近似，得到value加权输出。训练目标并非显式端到端训练，而是通过联合结构设计使页面表示与输出误差对齐。摘要未披露更细的模块、具体打分函数、页面大小、检索数量等实现细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "avg@k", "value": "在长推理基准上取得较强的avg@k性能，跨模型-基准对表现良好", "setting": "长推理基准，具体数值未在摘要中披露"}, {"metric": "长上下文理解与生成性能", "value": "在多个长上下文理解与生成设置上匹配或超过领先基线，具有适度解码开销", "setting": "长上下文理解与生成基准，具体数值未在摘要中披露"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出输出感知的输出基（output-aware page encoding），从keys与values的联合结构中导出页面表示。；在保持准确检索所需key信息的同时，使用value侧的联合结构优化页面表示。；训练free，且推理时无需额外的value相关统计量。；存储表示与key-only谱表示大小相同，解码时打分成本相当。

Abstract: Long context inference with large language models becomes increasingly expensive as attention must operate over an ever growing KV cache. Page sparse attention reduces this cost by representing each KV page compactly and retrieving only a subset for each query. Existing retrieval methods are designed to estimate attention scores or page relevance, but their objectives do not directly account for how approximation errors affect the resulting value weighted attention output. We introduce \method{}, an output aware page encoding derived from the joint structure of keys and values while preserving the key information needed for accurate retrieval. \method{} is training free and requires no additional value dependent statistics at inference time. Once constructed, its stored representation has the same size and decode time scoring cost as a key only spectral representation. Across long reasoning, long context understanding, and long generation benchmarks, \method{} consistently improves over the key only spectral baseline and performs competitively with recent KV cache compression and retrieval methods. On long reasoning benchmarks, it achieves strong avg@\(k\) performance across model benchmark pairs, while matching or surpassing leading baselines on several long context understanding and generation settings with modest decoding overhead. Code is available at \url{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [123] [Learning What to Imitate: Entropy-Aware Distribution Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06671)
*Juan Garcia Giraldo,Matteo Santelmo,Eduard Durech,Imanol Schlag,Valentina Pyatkin,Antoine Bosselut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对小模型模仿教师推理轨迹时的'自信冲突'问题，提出熵感知分布混合方法，以学生预测熵动态门控师生分布插值，稳定蒸馏并提升数学推理与通用能力保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型在模仿强教师模型推理轨迹时，常遇到学生模型对教师强制施加的低概率续写产生'自信冲突'（低熵状态下偏好不同续写），导致泛化下降和灾难性遗忘。

Method: 方法在教师模型和学生模型之间进行知识蒸馏。输入为推理任务提示，教师模型生成推理轨迹。学生模型对每个token产生预测分布。Entropy-Aware Mixing在逐token层级动态混合学生和教师分布：混合权重由学生模型在当前token的预测熵门控。当学生处于低熵状态（自信但可能偏好不同续写）时，降低教师影响以避免'自信冲突'；当学生处于高熵状态（不确定）时，增加教师影响以提供指导。论文实现了两种插值形式：凸插值 p = (1-α)p_student + α p_teacher，以及几何插值 log p = (1-α)log p_student + α log p_teacher。α 由学生熵通过一个调度函数决定（凹、线性或凸）。训练范式有两种：1）离线轨迹生成：教师生成轨迹，然后用推测解码结合熵感知混合生成调整后的轨迹，再进行监督微调（SFT）；2）在线策略蒸馏：学生生成轨迹，使用前向KL散度，在token级混合师生分布进行蒸馏。目标函数最小化学生与混合分布之间的差异。推理时直接使用学生模型，无需教师或额外开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "数学推理性能（分布内与分布外）", "value": "论文声称熵感知混合稳定了蒸馏，改善分布内和分布外数学推理，且比固定教师监督更好地保留通用能力；未提供具体数值", "setting": "小语言模型后训练，离线轨迹生成+推测解码后SFT，以及在线策略前向KL蒸馏"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Entropy-Aware Mixing：以学生模型预测熵为门控，对师生分布进行逐token动态插值；实现凸组合与几何组合两种插值方式；支持离线轨迹生成（通过推测解码后SFT）和在线策略前向KL蒸馏两种训练范式；系统分析熵调度（凹/线性/凸）与训练来源（离线/在线）的匹配关系

Abstract: Small language models are often post-trained as students on reasoning traces from stronger teacher models to efficiently learn new skills. However, token-level imitation on traces that lie far outside the student's expected distribution often produces \textit{confident conflicts}, whereby the student is required to imitate a continuation that it deems unlikely (i.e., low-probability) despite being confident in a different continuation (i.e., in a low-entropy state). To mitigate the degradation in generalisation and catastrophic forgetting caused by these conflicts, we propose \textbf{Entropy-Aware Mixing}: a dynamic per-token interpolation of the student and teacher distributions, gated by the student's predictive entropy. We implement both convex and geometric interpolations for both offline trace generation (via speculative decoding, then SFT) and on-policy forward-KL distillation. Our results show that entropy-aware mixing stabilises distillation, improving in-distribution and out-of-distribution math reasoning while better preserving general capabilities than fixed-teacher supervision. Nonetheless, the optimal entropy schedule depends on the training source, with offline-generated traces favouring concave schedules (greater overall teacher influence) and on-policy training favouring linear or convex schedules (teacher concentrated in high-entropy states).

</details>


### [124] [MatrixFormer: A Foundation Model for Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06751)
*Dwaipayan Saha,Jacob Feitelberg,Kyuseong Choi,Raaz Dwivedi,Anish Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出矩阵原生预训练Transformer基础模型MatrixFormer，单次前向预测所有缺失条目的完整分布，在推荐系统矩阵补全等任务上零样本取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格基础模型将矩阵补全视为逐条目预测，重复使用上下文并丢弃矩阵二维结构，且无法一次性预测全部缺失条目

Method: MatrixFormer 是一种矩阵原生Transformer基础模型。输入为带有缺失条目的矩阵（如用户-物品评分矩阵或面板数据矩阵），模型直接以矩阵形式接收数据并编码其二维结构，而非将每个待预测条目与上下文拼接成独立序列。主要模块包括：矩阵原生编码器，用于在单次前向传播中同时利用行、列及全局结构信息；输出头为每个缺失条目输出一个完整分布，从而支持点估计与不确定性量化。训练阶段完全使用合成的低秩矩阵与潜在因子矩阵，并施加多种缺失模式以模拟真实缺失机制，训练目标为对缺失条目分布的预测（如似然或分布匹配）。推理阶段零样本使用同一模型权重，直接对目标矩阵进行一次前向传播即可得到所有缺失条目的分布预测，无需针对每个目标重复构造上下文。该设计解决现有表格基础模型逐条目预测效率低、上下文重复、丢弃矩阵二维结构的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 矩阵原生Transformer架构，直接建模矩阵二维结构；单次前向传播为每个缺失条目预测完整分布，而非逐条目预测；完全在合成低秩与潜在因子矩阵上预训练，覆盖多种缺失模式；同一模型权重零样本迁移到因果推断面板数据、语言模型基准分数补全、表格插补与推荐系统矩阵补全

Abstract: Matrix completion underlies problems from tabular imputation to causal inference, yet existing tabular foundation models treat it as entry-by-entry prediction, repeating context for every target and discarding the matrix's two-dimensional structure. We introduce MatrixFormer, a pre-trained matrix-native transformer that predicts a full distribution for every missing entry in a single forward pass. MatrixFormer is trained entirely on synthetic low-rank and latent-factor matrices under diverse missingness patterns. Applied zero-shot and with the same model weights, MatrixFormer achieves competitive performance on causal inference panel-data tasks, language-model benchmark-score completion, tabular imputation, and recommendation systems matrix completion. These results position MatrixFormer as a general-purpose foundation model for matrix completion.

</details>


### [125] [Sharpen Without Search: On-Policy Distillation of Sequence-Level Power Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06804)
*Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Seyedarmin Azizi,Arya Fayyazi,Saeid Shokoufa,Mehdi Kamal,Souvik Kundu,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在策略幂蒸馏（OPPD），通过序贯蒙特卡洛采样将幂分布采样能力蒸馏到单次生成中，在MATH500、GSM8K等推理任务上相比未训练模型最高提升27.3点，优于幂采样64候选和GRPO，无需参考答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可能给正确答案更高概率，但由于错误答案的总概率更大，采样时仍常得到错误答案；幂分布采样可改善推理但需要多次评分候选，成本高。如何训练模型一次生成即可产生幂分布采样的答案。

Method: OPPD 使用序贯蒙特卡洛采样进行在策略蒸馏。待训练模型生成完整答案候选，冻结教师模型根据幂分布对候选进行加权（概率的幂次再归一化）。相同的概率权重用于最大似然更新，使模型在单次生成中直接产生幂采样意义上的高质量答案。训练目标包含一个损失系数，控制模型吸收的锐化指数。推理时模型单次生成即可得到答案，无需多候选评分。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "单次生成准确率提升", "value": "最高23.0点", "setting": "在MATH500上，与未训练模型相同温度下比较"}, {"metric": "单次生成准确率提升", "value": "最高27.3点", "setting": "在GSM8K上，与未训练模型相同温度下比较"}, {"metric": "单次生成准确率", "value": "比已发表幂采样（64候选）高2.4点", "setting": "MATH500"}, {"metric": "单次生成准确率", "value": "比已发表幂采样（64候选）高3.5点", "setting": "GSM8K"}, {"metric": "恢复增益比例", "value": "94%", "setting": "相对于16候选给未训练模型带来的增益"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "比GRPO高3.8点", "setting": "MATH500，相同检查点和预算，无参考答案"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "比GRPO高4.0点", "setting": "GSM8K"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "比GRPO高5.4点", "setting": "AIME"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "最高9.3点", "setting": "OPPD在GRPO之后应用，MATH500"}, {"metric": "HumanEval准确率提升", "value": "最高5.3点", "setting": "仅在数学上训练"}, {"metric": "MATH500准确率提升", "value": "4.4点", "setting": "已用验证奖励训练的模型，降低温度无效"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出在策略幂蒸馏（OPPD），使用序贯蒙特卡洛采样，由待训练模型生成候选，冻结教师的幂分布加权候选，并用相同概率进行最大似然更新。；无需参考答案或验证奖励，即可在单次生成中达到幂采样多候选的效果。；通过一个损失系数控制模型吸收的锐化指数在1.19到2.02之间。；在多个模型家族和规模上验证增益，包括已用验证奖励训练的模型。

Abstract: A language model can give a correct answer more probability than any single incorrect answer and still usually sample an incorrect one, because the incorrect answers together hold more probability. The power distribution raises each complete answer's probability to a power above one and renormalizes, shifting probability toward answers the model finds most likely (sharpening). Sampling from it improves reasoning without changing parameters, but needs many scored candidates per query. We show that a model can instead be trained to produce such answers in one generation. On-policy power distillation (OPPD) runs a sequential Monte Carlo sampler in which the model being trained generates candidates and a frozen teacher's power distribution weights them; the same probabilities weight each answer in a maximum-likelihood update. Training raises single-generation accuracy by up to 23.0 points on MATH500 and 27.3 on GSM8K over the untrained model at the same temperature, and one generation scores 2.4 and 3.5 points above published power sampling with 64 candidates, recovering 94 percent of the gain that 16 candidates give the untrained model. For context, against GRPO trained with verified rewards from the same checkpoint and budget, OPPD scores 3.8, 4.0 and 5.4 points higher on MATH500, GSM8K and AIME using no reference answers; the two are complementary, and OPPD applied after GRPO adds up to 9.3 points. Trained only on mathematics, OPPD raises HumanEval accuracy by up to 5.3 points. One loss coefficient moves the sharpening exponent the model absorbs between 1.19 and 2.02, against 1.14 for ordinary on-policy distillation, and it rises mostly on the model's own answers. Gains hold across model families and sizes, including a model already trained with verified rewards, where lowering the temperature gives nothing and OPPD adds 4.4 points on MATH500. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [126] [Closing the Context Gap: Activation Alignment for Tabular In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06679)
*Yoel Zeldes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出激活对齐方法，通过轻量线性变换将数据受限学生模型中间激活映射到全上下文教师激活，无需GPU训练，在TabPFN-3和TabFM的38个数据集上显著提升有限上下文下的预测性能，低数据机制下恢复近半教师优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型在上下文学习中需处理所有训练样本导致每次预测成本过高，限制上下文数量虽降低开销但显著损害性能。

Method: 方法基于表格基础模型的上下文学习框架。教师模型使用全部训练样本作为上下文，学生模型仅使用部分上下文。训练阶段在合成无标签数据上运行教师和学生，提取学生中间层的激活和教师对应的中间层激活，学习一个轻量线性变换（对齐器）使学生激活尽可能接近教师激活。对齐器训练无需GPU，收敛快。推理阶段，学生模型在有限上下文下前向传播，其激活经线性对齐器变换后再继续后续计算，从而在保持少量上下文推理速度的同时，获得接近全上下文教师的预测性能。整体优化目标是最小化学生对齐后激活与教师激活之间的差异。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预测性能提升", "value": "广泛且统计显著优于未对齐基线", "setting": "TabArena 38个分类数据集，TabPFN-3和TabFM，所有上下文预算"}, {"metric": "教师优势恢复比例", "value": "低数据机制下恢复近一半", "setting": "低数据机制，与全上下文教师对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出激活对齐方法：利用全上下文教师模型指导数据受限学生模型学习，通过轻量线性变换将学生中间激活映射到教师中间激活。；在合成无标签数据上训练线性对齐器，无需GPU，几秒到几分钟收敛。；在TabPFN-3和TabFM两个主流表格基础模型上验证，覆盖38个分类数据集，对齐学生模型在所有上下文预算下均取得统计显著提升。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models perform in-context learning (ICL) by conditioning predictions on labeled training examples provided as context. Unlike traditional models that separate training from inference, these models must process all training examples in every forward pass, making each prediction expensive. Restricting the number of training examples reduces this cost but substantially degrades performance. Instead of discarding context, we propose activation alignment, a method that leverages the full context to teach a model how to behave when seeing only a subset. This is achieved by training a lightweight linear transformation on synthetic unlabeled data to map the intermediate activations of a data-constrained "student" (using partial context) toward those of a full-context "teacher" (using all data). Training the aligner requires no GPU and converges in seconds to minutes on commodity hardware. We evaluate on 38 classification datasets from the TabArena benchmark using the leading two tabular foundation models, TabPFN-3 and TabFM. Across all context budgets, the aligned student yields broad, statistically significant improvements over the unaligned baseline for both models. In low-data regimes, alignment recovers nearly half of the teacher's predictive advantage. The method provides a practical, low-overhead approach to achieving the inference speed of compact contexts while closing a significant fraction of the performance gap to the full-context teacher.

</details>


### [127] [BRANCH-MoE: Balance-Aware Tree Routing for Large Embedding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06725)
*Gang Fu,Adel Javanmard,MohammadHossein Bateni,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRANCH-MoE提出平衡感知的树路由架构，将专家放在二叉决策树叶子并用指数移动平均估计分支概率，无需辅助负载均衡损失即可均衡利用专家，并在Criteo等CTR任务上验证，同时从理论上说明通信与收敛性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扁平MoE路由存在专家利用率不均衡、专家索引无拓扑结构、负载均衡依赖辅助损失，以及专家跨设备通信开销大的问题。

Method: BRANCH-MoE将E个专家放置在深度为log2(E)的二叉决策树叶子节点上。输入经过模型底层后到达树路由层；在每个内部节点，分支概率不以可学习的独立偏置直接决定，而是以到达该节点的流量加权平均得分为中心，并用指数移动平均(EMA)进行估计。该EMA估计诱导出噪声-滞后权衡：更新越快则响应新分布越快但噪声大，更新越慢则噪声小但滞后大。该机制在不引入辅助负载均衡损失的前提下，促进左右子树都被利用。当专家按树前缀分配到不同设备时，靠近根节点的决策如果置信度高，则大部分流量只进入局部子树，从而减少跨设备通信。训练目标仍是主任务损失（如CTR二分类交叉熵），路由分支概率由EMA统计动态调整；理论上在线性节点映射与对数凹到达分布下可防止路由质量坍塌；冻结路由器时，专家执行频率控制其随机梯度收敛速率。推理时按树逐层分支，最终将样本路由到叶子专家执行，通信开销受树前缀分配与根部决策置信度影响。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "CTR预测任务质量", "value": "保持任务质量与均衡专家利用率，支持局部化专家共激活并降低通信", "setting": "在Criteo点击率预测、Forest Covertype、HIGGS、YearPredictionMSD数据集上，E=16，top-4路由，5个随机种子，对比Switch softmax、DeepSeek-V3动态偏置、Skywork logit归一化和确定性哈希路由。"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将E个专家放置在深度为log2(E)的二叉树叶子节点上，使专家索引具有层次拓扑结构。；在每个内部节点使用到达流量加权的均值分支概率，并以指数移动平均估计，从而在不需要辅助负载均衡损失的情况下促进两个子树的利用。；证明了该移动平均估计存在显式的噪声-滞后权衡，并在线性节点映射与对数凹到达分布下防止路由质量坍塌。；证明在冻结路由器下专家执行频率控制其随机梯度收敛速率，并说明靠近根节点的置信决策可界定按树前缀分配专家到设备时的跨设备通信。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) layers increase model capacity without a proportional increase in per-example computation. However, conventional flat routers can yield imbalanced expert utilization and treat experts as an unstructured collection, whose indices carry no topological meaning. We introduce {\bf BRANCH-MoE}, a routing architecture that places \(E\) experts at the leaves of a binary decision tree of depth \(\log_2 E\). At each internal node the branching probability is centered on the arrival-weighted mean score of the traffic reaching that node. This mean is estimated using an exponential moving average, which promotes utilization of both child subtrees without an auxiliary load-balancing loss. We show that this moving-average estimate admits an explicit noise-lag trade-off. We prove that for linear node maps and log-concave arrival distributions, this mechanism prevents routing-mass collapse. We further establish that, under a frozen router, an expert's execution frequency controls its stochastic-gradient convergence rate, and that confident decisions near the root bound cross-device communication when experts are assigned to devices by tree prefix. We evaluate BRANCH-MoE against Switch softmax, DeepSeek-V3 dynamic-bias, Skywork logit-normalized, and deterministic hash routing on Criteo click-through-rate prediction, Forest Covertype, HIGGS, and YearPredictionMSD, using \(E=16\), top-\(4\) routing, and five random seeds. Our results show that hierarchical routing can preserve task quality and balanced utilization while inducing a topology that supports localized expert co-activation and reduced communication.

</details>


### [128] [Training-Free Transformer Merging via Sequential Local Operator Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06415)
*Akansh Maurya,Ya-Wei Eileen Lin,Stefanie Jegelka,Sebastian U Stich,Rotem Mulayoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无需训练的 Transformer 合并方法 Sequential Local Operator Alignment，沿部分合并模型的执行路径顺序对齐功能算子并用校准数据估计局部行为，再把合并算子分解回合法参数，支持秩扩展提升多任务容量；在 CLIP、RoBERTa、十亿参数 LLM 与 LoRA 合并上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无需训练地将多个微调后的 Transformer 模型合并为单一模型时，逐层独立合并会破坏共享注意力计算（QK/VO 算子由组合矩阵决定），且合并前层后下游组件收到的激活与原始路径不一致，导致执行路径错配与误差累积。

Method: 方法在无需额外训练的条件下合并多个已微调 Transformer。输入为若干微调模型（如 CLIP、RoBERTa、十亿参数 LLM 以及 LoRA 微调模型）与一组校准数据。流程沿部分合并模型的执行路径进行：对每个功能组件（注意力中的 QK 与 VO 算子、以及 FFN 等），先用校准数据估计其在当前输入分布下的局部行为；然后按顺序对齐算子，使合并后的组件在部分合并模型产生的中间激活上匹配原模型行为，从而缓解逐层独立合并造成的路径错配与误差累积；随后将对齐后的合并算子分解回合法的 Transformer 参数矩阵。该分解步骤支持秩扩展，即提升合并算子的秩，从而在不重新训练的情况下增加多任务容量，并在精度、推理成本之间提供可调权衡。训练目标为对齐局部算子行为（无需标注数据、无需梯度优化），推理时直接使用合并后的标准 Transformer 参数进行前向计算。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Sequential Local Operator Alignment：沿部分合并模型的执行路径顺序对齐功能算子，而非逐层独立合并；使用校准数据估计每个功能组件的局部行为，减少跨层误差累积；算子分解回合法 Transformer 参数的 factorization 步骤，支持秩扩展以提升多任务容量；自然扩展到 LoRA 微调模型的合并

Abstract: Training-free model merging aims to combine multiple fine-tuned models into a single model without further optimization on labeled data. Yet, in transformers, independently merging individual layers can affect a shared attention computation because the query-key and value-output operators depend on composed matrices, overlooking the functional structure. Moreover, when merging earlier components, downstream components receive different activations than they do in the original model, thus, the merged and original execution paths no longer match. In this paper, we introduce Sequential Local Operator Alignment, a training-free method that merges transformers along the execution path of the partially merged model. Our method uses calibration data to estimate the local behavior of each functional component, aligns operators sequentially under the intermediate activation of the partially merged model, and subsequently factorizes the merged operators back into valid transformer parameters. We empirically show that this sequential step reduces error accumulation across layers. Furthermore, the proposed operator factorization step enables rank expansion, providing a principled mechanism for increasing multi-task capacity. We demonstrate that our approach generalizes across modalities, model scales, and varying numbers of tasks, from CLIP and RoBERTa to billion-parameter LLMs, and further extends naturally to the merging of LoRA-fine-tuned models. The results indicate improvements over strong merging baselines without requiring rank expansion, while optional expansion provides a further accuracy-inference-cost trade-off. Project link: this https URL

</details>


### [129] [Dynamic Minimax Regret Optimization for Robust LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06329)
*Chengbo Zang,Haoyu Dong,Mehmet Kerem Turkcan,Gil Zussman,Zoran Kostic,Javad Ghaderi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DUCB-OGD，一种用于组分布鲁棒LLM后训练的动态极小极大后悔算法，通过折扣UCB采样器和在线梯度下降优化器的耦合，在无需重新评估历史数据的约束下跟踪最差数据源，并具有理论保证，实验表明能提升最差组鲁棒性且计算开销可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在动态环境且仅能获取瞬时小批量bandit反馈、不重新评估历史数据的实际约束下，如何实现组分布鲁棒的LLM后训练，以提升最差组性能。

Method: 框架将LLM后训练建模为两玩家的采样器-优化器过程：采样器从K个数据源中适应性地选择当前批次数据，优化器使用所选源的随机梯度更新模型参数。采样器维护每个源的损失指数移动平均估计和基于折扣有效样本量的置信半径，采用折扣上置信界（DUCB）策略选择源，以跟踪瞬时最差源；优化器使用在线梯度下降（OGD）更新模型。算法不重新评估历史数据，仅利用新批次反馈更新估计，因此能无缝集成到标准训练流程中。理论上证明了动态极小极大后悔界。推理阶段沿用常规LLM推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最差组鲁棒性", "value": "改进（未提供具体数值）", "setting": "有监督微调、偏好优化、强化学习实验，与标准采样基线对比"}, {"metric": "计算开销", "value": "可忽略（与标准采样基线相比）", "setting": "同上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将LLM后训练形式化为两玩家的采样器-优化器动态极小极大后悔问题。；提出DUCB-OGD算法，耦合折扣上置信界采样器和在线梯度下降优化器，仅依赖折扣有效样本量维持损失估计与置信半径，无需重新评估历史数据或改动标准训练流程。；证明动态极小极大后悔上界为Õ(K^{1/4}T^{3/4})，在对数因子下达到该反馈模型下未折扣目标的最优。

Abstract: Modern LLM training increasingly relies on heterogeneous data sources spanning different domains, tasks, preference distributions, and difficulty levels. We study dynamic minimax regret for group-distributionally robust LLM post-training under instantaneous mini-batch-only bandit feedback. The framework views the training as a two-player sampler-optimizer process: a sampler adaptively selects among data sources using bandit feedback, while an optimizer updates the model parameters using stochastic gradients from the selected source. We focus on the practically restrictive setting where source losses evolve with model training but historical data are not re-evaluated, requiring the sampler to track instantaneous worst-sources from stale partial feedback. We propose DUCB-OGD, a simple and scalable algorithm that couples a Discounted Upper-Confidence-Bound sampler with an Online Gradient Descent optimizer. The sampler maintains exponential moving average loss estimates and confidence radii based on discounted effective sample sizes, avoiding costly re-evaluation of past data or intrusive changes to standard training pipelines. For $K$ data sources and $T$ training steps, we prove that DUCB-OGD achieves a dynamic minimax regret of $\tilde{O}(K^{1/4}T^{3/4})$, which is optimal up to logarithmic factors for the undiscounted objective under our feedback model. Extensive experiments across supervised fine-tuning, preference optimization, and reinforcement learning show that DUCB-OGD integrates seamlessly into modern LLM training pipelines and improves worst-group robustness with negligible computational overhead compared with standard sampling baselines.

</details>


### [130] [RoSA: Rotational Sparse Adaptation for Memory-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06243)
*Muhammad Azeem Lodhi,Chao Zhou,Rebekka Burkholz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RoSA 提出一种旋转式稀疏适配策略：训练时冻结靠近输入的层，并让可训练块在后续层间旋转，逐步增加冻结层数，从而降低优化器状态显存、缩短反向传播，并可通过缓存激活缩短前向传播；该方法与现有 PEFT/稀疏优化器正交，在多种 LLM 架构与任务上保持较强微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在参数高效微调（PEFT）中进一步降低优化器状态显存与反向传播开销，同时保持微调性能，从而在有限显存下适配更大或更多的基础模型。

Method: RoSA 在标准 LLM 微调流程上进行改造。输入为训练序列，模型由若干 Transformer 层组成。训练开始时，靠近输入端的较低层被冻结，一组连续的可训练块位于后续层中。随着训练进行，该可训练块在层之间“旋转”，同时逐步把靠近输入端的层加入冻结集合。冻结层不更新参数，因此不产生对应优化器状态，直接降低优化器状态显存；由于反向传播不需要穿过这些冻结层，反向路径变短；若在训练过程中缓存最后一个冻结层的激活值，则前向传播也可缩短。RoSA 不改变层内可训练参数化方式：在活跃块内部仍可采用任意 PEFT 方法或稀疏优化器。训练目标沿用原任务的损失函数，只是把梯度更新限制在每次活跃块内的可训练参数上。推理阶段与常规微调后模型一致，不引入额外推理开销，网络结构不改变，仅参数更新范围发生调度变化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "峰值显存", "value": "降低（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "跨多种 LLM 架构与任务的微调实验"}, {"metric": "微调性能", "value": "保持较强（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "跨多种 LLM 架构与任务的微调实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: RoSA（Rotational Sparse Adaptation）：训练中固定靠近输入的较低层，并将可训练块在后续层之间旋转，逐步增加靠近输入的冻结层数量。；该设计缩短反向传播路径；若缓存最后一个冻结层的激活，还可缩短前向传播。；与可训练参数化正交，可与现有 PEFT 方法或稀疏优化器组合使用。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) reduces the cost of adapting foundation models by focusing training on a small parameter subset. Complementary to this idea, we introduce RoSA (Rotational Sparse Adaptation), which narrows adaptation to a subset of layers at a time. RoSA freezes lower layers close to the input throughout training and rotates a trainable block over later layers, progressively increasing the number of frozen layers close to the input. This design reduces optimizer-state memory, shortens backpropagation, and even forward propagation if activations at the last frozen layer are cached. Because RoSA is orthogonal to the choice of trainable parameterization, it can be combined with PEFT methods or sparse optimizers within each active block. Experiments across multiple LLM architectures and tasks show that RoSA reduces peak memory while maintaining strong fine-tuning performance.

</details>


### [131] [ORCA: The Annealed Spectral Conditioning Optimizer for Faster, Better LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06116)
*Yuanshi Liu,Boyuan Jiang,Liang Hou,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Zhouchen Lin,Cong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA 提出一种训练早期施加强软正交正则、后期移除的优化器干预，在 LLaMA、Qwen3 和 MoE（130M–8B）上取得比 Muon 更低的最终验证损失，且无需架构改动、额外开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 LLM 优化器（如 Muon）产生的权重矩阵有效秩较高，但进一步的光谱控制收益有限；论文识别出集中谱会抑制耦合权重矩阵中的梯度方向、拖慢优化，而全程维持约束又会限制任务适应并抬高可达损失下界，因此需要一种在训练早期塑造谱、后期释放的优化策略。

Method: ORCA 是一种对现有优化器（以 Muon 为基线）的最小干预：在训练优化过程中，对权重矩阵施加软正交正则化，并设置调度使该正则化在训练早期较强、随后冷却移除。其数据流为：输入训练批次，计算梯度，优化器更新权重；在更新目标中额外加入软正交正则项，正则强度随时间衰减至零，后期仅保留原始优化器目标。该设计旨在早期通过正交约束使权重谱更宽、避免梯度方向被抑制，后期移除约束以实现任务特定适应，从而降低最终验证损失下界。论文未披露具体网络架构改动，且明确无需架构变化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最终验证损失降低相对 Muon", "value": "ORCA 相对 Muon 的损失下降幅度等于或超过 Muon 相对 Adam 的下降幅度", "setting": "LLaMA、Qwen3、细粒度 MoE，130M–8B 参数，训练至收敛的验证损失对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 ORCA（Orthogonal Regularization, Cooled After），在训练早期施加强但临时的软正交正则化，之后移除该约束。；通过“早期塑形、后期释放”的设计，让权重早期获得更宽谱，后期自由适应任务，从而降低最终验证损失。；在 LLaMA、Qwen3 以及细粒度混合专家模型上，130M 至 8B 参数规模验证 ORCA 相对 Muon 的最终验证损失更低。；ORCA 无需修改模型架构，额外开销极小。

Abstract: Modern LLM optimizers such as Muon often produce weight matrices with higher effective rank than Adam, yet further spectral control has delivered only modest gains. We identify a tension behind this result: concentrated spectra can suppress gradient directions in coupled weight matrices and slow optimization, while constraints maintained throughout training can limit task-specific adaptation and raise the attainable loss floor. We introduce ORCA (Orthogonal Regularization, Cooled After), a minimal optimizer intervention that applies strong but temporary soft orthogonality regularization early in training, then removes it. This allows the weights to benefit from a broader spectrum early on and adapt freely afterward. Across LLaMA, Qwen3, and fine-grained mixture-of-experts models ranging from 130M to 8B parameters, ORCA achieves lower final validation loss than Muon. Its loss reduction relative to Muon matches or exceeds Muon's reduction relative to Adam. Ablations support the early-shaping, later-release design. Further, ORCA requires no architectural changes and adds minimal overhead.

</details>


### [132] [Transfer-Stratified On-Policy Distillation for RL-Improved Reasoning Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05974)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Bo Shao,Tiangang Zhu,Bintao Wu,Linjun Shou,Fengge Wu,Feng Sun,Wenbiao Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究 RL 改进的推理教师模型向更小学生模型蒸馏时的迁移特性，提出 TS-OPD 框架，根据学生-教师联合采样成功率对问题分层，并路由至带门控的前向/反向 KL 损失，同时用熵刹车保护采样覆盖。离线数学推理实验表明 TS-OPD 在 macro 平均正确率上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 RL 改进后的推理教师模型在监督更小的 on-policy 学生模型时，哪些改进能够有效迁移，并设计迁移感知的蒸馏目标以提升学生表现。

Method: TS-OPD 是一个基于 on-policy 蒸馏的两阶段训练框架。输入为训练问题集，首先利用学生模型和教师模型对每个问题分别采样，计算联合采样成功率，据此将问题分层为需要习得（acquisition）或巩固（consolidation）的类型。对于习得型问题，采用带门控的前向 KL 损失，让学生从教师的输出分布中学习新知识；对于巩固型问题，采用带门控的反向 KL 损失，使学生聚焦于教师已有而学生尚未掌握的区域。此外引入熵刹车项，当学生输出分布的熵过低时施加惩罚，防止采样覆盖度下降。整体训练目标由上述分层损失与熵刹车加权组成，通过 on-policy 方式采样学生轨迹并优化，推理时直接使用训练后的学生模型生成答案。该架构旨在解决教师到学生迁移过程中监督信号方向与 token 预算不匹配的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "macro average correctness", "value": "TS-OPD 为最强学生目标（与 GRPO 改进教师配对时）", "setting": "数学推理任务，主对比实验"}, {"metric": "pass@K", "value": "表现更混合（mixed）", "setting": "同上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现蒸馏迁移是分层结构而非标量：教师强度本身不足以使稠密蒸馏具有竞争力，RL 改进的教师产生的增益依赖于具体指标。；提出 Transfer-Stratified On-Policy Distillation (TS-OPD)：根据学生和教师的联合采样成功率对训练问题进行分层。；将习得（acquisition）问题路由至带门控的前向 KL，将巩固（consolidation）问题路由至带门控的反向 KL。；引入熵刹车（entropy brake）以保护采样覆盖度。；消融表明增益来自路由和 token 门控，而非跳过问题。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning can substantially improve a reasoning teacher, but it is unclear which of those improvements survive when the teacher supervises a smaller on-policy student. We study this question in mathematical reasoning by comparing teacher lineages before and after GRPO, multiple student scales, direct GRPO, and several on-policy distillation objectives. The central finding is that transfer is structured rather than scalar: teacher strength alone does not make dense distillation competitive, while an RL-improved teacher creates useful but metric-dependent student gains. This motivates Transfer-Stratified On-Policy Distillation (TS-OPD), which screens training problems by the joint sampled success of the student and teacher, routes acquisition problems to gated forward KL, routes consolidation problems to gated reverse KL, and adds an entropy brake to protect sampled coverage. Across the main comparison, TS-OPD is the strongest student objective for macro average correctness with the GRPO-improved teacher, while pass@K remains more mixed. Ablations show that the gains come from routing and token gating rather than skipping problems. These results support a transfer-aware view of OPD: stronger teachers help when the supervision direction and token budget match the student's observed ability, not merely because the teacher endpoint is stronger.

</details>


### [133] [Discovered, Not Designed: Population Evolution for Collaborative and Compute-Intensive Model Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05950)
*Bo Peng,Lizhu Zhang,Yuhang Zhou,Mingyi Wang,Yifan Wu,Serena Li,Xiangjun Fan,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Population Evolution（PE）协同分层演化框架，用共享实验证据连接多个 LLM 驱动的局部模型搜索，并通过小规模筛选再晋升到大训练规模来降低昂贵训练成本；配套 RMD-Bench 覆盖 ranking、watch-time、RL 算法与 LLM/VLM 预训练，ranking 与 watch-time 局部增益分别提升到 8.97% 与 3.85%，匹配 GPU 算力下 LLM 发现相对准确率增益 2.48% 对 0.92%、VLM loss 降低 8.78% 对 5.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 驱动的模型演化存在两个未解决问题：如何找到能跨相关任务迁移的模型设计，以及如何在训练代价高昂时仍能持续改进

Method: PE 是一个协同、分层的 LLM 驱动模型发现框架。输入是代码改动提案与相关训练实例集合；每个局部搜索（local search）由一个 LLM proposer 生成候选代码改动，在相关训练实例上评估，并把实验结果写入共享的实验证据池。不同局部搜索通过该证据池相互连接：后续提案会参考他人已提交的改动与评估结果，避免重复探索并引导搜索方向。评估采用分层机制：先在较小训练子集上做同伴评估（peer evaluation）与中间评估（intermediate evaluation）筛选候选，筛选通过的候选才被晋升（promotion）到更大的训练规模乃至全目标训练，从而在昂贵训练预算下控制成本。整个过程以迭代方式运行，候选设计在源迭代次数匹配的条件下与 standalone evolution 对比，并跨 ranking、watch-time、RL 算法发现、LLM/VLM 预训练等目标验证迁移能力。训练目标即各目标任务自身的损失/指标；推理路径与常规模型一致，PE 本身不改变线上服务链路，而是产出更优的模型设计。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均最佳局部增益", "value": "7.01% -> 8.97%", "setting": "ranking 任务，匹配源迭代次数下 standalone evolution 对比 PE"}, {"metric": "平均最佳局部增益", "value": "2.84% -> 3.85%", "setting": "watch-time 任务，匹配源迭代次数下 standalone evolution 对比 PE"}, {"metric": "最佳更大规模结果", "value": "提升", "setting": "三个联合发现（joint-discovery）家族全部改善"}, {"metric": "更大规模最佳增益", "value": "提升", "setting": "watch-time 发现，五个 harness 中的四个"}, {"metric": "更大规模最佳增益", "value": "提升", "setting": "watch-time 发现，四个 proposer 全部"}, {"metric": "平均性能", "value": "全部 PE 设计优于参考（reference）", "setting": "新推荐数据集，共享目标侧校准"}, {"metric": "最佳相对准确率增益", "value": "2.48%", "setting": "匹配总 GPU 算力下完成的 LLM 发现运行，PE"}, {"metric": "成功候选数", "value": "13", "setting": "匹配总 GPU 算力下完成的 LLM 发现运行，PE"}, {"metric": "最佳相对准确率增益", "value": "0.92%", "setting": "匹配总 GPU 算力下完成的 LLM 发现运行，direct evolution"}, {"metric": "成功候选数", "value": "0", "setting": "匹配总 GPU 算力下完成的 LLM 发现运行，direct evolution"}, {"metric": "VLM loss 降低", "value": "8.78%", "setting": "匹配总 GPU 算力，PE"}, {"metric": "VLM loss 降低", "value": "5.05%", "setting": "匹配总 GPU 算力，direct evolution"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Population Evolution（PE）协同分层演化框架，通过共享实验证据连接多个局部搜索；跨相关训练实例评估代码改动并共享结果，引导后续提案与向更大训练规模的晋升；针对昂贵目标先用小训练子集搜索，再经过同伴评估与中间评估筛选候选，最后做全目标训练；RMD-Bench 基准，覆盖 ranking、watch-time prediction、RL 算法发现与 LLM/VLM 预训练

Abstract: LLM-driven evolution enables iterative model development, but two practical goals remain underexplored: finding model designs that transfer across related tasks and sustaining improvement when training is expensive. We introduce Population Evolution (PE), a collaborative, hierarchical framework that connects ongoing local searches through shared experimental evidence. PE evaluates code changes across related training instances and shares the results to guide subsequent proposals and promotion to larger training scales. For expensive targets, PE searches small training subsets and screens candidates through peer and intermediate evaluations before full-target training. We introduce RMD-Bench to evaluate both settings across ranking, watch-time prediction, RL algorithm discovery, and LLM/VLM pretraining. Compared with standalone evolution at matched source iterations, PE raises mean best local gains from 7.01% to 8.97% in ranking and from 2.84% to 3.85% in watch-time, while improving the best larger-scale outcome in all three joint-discovery families. In watch-time discovery, PE improves best larger-scale gains with four of five harnesses and all four proposers. On new recommendation datasets under shared target-side calibration, every evaluated PE design improves over the reference in mean performance. Under matched total GPU compute, completed LLM discovery runs yield a best relative accuracy gain of 2.48% and 13 successful candidates for PE, versus 0.92% and none for direct evolution. VLM loss reduction reaches 8.78% versus 5.05% under matched total GPU compute.

</details>


### [134] [ThunderSyncRL: Lossless Acceleration of Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05935)
*Seil Kang,Hangoo Kang,Tarun Suresh,Youngeun Kim,Shreyas Pimpalgaonkar,Seong Jae Hwang,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ThunderSyncRL 提出无策略陈旧的梯度流式训练框架，用于智能体强化学习后训练；在 GRPO 中按轨迹奖励到达即计算梯度，在 OPD 中按已完成回合教师打分即计算梯度，理论证明与批同步更新等价，实验在 SWE-bench Verified 和 Terminal Bench 4.0 上最高实现 1.9× 加速，并比异步训练在固定预算下高 2.47 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步式智能体强化学习后训练中，学习器引擎必须等待 rollout 与验证全部完成，形成流水线气泡，而异步训练会引入策略陈旧（policy staleness），影响收敛与最终效果。

Method: ThunderSyncRL 是一个面向智能体强化学习后训练的执行框架，核心是在不引入策略陈旧的前提下消除同步训练的流水线气泡。输入为智能体在交互式环境中的长时序异构轨迹，每条轨迹包含多个回合（agentic turn）、工具调用、环境反馈以及奖励/验证信号。框架维持与同步训练相同的数据依赖：对于 GRPO，需要根据同一组内所有轨迹的奖励计算组相对优势；对于 OPD，需要教师模型对每个已完成回合的动作打分。与批同步不同，ThunderSyncRL 将梯度计算拆解为可按轨迹或按回合独立触发的流式更新。当 GRPO 中某条轨迹的奖励到达时，立即基于当前固定的组统计量计算该轨迹的分数梯度并累积；由于组内其他轨迹尚未完成，组统计量保持为已确定输入，从而不产生策略滞后。对于 OPD，当某个智能体回合完成且教师已对该回合动作评分后，即可在沙箱中继续执行后续工具调用和其他回合的同时，并行计算该回合的梯度。训练目标保持不变：GRPO 仍优化组相对策略梯度，OPD 仍最小化与教师动作分布之间的蒸馏损失，只是更新时机从批级同步变为流式累积。作者证明流式梯度更新在与批同步相同的目标函数下产生等价的参数更新。推理/服务路径上，rollout 与验证在环境沙箱中持续进行，学习器引擎按可用输入实时消费并更新参数，避免等待整批完成，从而在 SWE-bench Verified 与 Terminal Bench 4.0 上达到同等性能时训练速度最高提升 1.9×，并在相同预算下相比异步训练因零策略陈旧而取得最高 2.47 个百分点的性能优势。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "训练加速比（达到同等性能）", "value": "最高 1.9×", "setting": "SWE-bench Verified 与 Terminal Bench 4.0，与同步训练对比"}, {"metric": "性能提升（固定预算下）", "value": "最高 2.47 个百分点", "setting": "零策略陈旧条件下与异步训练对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出梯度流式（gradient streaming）思想：在满足所需输入固定后立即开始梯度计算，不等待整个组完成，同时不引入策略陈旧。；针对 GRPO，单条轨迹的奖励一到就计算该轨迹的分数梯度，而不是等整组轨迹收齐再算。；针对在线策略蒸馏（OPD），在工具调用在沙箱中运行时，即对已完成回合中教师打分过的动作计算梯度。；理论证明梯度流式产生与批同步训练相同的 GRPO 和 OPD 更新，不改变任何目标函数。

Abstract: Language models are moving beyond generating answers to pursuing long-horizon goals in interactive environments. Post-training these agents requires long, heterogeneous trajectories, and synchronous systems leave learner engines idle until rollout and verification finish. To squeeze out these pipeline bubbles, asynchronous training overlaps rollout and learning across updates, but comes at the cost of policy staleness. We introduce ThunderSyncRL, which starts gradient computation as soon as all required inputs are fixed, without policy staleness. For group relative policy optimization (GRPO), ThunderSyncRL computes each trajectory's score gradient as soon as the reward for that trajectory arrives, without waiting for the group. For on-policy distillation (OPD), it computes gradients for each completed agentic turn's teacher-scored actions while tool calls run in the sandbox. We prove that gradient streaming produces the same GRPO and OPD updates as batch-synchronous training, without changing either objective. On SWE-bench Verified and Terminal Bench 4.0, we train models to the same performance up to $1.9 \times$ faster than synchronous training. With zero policy staleness, ThunderSyncRL also outperforms asynchronous training at a fixed budget by up to $2.47$ percentage points.

</details>


### [135] [Collaborative Personalized Preference Alignment for LLMs under Data Deficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05898)
*Liyan Yang,Yige Yuan,Zhiqin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLM个性化对齐中用户反馈稀缺且偏好异构导致的梯度冲突问题，提出APO方法，通过用户分组、帕累托最优初始化学习及少样本迭代精炼，在Fed-ChatbotPA和UltraFeedback上仅用20个本地示例即取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在用户偏好异构且用户反馈稀缺的情况下，如何协同学习对齐器初始化，从而支持对多样用户偏好的少样本适应并避免用户间及用户内目标梯度冲突。

Method: APO方法首先根据用户更新方向的兼容性进行用户分组，使得组内用户信息可以低干扰地合并。在每个组内，采用梯度下降与有控制上升相结合的方式协调竞争目标，朝着帕累托前沿上偏好特定的点移动，从而生成一个靠近组内用户最优点的初始化。随后，通过少样本本地适应获得的更新迭代精炼该初始化，使其对个性化更加有效。论文还给出了单本地步协同更新的条件次优性界，并分析初始化误差如何影响后续随机适应。具体网络结构、输入表征及优化目标等细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "改进", "value": "一致优于现有方法", "setting": "Fed-ChatbotPA与UltraFeedback数据集，仅使用20个本地示例"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出APO方法，将更新方向兼容的用户分组，减少信息合并时的干扰。；在组内结合梯度下降与有控制上升，协调竞争目标，逼近帕累托前沿上的偏好特定点。；利用少样本局部适应的更新迭代精炼初始化，提升个性化效果。；为单局部步的协同更新建立条件次优性界，并刻画初始化误差对后续随机适应的影响。

Abstract: Real-world users often exhibit highly heterogeneous preferences over multiple objectives for LLM responses. A lightweight aligner can tailor these responses to individual preferences, but scarce user-specific feedback makes personalized training difficult. Learning shared initializations across users can support few-shot adaptation. However, heterogeneous preferences and competing objectives cause gradient conflicts across users and within each user, hindering effective initialization learning. This raises a central question: \textbf{how can we collaboratively learn aligner initializations that support few-shot adaptation to diverse user preferences?} To answer this question, we propose \textbf{A}pproximate \textbf{P}areto \textbf{O}ptimality (APO). We first group users whose updates are compatible, so that their information can be combined with less interference. Within each group, we combine gradient descent with controlled ascent to coordinate competing objectives and move towards preference-specific points on the Pareto front. This produces an initialization that is close to the optima of the users in the group. We then iteratively refine it using updates from few-shot local adaptation, making it more effective for personalization. Furthermore, we establish conditional suboptimality bounds for a one-local-step collaborative update and characterize how initialization error affects subsequent stochastic adaptation. Experiments on Fed-ChatbotPA and UltraFeedback show consistent improvements over existing methods using only 20 local examples.

</details>


### [136] [Underscoring the Problem: Why Softpick Fails at Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05488)
*Aryan Sood,Jaikaran Singh,Ishaan Bansal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将softpick注意力在扩展时的训练失败归因于初始化时正负分母混合导致的梯度爆炸和死行问题，提出分离分母的算子家族和stop-gradient变体，在230M参数模型上实现从零训练，并在死头、passkey检索等指标上优于softpick。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softpick注意力在扩展时训练失败的根本原因在于初始化时正负分母混合，导致梯度爆炸和死行问题，无法从零训练。

Method: 论文研究softmax注意力的变体softpick。Softpick通过对注意力分数进行整流（rectify）来消除注意力汇聚（sinks）并降低隐藏状态峰度。标准softmax注意力中每个token获得非零权重，导致在训练模型中注意力汇聚和大量激活，增加低精度推理的动态范围。Softpick去除了这一约束。本文发现softpick在扩展时优势减弱，并将其失败重新定义为归一化问题：softpick的分母可分解为正移位和D+与负移位和D-，前向和反向传播中两者使用方式相同，无法分离其角色。作者将D+和D-分离，构建了一个算子家族，每个算子独立选择每个分母。失败源于初始化：每一层都存在D+恰好为零的行，而近死行产生超过10^12的梯度范数，无论反向分母如何。只有softpick和一个stop-gradient变体（前向保留D+ + D-，反向仅通过D+传播）能够从零训练。在230M参数规模下，stop-gradient算子在量化上匹配softpick，死头更少，更可靠地检索passkey，仅在峰值注意力权重峰度上落后。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "梯度范数", "value": ">10^12", "setting": "近死行，与反向分母无关"}, {"metric": "死头数量", "value": "更少", "setting": "230M参数模型，stop-gradient与softpick对比"}, {"metric": "passkey检索准确率", "value": "更可靠", "setting": "230M参数模型"}, {"metric": "量化性能", "value": "匹配softpick", "setting": "230M参数模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将softpick分母分解为独立的正、负部分（D+和D-），并构建一个灵活选择分母的算子家族。；识别出训练失败源于初始化时D+为零的行以及近死行产生超过10^12的梯度范数。；提出stop-gradient变体：前向保持D+ + D-，反向仅通过D+传播，使其能够从零训练，并在多个指标上匹配或优于softpick。

Abstract: Softmax attention gives every token a nonzero weight, which in trained models concentrates into attention sinks and massive activations that widen the dynamic range low-precision inference must cover. Softpick removes this constraint by rectifying scores, eliminating sinks and lowering hidden-state kurtosis, but its advantage fades at scale. We reframe this failure as a normalization problem. Softpick's denominator splits into positive- and negative-shifted sums $D^+$ and $D^-$, used identically in the forward and backward pass, preventing their roles from being isolated. We separate them into a family of operators that independently choose each denominator. The failure originates at initialization: every layer contains rows where $D^+$ is exactly zero, while near-dead rows produce gradient norms above $10^{12}$ regardless of the backward denominator. Only Softpick and a stop-gradient variant, which keeps $D^+ + D^-$ forward but backpropagates through $D^+$ alone, train from scratch. At 230M parameters, the stop-gradient operator matches Softpick on quantization, has fewer dead heads, and retrieves passkeys more reliably, trailing only on peak attention-weight kurtosis.

</details>


### [137] [Logic-Logit: A Logic-Based Approach to Choice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05501)
*Shuhan Zhang,Wendi Ren,Shuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Logic-Logit 是一种基于 OR-of-ANDs 逻辑规则的可解释选择模型，先学规则筛选考虑集再做选择，用列生成与 Frank-Wolfe 优化规则提取；在合成、商业和医疗数据上验证了可解释性与准确率优势，但未披露生产部署或在线指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化、非参数化和深度网络选择模型要么可解释性不足，要么参数多、透明度低，难以在需要透明决策的场景中有效学习并解释人类选择。

Method: Logic-Logit 以 OR-of-ANDs 逻辑规则作为选择行为的基本表示：每个规则是若干条件（合取/AND）组成的可解释模式，多个规则通过析取（OR）形成考虑集。模型首先从备选项中依据这些逻辑规则筛选出考虑集，再在考虑集内通过选择模型完成最终选择，模拟“先筛选、再选择”的决策过程。为学习规则，作者将规则提取形式化为 NP-hard 优化问题，并采用列生成（column generation）逐步生成候选规则列，结合 Frank-Wolfe 算法对连续松弛目标进行优化，从而高效地搜索规则空间并更新规则权重。训练目标同时兼顾拟合人类选择数据与模型可解释性。推理时，模型使用已学得的逻辑规则评估每个备选项是否进入考虑集，再基于规则结构给出选择概率或偏好排序。摘要未披露具体参数规模、训练数据规模、训练硬件、推理硬件、延迟、吞吐或在线服务流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于逻辑规则的 Logic-Logit 可解释选择模型，用 OR-of-ANDs 规则刻画考虑集，符合人类启发式决策过程。；将列生成技术与 Frank-Wolfe 算法结合，用于高效提取逻辑规则，解决偏好建模中规则提取的 NP-hard 问题。；在合成数据、商业和医疗真实数据上验证，在可解释性和准确率上优于基线模型。

Abstract: In this study, we propose a novel rule-based interpretable choice model, Logic-Logit, designed to effectively learn and explain human choices. Choice models have been widely applied across various domains---such as commercial demand forecasting, recommendation systems, and consumer behavior analysis---typically categorized as parametric, nonparametric, or deep network-based. While recent innovations have favored neural network approaches for their computational power, these flexible models often involve large parameter sets and lack interpretability, limiting their effectiveness in contexts where transparency is essential. Previous empirical evidence shows that individuals usually use heuristic decision rules to form their consideration sets, from which they then choose. These rules are often represented as disjunctions of conjunctions (i.e., OR-of-ANDs). These rules-driven, consider-then-choose decision processes enable people to quickly screen numerous alternatives while reducing cognitive and search costs. Motivated by this insight, our approach leverages logic rules to elucidate human choices, providing a fresh perspective on preference modeling. We introduce a unique combination of column generation techniques and the Frank-Wolfe algorithm to facilitate efficient rule extraction for preference modeling---a process recognized as NP-hard. Our empirical evaluation, conducted on both synthetic datasets and real-world data from commercial and healthcare domains, demonstrates that Logic-Logit significantly outperforms baseline models in terms of interpretability and accuracy.

</details>


### [138] [LiFT: Loop Flow Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05538)
*Mohammad Mahdi Derakhshani,Pedro M. P. Curvo,Gertjan J. Burghouts,Jan-Willem van de Meent,Cees G. M. Snoek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiFT 提出循环流 Transformer，通过重复应用共享 DiT 核心并训练连续深度上的中间回归目标，使模型可在推理时无限循环提升生成质量而不增加参数。在 ImageNet 256×256 上，以约 60% 更少参数、32% 更少训练 FLOPs 和 52% 更少推理 FLOPs，实现比 DiT-XL/2 低 3.34 的 FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不增加参数量的前提下，通过循环复用共享的 Diffusion Transformer 核心来扩展测试时计算，从而提升生成质量并降低训练与推理开销。

Method: LiFT 在标准 Diffusion Transformer (DiT) 基础上引入循环机制：输入为图像潜在表示与时间步条件，核心是一个共享的 DiT 模块，被反复应用多次（循环步）。每个循环步不直接预测最终流匹配目标，而是回归一个连续深度坐标索引的中间目标——从模型初始估计到流匹配目标之间直线路径上的点。这种训练方式使模型可以外推到训练深度之外：推理时可执行任意多步循环，无需重新训练、无需早退机制或结构修改。更长的 rollout 会增加测试时计算量并提升生成质量，但模型参数量不变，因此实现推理计算扩展与参数效率的权衡。训练目标为流匹配回归，优化连续深度上的路径点；推理路径为按顺序重复调用共享 DiT 核心，逐步更新潜在表示。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "FID", "value": "比 dense DiT-XL/2 基线低 3.34 点", "setting": "ImageNet 256×256 图像生成"}, {"metric": "参数量", "value": "减少约 60%", "setting": "LiFT-L/2 对比 dense DiT-XL/2 基线"}, {"metric": "训练 FLOPs", "value": "减少 32%", "setting": "LiFT-L/2 对比 dense DiT-XL/2 基线"}, {"metric": "推理 FLOPs", "value": "减少 52%", "setting": "LiFT-L/2 对比 dense DiT-XL/2 基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Loop Flow Transformers (LiFT)，循环应用共享 DiT 核心，仅对标准架构做轻量修改。；每个循环步骤使用单一回归目标：模型初始估计到流匹配目标之间直线路径上的点。；以连续深度坐标索引目标，使训练后的模型可在不重新训练、不早退、不修改结构的情况下循环远超训练深度。；更长的推理 rollout 可提升生成质量，实现推理计算扩展而不增加参数。

Abstract: We introduce Loop Flow Transformers (LiFT), a family of looped generative models that scales computation by repeatedly applying a shared Diffusion Transformer (DiT) core, with only light changes to the standard architecture. Rather than asking every recurrent step for the final prediction, LiFT trains each step with a single regression target: a point on a straight path from the model's initial estimate to the flow-matching target. Because we index these targets by a continuous depth coordinate, a trained model can loop far beyond its training depth with no retraining, early exits, or other modifications. In our experiments, these longer rollouts improve generation, so inference computation can grow without adding parameters. On ImageNet at 256x256, LiFT-L/2 achieves an FID 3.34 points lower than our dense DiT-XL/2 baseline while using approximately 60% fewer parameters, 32% fewer training FLOPs, and 52% fewer inference FLOPs.

</details>


### [139] [VERA: Verdict-Conditioned Reliability for Adaptive LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05452)
*Qiushui Xu,Syamil Mohd Razak,Tao Yuan,Piotr Habas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VERA 提出判定条件化可靠性轴，从 LLM judge 隐藏激活中估计判断可靠性，并据此设计纠错更新、可靠性残差回放与周期性方向刷新的适配框架；在 Chatbot Arena 适配后 8B/14B judge 在四个公开基准上相对最强基线最高提升 23.01%，专有审计任务焦点召回相对提升 16.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在将 LLM judge 适配到新验证反馈时，如何在不遗忘既有判断行为的前提下准确估计其判断可靠性；现有输出级置信度过度自信且与判断正确性对齐差。

Method: VERA 以 LLM judge 的隐藏激活作为输入表征。对于每个输入样本，judge 先产生一个预测判定（verdict）；VERA 不直接使用输出级置信度，而是在同一预测判定组内学习一个可靠性轴，将隐藏激活投影到该轴上以区分正确与错误判断。基于该可靠性信号，框架设计周期性适配：1）可靠性排序的纠错更新：按估计可靠性排序，优先对低可靠/可能错误的判断进行纠正性参数更新；2）可靠性残差回放：在更新时回放与可靠性残差相关的样本以保留原行为、防止遗忘；3）周期性刷新可靠性方向：随着模型参数更新，定期重新估计可靠性轴方向，避免可靠性与新判定分布漂移。适配流程在 Chatbot Arena 数据上进行，后接四个公开 held-out 基准评估以及一个专有 temporal auditing 任务评估。论文未披露具体网络层选择、投影维度、优化器、学习率或推理延迟等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "四留出公开基准上相对最强基线的相对提升", "value": "up to 23.01%", "setting": "8B和14B参数judge，Chatbot Arena适配后，四个held-out公开基准"}, {"metric": "focal-class recall相对提升", "value": "up to 16.1%", "setting": "专有 temporal auditing 任务，相对最强自适应基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: VERA：从隐藏激活中学习判定条件化可靠性轴（Verdict-Conditioned Reliability Axis），在每个预测判定组内区分正确与错误判断，而不是使用输出级置信度。；基于 VERA 的引导式周期性适配框架：整合可靠性排序的纠错更新、可靠性残差回放，以及可靠性方向的周期性刷新，以缓解灾难性遗忘并提升可靠判断。；在 Chatbot Arena 上的适配实验显示 8B/14B judge 在四个留出公开基准上均超过最强基线，相对提升最高 23.01%；在专有 temporal auditing 任务上焦点类召回相对最强自适应基线最高提升 16.1%。

Abstract: Accurately estimating judgment reliability is a central challenge in adapting LLM judges to newly verified feedback while preserving previously learned behavior. However, existing approaches often rely on output-level confidence, which can be overconfident and poorly aligned with judgment correctness. We propose VERA, a VErdict-conditioned Reliability Axis that estimates reliability from hidden activations by distinguishing correct from incorrect judgments within each predicted-verdict group. Using VERA as a control signal, we develop a VERA-guided periodic adaptation framework that integrates reliability-ranked corrective updates, reliability-residual replay, and periodic refresh of the reliability directions. After VERA-guided adaptation on Chatbot Arena, 8B- and 14B-parameter judges outperform the strongest baseline on each of four held-out public benchmarks, with relative gains of up to 23.01%. The framework also improves focal-class recall by up to 16.1% relative to the strongest adaptive baselines on a separate proprietary temporal auditing task.

</details>


### [140] [Distributed Subliminal Learning: Replacing Model Updates with Random-Carrier Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05378)
*Dario Fenoglio,Gabriele Dominici,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DSL：参与者本地适配模型后用任务无关随机输入探测，仅传输载体输出，由协调者蒸馏成共享模型。在 LLM 组合中偏好保留 94.56% 对 LoRA 平均的 87.76%，GSM8K 增益 22.0 对 0.6，上传量减少 30.6–49.0×；联邦分类 MNIST 达 96.83%，上行通信比 FedAvg 少 8.9×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作学习中交换模型参数或适配器导致通信量随模型规模增长，且局部专精在权重空间合并时易发生干扰；能否仅通过模型在任务无关输入上的行为来共享知识。

Method: DSL 的端到端流程如下：首先，多个参与者各自在本地对同一基础模型进行适配（如微调或适配器训练），得到局部专精模型。随后，参与者用与任务无关的随机输入（carrier inputs）探测本地模型，收集模型输出作为载体输出（carrier outputs）；这些输出不包含模型参数，仅承载局部知识的行为特征。协调者汇总所有参与者的载体输出，将其作为蒸馏目标训练一个共享模型，从而将各局部专精聚合到单一权重空间中。该原语有两种实现模式：一次性基础模型组合使用载体补全（carrier completions），适用于独立适配的基础模型合并；迭代联邦学习使用载体 logits，在每个通信轮次中传输载体输出而非模型更新。训练目标为蒸馏损失，使共享模型在随机载体输入上匹配池化后的载体输出分布。推理时使用蒸馏后的共享模型进行标准前向计算，无需额外载体输入。该设计通过将通信内容从参数空间转移到行为空间，解决了通信量随模型规模增长以及权重空间合并干扰的问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "偏好保留率", "value": "94.56% vs. 87.76%", "setting": "LLM 组合，与 LoRA averaging 对比"}, {"metric": "GSM8K 增益（相对基座模型）", "value": "22.0 vs. 0.6 分", "setting": "LLM 组合，与 LoRA averaging 对比"}, {"metric": "上传量减少", "value": "30.6–49.0×", "setting": "LLM 组合"}, {"metric": "MNIST 准确率", "value": "96.83%", "setting": "联邦分类，上行通信量比 FedAvg 少 8.9×"}, {"metric": "CIFAR-10 / Tiny ImageNet", "value": "提供更低通信量的工作点", "setting": "联邦分类"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出分布式潜隐学习（DSL）原语：参与者本地适配共享模型，用任务无关输入探测并仅传输载体输出，由协调者池化并蒸馏回共享模型；支持一次性基础模型组合（载体补全）与迭代联邦学习（载体 logits）两种模式，无需传输模型更新或任务相关代理数据；在 LLM 组合中相比 LoRA 平均取得更高的偏好保留率和更大的 GSM8K 增益，同时大幅降低上传量；在联邦分类中提供低通信量的工作点，优于 FedAvg 的上行通信效率

Abstract: Collaborative learning typically exchanges model parameters: federated clients communicate updates, while independently adapted foundation models are combined by exchanging adapters or checkpoints. This makes communication scale with model size and requires local specializations to be reconciled in weight space, where interference is common. We ask whether knowledge can instead be shared through model behavior on task-unrelated inputs. We introduce Distributed Subliminal Learning (DSL), a collaborative learning primitive in which participants adapt a common model locally, probe it with task-unrelated inputs, and transmit only the resulting carrier outputs. A coordinator pools these outputs and distills them into a shared model. The primitive supports one-shot foundation-model composition through carrier completions and iterative federated learning through carrier logits, without transmitting model updates or requiring task-related proxy data. In LLM composition, compared with LoRA averaging, DSL achieves higher preference retention (94.56% vs. 87.76%) and a larger GSM8K gain over the base model (22.0 vs. 0.6 points), while reducing upload by 30.6-49.0$\times$. In federated classification, DSL reaches 96.83% on MNIST with 8.9$\times$ less uplink than FedAvg and provides lower-communication operating points on CIFAR-10 and Tiny ImageNet. These results establish random-carrier outputs as a practical communication primitive for knowledge sharing across distinct collaborative learning paradigms.

</details>


### [141] [Reflections and Fragments: Securing LLMs Against Sequential Mosaic Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05346)
*Emanuele La Malfa,Saar Cohen,Gabriele La Malfa,Mickel Liu,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Natasha Jaques,Michael J. Wooldridge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对多轮对话中的'马赛克攻击'（各轮片段单独无害但组合成有害负载）提出防御理论：证明固定近期窗口不足，形式化可携带历史安全信息的在线'看门人'状态机制，并给出零失败防御、最优性及状态/查询下界。方法上用冻结LLM训练攻击者与防御者角色专用LoRA适配器进行多轮自博弈，双方能力均增强且可泛化到未见攻击目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实对手采用'马赛克攻击'：多轮对话中每个片段单独无害，但组合起来构成有害负载；现有基于固定近期窗口的防御无法应对，且自博弈均衡本身不能保证有用性。

Method: 论文的框架分为三部分。理论层：将多轮交互建模为序列，攻击者按轮次产生片段（每个片段单独无害），这些片段在上下文累积后形成有害负载。作者首先证明不存在固定长度的有界近期窗口能普遍保证安全，因为关键安全信息可能出现在任意久远的历史轮次。为弥补这一点，形式化一个在线状态机制'看门人'(watchman)，它接收当前轮提示与既有状态，输出更新后的状态；状态被设计为携带历史上全部与安全相关的信息。在明确假设下，看门人可同时实现零失败防御与正的有用性收益，并在更强条件下最优。作者进一步给出精确看门人需要指数级状态数的下界，以及黑盒模型下精确恶意检测需要指数级查询的下界，并说明状态下界对应的构造可从标注样本中高效学习，但受限访问下的最坏情况安全认证需要更多信息。方法层：将防御问题重新表述为约束优化——在零失败防御者集合中，最大化最坏情况下的良性有用性，因为自博弈均衡本身只保证攻防零和，不保证模型对良性请求仍有用。实现层（实证）：以冻结LLM为基础模型，分别训练攻击者与防御者的角色专用LoRA适配器，通过多轮自博弈交替迭代：攻击者学习在多个轮次中分散并组合有害意图以规避防御，防御者学习在状态中保留早前轮次的安全线索以识别整体有害意图。评估时观察攻击者诱导有害回答的成功率提升、防御者对攻击的鲁棒性提升，并测试对未见攻击目标的泛化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 建立马赛克防御理论：证明不存在固定有界近期窗口在一般情况下足够，因为安全相关信息可能出现在任意久远的交互历史中；形式化'看门人'(watchman)在线状态机制，将安全相关信息向前携带，并在明确假设下实现零失败防御与正的有用性收益；在更强条件下证明看门人机制在零失败防御者中最优；给出精确看门人的指数级状态下界与黑盒模型精确恶意检测的指数级查询下界，并区分'可学习'与'可认证最坏情况安全'的差异；提出约束式公式：在零失败防御者中最大化最坏情况良性有用性，指出自博弈均衡不足以认证有用性；实证：在冻结LLM上通过多轮自博弈训练角色专用攻击者与防御者LoRA适配器，攻防双方均增强且可迁移到未见攻击目标

Abstract: Self-play red-teaming improves language-model safety by pitting attacker and defender roles against each other in a zero-sum game. However, real adversaries increasingly use mosaic attacks: multi-turn sequences whose individual fragments are innocuous in isolation yet assemble into a harmful payload. We develop a theory of mosaic defense that characterizes what is required to prevent such attacks without sacrificing helpfulness. We first show that no fixed bounded window of recent prompts is sufficient in general: safety-relevant information may occur arbitrarily far back in the interaction. We formalize a watchman, an online state mechanism that carries this information forward, and show that under explicit assumptions it enables zero-failure defense with positive benign helpfulness. Under stronger conditions, it is also optimal among zero-failure defenders. An exact watchman may nevertheless require exponentially many states, while exact maliciousness detection can require exponentially many queries in an unstructured black-box model. These state and query lower bounds do not by themselves imply hard learning: the construction underlying the state lower bound is efficiently learnable from labeled examples, whereas certifying worst-case safety can require substantially more information under restricted access. We also show that self-play equilibrium alone does not certify usefulness, motivating a constrained formulation that maximizes worst-case benign helpfulness among zero-failure defenders. Empirically, training role-specific attacker and defender LoRA adapters over frozen LLMs via multi-turn self-play strengthens both roles: attackers become more effective at eliciting harmful responses, while defenders become more robust to attack, with improvements also observed on unseen attack objectives.

</details>


### [142] [ASCENT: Online Test-Time Training of Long-Horizon Agents via Self-Distillation of Verified Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05303)
*Haodong Lu,Dong Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ASCENT提出在线测试时训练框架，利用冻结的初始LLM作为教师，将已验证的智能体轨迹通过自蒸馏更新持久LoRA权重，使LLM智能体在长程任务流中持续提升成功率和交互效率，无需外部记忆检索或独立训练阶段，在ALFWorld、WebShop、AppWorld上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署中的LLM智能体面临长程任务流，每个任务仅在一次执行后获得终止验证信号，如何利用这些单次轨迹在线更新模型权重，避免直接模仿或强化生成token导致策略不稳定。

Method: ASCENT框架在部署阶段对LLM智能体进行在线测试时训练。输入为智能体执行每个任务产生的轨迹（包括推理步骤、动作及环境反馈）以及终止时的验证结果。核心模块包括：（1）冻结的初始LLM副本作为稳定教师，接收已验证轨迹作为特权信息，预测该轨迹上的下一token分布；（2）持久化的LoRA快速权重作为可训练学生，通过蒸馏教师带有后见之明的分布来更新自身参数；（3）无效动作回合过滤模块，在蒸馏前移除失败或无效的交互片段，生成增强的特权经验。数据流：智能体按顺序执行任务流，每个任务只执行一次，执行完成后得到验证信号；将验证过的轨迹（经无效动作过滤）送入冻结教师生成目标分布；然后用KL散度等蒸馏损失更新LoRA权重，更新后的策略用于后续任务。整个过程中无需外部参考解、更强教师或独立的记忆检索模块。推理时仅使用更新后的LoRA权重增强的LLM进行决策。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "task success", "value": "improves as experience accumulates", "setting": "ALFWorld, WebShop, AppWorld at varied model scales"}, {"metric": "interaction efficiency", "value": "improves as experience accumulates", "setting": "ALFWorld, WebShop, AppWorld at varied model scales"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出在线智能体测试时训练（OaTTT）设定：在部署过程中对自身执行轨迹进行单次通过、顺序更新的权重训练。；ASCENT框架：使用冻结的初始LLM作为稳定教师，接收已验证轨迹作为特权信息预测下一token分布，并将其蒸馏到持久LoRA快速权重中，无需外部参考解或更强教师。；通过移除无效动作回合来增强特权经验，提高蒸馏效率与执行效率。；刻画了在线自蒸馏的总体目标与稀疏结果选择的局限性。
