Abstract: A large language model (LLM) agent solves long-horizon tasks through many reasoning-action turns, with one verification signal at termination. Deployed agents face streams of related tasks, making their trajectories a natural resource for improvement. In-context adaptation agents store reflections, memories, or skills as text, so reuse depends on retrieving the right experience and on a frozen policy executing it. We study Online Agentic Test-Time Training (OaTTT), which trains the LLM's weights on its own execution trajectories during deployment. The agent executes each task once, in one pass over the stream, and the executed trajectory with its verification result is the only learning signal for weight updates that persist across tasks. Directly imitating or reinforcing the generated tokens of this single attempt destabilizes the policy. We introduce ASCENT (Agentic Self-distillation for Cross-task EvolutioN at Test-time), which instead self-distills verified experience. A stable version of the LLM, its frozen initial copy, receives the verified trajectory as privileged information and predicts next-token distributions along it with this hindsight. Distilling them into persistent LoRA fast weights updates the agent for later tasks, without an external reference solution or stronger teacher. By further removing invalid-action turns, ASCENT distills enhanced privileged experience for more efficient execution. We characterize its population target and the limits of sparse outcome selection. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and AppWorld at varied model scales, ASCENT improves task success and interaction efficiency as experience accumulates, outperforms online adaptation methods, and transfers to held-out scenes, showing that an agent can consolidate verified experience into its weights without a separate training phase or memory retrieval. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [143] [Understanding the Weight Averaging Mechanism in LLM Training for Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05329)
*Hanzhang Wang,Tianqi Shen,Zonglin Liu,Junze He,Difan Zou,Ziye Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文理论分析了LLM预训练中权重平均改善PTQ的机制，提出连续平均核族和统一框架，为不同量化条件提供策略指导，实验验证了预测行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解权重平均在LLM训练中改善PTQ性能的机制，从而提供统一的理论指导，使该技术稳定可复现。

Method: 论文并非提出新的LLM架构，而是对LLM预训练中的权重平均进行理论分析。输入为训练过程中的一系列权重检查点，通过平均核（如指数移动平均、均匀平均等）生成平均权重，再用于PTQ。理论框架将权重平均建模为保留训练进度与抗扰动鲁棒性之间的权衡，推导出连续平均核族以统一现有策略并刻画帕累托前沿。进一步分析量化粒度对扰动敏感性的影响，预测不同PTQ设置下的最优平均策略。实验在LLM上进行，验证理论预测并比较所提平均策略与传统方法。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将权重平均形式化为保留训练进度与提升扰动鲁棒性之间的权衡。；推导出一族连续平均核，统一传统策略并达到帕累托前沿。；建立PTQ下权重平均的理论框架，揭示量化粒度与扰动敏感性的关系。；提出并验证在所提理论指导下的平均策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically pretrained in high precision but increasingly deployed with low-precision post-training quantization (PTQ). Recent studies have shown that using weight averaging during pretraining can improve PTQ performance compared with learning-rate decay, suggesting that it might provide a simple way to improve the pretraining-to-quantization transition. But the mechanism behind weight averaging remains insufficiently explained. This leads to inconsistent and fragile performance gains, thereby preventing practitioners from applying such a technique confidently. As a response, we formulate weight averaging as a trade-off between retaining training progress and improving robustness under perturbation. We further derive a continuous family of averaging kernels that unifies conventional strategies and achieves the Pareto frontier between the two competing goals. Critically, a theoretical framework for performing weight averaging under PTQ is developed. It can be shown that coarser quantization is more susceptible to perturbations, whereas finer quantization could be less affected. Thus, our results could provide unified theoretical guidance for performing weight averaging under different PTQ conditions. Experiments validate both the predicted behavior and the proposed averaging strategy. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [144] [Ranking Bandits for Carousel Interfaces with Observable Browsing Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05220)
*Takuma Yasuda,Atsuyoshi Nakamura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将轮播推荐中的排序问题建模为可观察浏览深度的 bandit 问题，提出 UCB、TS 和 DMED 三种算法，并给出理论遗憾界，在模拟和真实日志参数化实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可观察浏览深度的轮播界面中，如何利用浏览深度信息区分已展示未点击和未展示物品，从而更高效地进行排序 bandit 学习。

Method: 论文考虑一个排序 bandit 框架：学习器在每个回合展示包含 L 个物品的列表，用户浏览该列表直到最大深度 D，学习器观察到 D，并且仅对位置 1 到 D 的物品获得点击反馈（每个物品被点击概率由其吸引力参数决定）。目标是最大化期望点击数。物品吸引力向量和浏览深度分布均未知。所提三种算法（UCB、TS、DMED）维护每个物品的统计量，但仅根据观察到的曝光（即位置 ≤ D）更新。UCB 算法基于物品的置信上界选择列表顶部物品；TS 从后验分布中采样；DMED 基于信息论准则。理论上给出了 UCB 的实例相关对数遗憾上界和 DMED 的渐近上界，后者在 α → 0 时与下界匹配。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最终平均遗憾", "value": "OD-TS 与 PBM-TS 相近，所提方法低于 PBM-UCB", "setting": "合成浅层和深层浏览环境模拟，以及基于 RecGaze 交互日志参数化的实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 首次形式化可观察浏览深度的排序 bandit 问题，观察用户最大浏览深度，仅对深度内位置获得点击反馈。；提出基于 UCB、Thompson Sampling 和 DMED 的三种算法，仅从观察到的曝光更新物品统计量。；为 UCB 算法推导实例相关的对数遗憾上界，为 DMED 算法推导渐近上界，并在参数 α → 0 时与下界吻合，证明渐近最优性。

Abstract: Carousel interfaces allow a recommender system to directly observe how far a user has browsed. This signal distinguishes displayed but unclicked items from items that were never displayed, whereas conventional ranking-bandit models, including cascade and position-based models, generally treat examination as latent. We formulate a ranking-bandit problem in which a learner presents a list of $L$ items, observes the user's maximum browsing depth, and receives click feedback only for positions up to that depth. The objective is to maximize the expected number of clicks under an unknown item-attractiveness vector and a browsing-depth distribution. We propose three algorithms based on UCB, Thompson Sampling, and DMED, all of which update item statistics only from observed exposures. We derive an instance-dependent logarithmic upper bound for our UCB-based algorithm and an asymptotic upper bound for our DMED-based algorithm that coincides with the lower bound as its parameter $\alpha\downarrow 0$, establishing asymptotic optimality in this limit. Simulations in synthetic shallow- and deep-browsing environments, together with experiments parameterized from RecGaze interaction logs, show that OD-TS attains final mean regret similar to PBM-TS, while the proposed methods achieve lower final mean regret than PBM-UCB.

</details>


### [145] [Outcome-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05070)
*ZheXu Wang,Mao-Lin Luo,Yankun Hong,Zi-Hao Zhou,Bo Ye,Jian Zhao,Xialiang Tong,Min-Ling Zhang,Tong Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OG-OPSD 针对 on-policy self-distillation 训练不稳定问题，从 RLVR 优势角度分析并利用结果正确性和教师熵动态调整散度目标与蒸馏位置，在多个 Qwen3 规模及多模态、数学、OOD 任务上一致优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: vanilla On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) 对错误轨迹惩罚不足且奖励过度，因为其固定散度目标不考虑最终结果正确性；同时教师监督可靠性受轨迹结果和 rollout 中累计平均教师熵的影响，导致训练噪声和不稳定。

Method: OG-OPSD 建立在 on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) 框架之上。输入为策略模型在 on-policy rollout 中生成的轨迹，教师模型提供 token-level 监督。vanilla OPSD 使用固定的散度目标，与轨迹最终是否正确无关。论文从 RLVR 的 advantage 公式出发分析该目标，发现对错误轨迹惩罚不足且奖励过度。OG-OPSD 因此引入两个基于结果的动态调整：一是根据二元 outcome reward（轨迹正确/错误）调整散度目标，对正确轨迹和错误轨迹给予不同的奖励/惩罚强度；二是根据 rollout 中累计平均教师熵反映教师监督可靠性，动态决定蒸馏位置。训练目标仍为在策略轨迹上的自蒸馏损失，但散度项和蒸馏位置均随结果和熵信号自适应变化。推理阶段使用训练后的策略模型直接生成答案，不引入额外教师推理开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "performance improvement over vanilla OPSD and multiple strong baselines", "value": "consistent improvement", "setting": "mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, out-of-distribution tasks; Qwen3 1.7B/4B/8B and Qwen3-VL-2B"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从 RLVR advantage 角度分析 OPSD 目标，指出固定散度目标忽略 outcome correctness 导致错误轨迹惩罚不足。；提出根据二元结果奖励动态调整散度目标，对正确/错误轨迹施加不同的惩罚或奖励。；提出根据 rollout 中累计平均教师熵动态调整蒸馏位置（distillation position），以利用更可靠的教师监督。；在数学推理、多模态推理和分布外任务上，跨 Qwen3 1.7B/4B/8B 及 Qwen3-VL-2B 验证方法有效性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides denser token-level supervision and better computational efficiency than Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). However, this denser supervision may introduce substantial noise and training instability. Existing improvements often rely on high-variance per-token statistics and introduce extra hyperparameters and trade-offs. Based on the advantage formulation in RLVR, we analyze the OPSD objective from the same perspective, incorporating outcome correctness signals. We find that vanilla OPSD imposes insufficient penalties and excessive rewards on incorrect trajectories because it applies a fixed divergence objective regardless of outcome correctness. Furthermore, the reliability of teacher supervision is associated with both trajectory outcome and the cumulative average teacher entropy along the rollout. Based on these observations, we propose Outcome-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation (OG-OPSD), which dynamically adapts both the divergence objective and distillation position according to binary outcome rewards and the cumulative average teacher entropy. Extensive experiments show that OG-OPSD consistently improves the performance of vanilla OPSD and multiple strong baselines in mathematical reasoning, multimodal reasoning, and out-of-distribution tasks across Qwen3 models at 1.7B, 4B, and 8B scales, as well as Qwen3-VL-2B.

</details>


### [146] [E$^2$-OPSD: Taming Entropy Overshoot in On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05048)
*Yifei Liu,Minghao Fang,Xinyu Gu,Chengkai Yao,Mengdi Liu,Tengfei Ma,Jiangbin Zheng,Chang Yu,Zhangyang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对 on-policy self-distillation 中的熵过冲问题，提出 E^2-OPSD，通过 exemplar-guided teaching 和 entropy-aware distillation 实现更稳定的 token 级监督，在数学推理和域外评测上取得最高 4.3/4.9/5.5 分的提升，且不增加额外网络或前向计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy self-distillation 中出现的熵过冲（entropy overshoot）问题：学生 token 熵在训练中超过教师并持续偏高，导致前向 KL 不断扩散学生预测分布而没有有效拉回。

Method: E^2-OPSD 建立在 on-policy self-distillation 框架上：同一网络在训练中同时充当教师和学生。学生仅接收问题作为输入，教师额外接收参考解（reference solution）。原 OPSD 使用前向 KL 在教师和学生的 token 分布之间进行密集 token 级蒸馏。论文识别出熵过冲现象：学生 token 熵在训练中超过教师并持续偏高，归因于教师在其答案导向推理路径上过于自信，但这种自信无法迁移到学生生成的前缀，导致监督过度依赖答案特定线索而非可复用推理模式；同时前向 KL 只扩散学生预测而不拉回。E^2-OPSD 引入两项改动。第一，exemplar-guided teaching：将当前答案替换为检索到的已解决邻近问题作为教师条件，从而在不泄露目标答案的情况下提供可迁移的推理引导，并更好地匹配学生可达状态。第二，entropy-aware distillation：计算学生与教师之间的熵差，据此决定每个 token 校正的方向和强度。训练目标仍基于同一网络的师生角色，但调整了教师输入条件和 token 级校正信号。整个方法不需要额外前向传播或额外网络，推理路径与基础模型一致。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean@16", "value": "最多提升 4.3 分", "setting": "数学推理评测，与 OPSD 对比"}, {"metric": "mean@16", "value": "最多提升 4.9 分", "setting": "域外评测，与对应 base model 对比"}, {"metric": "pass@8", "value": "最多提升 5.5 分", "setting": "域外评测，与对应 base model 对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别并刻画 on-policy self-distillation 的熵过冲失败模式，将其归因于教师参考方案条件化导致的答案特定线索以及前向 KL 的分布扩散。；提出 exemplar-guided teaching：用检索到的已解决邻近问题替换当前答案作为教师条件，提供可迁移的推理指导且不泄露目标答案，更好匹配学生可达状态。；提出 entropy-aware distillation：利用学生-教师熵差决定每个 token 校正的方向和强度。；方法无需额外前向传播或额外网络，保持简单。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) provides dense token-level supervision without a second model: one network acts as teacher with the reference solution and as student with only the problem. We identify a specific failure mode of this recipe. During training, student token entropy rises past the teacher's and remains elevated, a pattern we call entropy overshoot. We trace it to both sides of distillation. The reference-conditioned teacher is confident along its answer-directed reasoning path, but this confidence transfers poorly to student-generated prefixes, making its supervision overly tied to answer-specific cues rather than reusable reasoning patterns; meanwhile, the forward KL used by OPSD continually diffuses the student's predictive distribution without pulling it back. We introduce E$^2$-OPSD to address both causes. Exemplar-guided teaching replaces the current answer with a retrieved solved neighboring problem, providing transferable reasoning guidance without revealing the destination and better matching student-reachable states. Entropy-aware distillation uses the student-teacher entropy gap to determine the direction and strength of each token's correction. E$^2$-OPSD improves math reasoning by up to 4.3 points in mean@16 over OPSD, while out-of-domain evaluations show gains over the corresponding base models of up to 4.9 points in mean@16 and 5.5 points in pass@8. Despite these gains, E$^2$-OPSD remains simple, requiring no additional forward passes or networks.

</details>


### [147] [Billion-Scale Thumbnail Optimization for Uncurated Short-Form Videos via Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04931)
*Ying Han,Ling Liu,Fabio Soldo,Vu Nguyen,Danio Wang,Liz Kidd,Yongle Cao,Theodore Rose,Su-Lin Wu,Romer Rosales*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Google 在大型短视频平台部署了十亿级(O(B))规模的实时缩略图优化系统，针对无人工封面的未精选视频，用多阶段候选生成、深度视觉质量先验初始化多臂老虎机，并在低延迟服务中动态选择缩略图，全球部署显著提升用户发现与互动指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量短视频没有创作者人工挑选的封面，传统静态默认帧效果差；需要在十亿级视频规模上自动选择最优缩略图，并控制探索成本、保证低延迟在线服务。

Method: 系统面向未精选短视频的缩略图优化，输入是视频候选帧(缩略图候选)及相关图像特征，输出是在服务时动态选出的最优缩略图。整体包含多阶段候选生成流水线与低延迟在线服务基础设施。候选生成阶段从视频中产生多个可用缩略图候选；随后在线多臂老虎机(MA B)框架负责在候选之间进行探索与利用，动态选择展示给用户的缩略图。为了降低探索成本，系统使用一个深度视觉质量模型生成图像特定的先验，并用这些先验初始化多臂老虎机框架，从而在冷启动或候选较多时快速聚焦高质量图像。服务时，在线系统实时接收请求并基于当前模型状态选择缩略图，同时收集用户互动反馈用于持续更新。论文未披露具体的候选生成模型结构、视觉质量模型细节、训练目标、参数规模、训练数据、硬件与延迟数值。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "核心用户发现与互动指标", "value": "统计显著提升", "setting": "全球部署，十亿级(O(B))规模未精选短视频缩略图优化"}]}

Conclusion: 首次公开报道在十亿级(O(B))未精选短视频场景中成功部署在线多臂老虎机框架用于缩略图发现。；多阶段候选生成流水线配合低延迟服务基础设施。；用深度视觉质量模型导出图像特定先验来初始化探索框架，以降低探索成本。；在服务时动态选择并展示最优缩略图，实现全自动端到端替换静态默认帧。

Abstract: This paper introduces a real-time thumbnail optimization system deployed at a global $O(B)$ scale on a major short-form video platform. Unlike traditional long-form content, where custom thumbnails are heavily curated by creators, a considerable fraction of short-form videos are published without human-selected artwork. To address this uncurated corpus, we present a fully automated, end-to-end framework that replaces static default frames with dynamic, data-driven selections across billions of videos. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first published work demonstrating an online Multi-Armed Bandit framework successfully deployed at an $O(B)$ scale for uncurated short-form video discovery. Our solution pairs a multi-stage candidate generation pipeline with a low-latency serving infrastructure. By initializing the exploration framework with image-specific priors derived from a deep visual quality model, the system minimizes exploration costs and dynamically serves optimal thumbnails at serving time. Global deployment demonstrates statistically significant improvements in core user discovery and engagement metrics.

</details>


### [148] [Why Subliminal Learning Needs So Much Data: A Noisy Inverse View through Steering Vector Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04907)
*Luoyu Chen,Xiaoyu Ding,Weiqi Wang,Chenhan Zhang,Zhiyi Tian,Jianhuan Huang,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM隐性学习（subliminal learning）的数据需求，通过steering vector recovery将行为特征迁移建模为噪声线性逆问题，揭示软监督（KL）能快速恢复教师特征而硬监督（NLL）需大量数据来抑制Fisher逆放大标签噪声，并在Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma-2-9B上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Subliminal learning（隐性学习）通常需要数万个载体样本才能成功，本文探究这一数据需求背后的原因，并对比token级硬监督与全分布软监督在恢复教师残差流引导向量Δ_T时的差异。

Method: 论文以subliminal steering为测试台：教师模型包含一个已知的残差流向量Δ_T表示某种行为特征。学生模型在语义无关的载体数据上训练，同时接收教师模型的输出。比较两种监督方式：token级硬标签（NLL）和全分布软标签（KL）。在相同载体前缀下，初始时两种目标的梯度与Δ_T几乎共线但都不对齐。迭代优化过程中，软监督在几百个载体样本下几乎精确恢复Δ_T，而硬监督即使数万样本也远低于此。作者将过程建模为噪声线性逆问题：局部载体任务通过Fisher矩阵F将特征映射为FΔ_T，梯度下降逐步逼近F^{-1}，软目标恢复低曲率方向，硬标签则在相同方向放大采样噪声，导致最优逆深度随独立载体数量增长。实验在Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma-2-9B上进行，验证了四个预测。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出subliminal steering测试台，将教师行为特征表示为已知残差流向量Δ_T，使迁移可直接测量为参数恢复问题。；将引导向量蒸馏建模为噪声线性逆问题：载体任务通过Fisher矩阵F将特征映射为FΔ_T，梯度下降逐步充当F的逆，低曲率方向被恢复而硬标签还放大采样噪声。；预测并验证了软监督在几百个载体样本下几乎精确恢复Δ_T，而硬监督即使数万样本也远低于此，揭示了最优逆深度随独立载体数量增长。；在Qwen2.5-7B和Gemma-2-9B上验证四个预测：初始梯度的Fisher扭曲、恢复从陡峭到平坦方向的顺序、最优深度随数据规模偏移、固定提示池重采样完成项与新增提示同样有效。

Abstract: Subliminal learning lets a student inherit a teacher's behavioral trait from semantically unrelated data, yet published demonstrations typically require tens of thousands of carrier examples. We ask where this data requirement comes from. Our testbed is subliminal steering: the teacher trait is a known residual-stream vector $\Delta_T$, so transfer can be measured directly as parameter recovery. On identical carrier prefixes, we compare token-level (hard) NLL supervision with full-distribution (soft) KL supervision. At initialization the two objectives give nearly collinear gradients, and both align poorly with $\Delta_T$. Under iterative optimization, however, they diverge: soft supervision recovers $\Delta_T$ almost exactly from a few hundred carriers, while hard supervision stays well below it even with tens of thousands. We explain this gap by casting steering-vector distillation as a noisy linear inverse problem. Locally, the carrier task maps the trait through its Fisher matrix $F$, so gradients point toward $F\Delta_T$ rather than $\Delta_T$. Gradient descent then acts as a progressively less-damped inverse of $F$. With soft targets, this inverse restores low-curvature directions. With hard labels, it also amplifies the sampling noise in those same directions. The result is an optimal inversion depth that grows with the number of independent carriers. Experiments on Qwen2.5-7B and Gemma-2-9B confirm four predictions: the Fisher distortion of the initial gradient, recovery ordered from steep to flat directions, an optimal depth that shifts with data scale, and the finding that resampling completions from a fixed prompt pool works as well as adding new prompts. In this setting, large carrier datasets are needed less to reveal the trait than to suppress label noise amplified by Fisher inversion. Code is available at \url{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [149] [ResOPD: Tail Residualization for Sparse On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04882)
*Penghui Yang,Long Xing,Xuanlang Dai,Ziyu Liu,Kai Chen,Yuhang Zang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ResOPD 提出尾部残差化方法，在稀疏 teacher 接口下实现无偏的全词表 reverse KL 梯度估计，通过聚合未观测词表为粗粒度 tail 事件并仅采样 tail 内残差，在不增加 teacher 查询的前提下降低梯度方差、稳定在线训练并提升下游性能，是即插即用的 on-policy distillation 方差缩减原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 on-policy distillation 中，学生模型基于自身生成的轨迹训练，但向长推理轨迹传输稠密 teacher 分布带来高昂通信与内存开销，因而实际系统改用稀疏 teacher 接口（采样 token 分数或 Top-k 分布）。稀疏设定下存在两难：采样 token 估计量无偏但梯度方差极大；直接优化 Top-k 目标则引入系统偏差。核心问题是在相同稀疏 payload 下改进无偏性与方差之间的权衡。

Method: ResOPD 面向 on-policy distillation 的稀疏 teacher 接口场景。输入为学生模型在自身生成轨迹上的逐 token 分布，teacher 仅提供稀疏信号：采样 token 的分数或一个小型 Top-k 分布。方法不增加 teacher 查询或前向传播。核心处理流程为：1) 学生采样轨迹并获取稀疏 teacher 信号；2) 将词表中未被观测的 token 集合聚合为一个可观测的粗粒度 tail 事件；3) 对该 tail 事件计算精确的聚合梯度，从而利用未观测词表的整体信息；4) 仅对 tail 内部的细粒度残差进行采样。通过这种“尾部残差化”分解，在相同的稀疏 payload 下重构出无偏的全词表 reverse KL 梯度估计，并显著降低梯度方差。训练目标为最小化学生与 teacher 分布之间的 reverse KL，整体可作为原语嵌入现有 OPD 训练循环；推理路径即常规学生模型前向生成，无额外推理开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "gradient variance", "value": "substantially reduced", "setting": "evaluated settings with on-policy sampling and same sparse payload"}, {"metric": "online training dynamics stability", "value": "stabilized", "setting": "evaluated settings"}, {"metric": "downstream performance", "value": "improved", "setting": "evaluated settings"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将未观测词表聚合为一个可观测的粗粒度 tail 事件，并计算其精确聚合梯度，避免对全部未观测 token 逐一采样；仅对 tail 内部的细粒度残差进行采样，在不增加额外 teacher 查询或前向传播的前提下获得方差缩减；提供在 on-policy 采样下无偏的全词表 reverse KL 梯度估计，作为即插即用的方差缩减原语

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model on self-generated trajectories, but transmitting dense teacher distributions across long reasoning traces creates prohibitive communication and memory bottlenecks. Practical systems therefore rely on sparse teacher interfaces, typically transmitting either the sampled-token score or a small Top-$k$ distribution. However, this sparse setting faces a fundamental dilemma: sampled-token estimators are unbiased but suffer from severe gradient variance, whereas directly optimizing Top-$k$ objectives introduces systematic bias. To improve this trade-off, we propose ResOPD (On-Policy Distillation with Tail Residualization), which provides unbiased full-vocabulary reverse KL gradient estimation under on-policy sampling, with substantial variance reduction under the same sparse payload in the evaluated settings. ResOPD aggregates the unobserved vocabulary into an observable coarse tail event, computes its exact aggregate gradient, and samples only the fine-grained within-tail residual, which requires no additional teacher queries or forward passes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ResOPD substantially reduces gradient variance, stabilizes online training dynamics, and improves downstream performance across the evaluated settings. These results establish ResOPD as an efficient, plug-and-play variance reduction primitive for sparse on-policy distillation.

</details>


### [150] [Greedy Local Learning for Language Model Pretraining: Gaps and Objective Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04867)
*Jihwan Moon,Sheir A. Zaheer,Jinmyoung Lee,Gunhee Kim,Chan Y. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文在125M/400M参数、Chinchilla最优token预算下实证分析了贪心块级局部学习用于语言模型预训练的损失差距，发现差距随块数增加而增大但在更大模型上缩小；用Transformer辅助网络可恢复22-41%的差距，MTP目标限制在第一块最佳，部署式执行可降低最多2.2倍激活内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贪心块级局部学习在自回归语言模型预训练中相对于端到端反向传播的损失差距如何随规模变化，以及哪些辅助网络/目标设计能缩小该差距。

Method: 该方法将网络划分为K个梯度隔离的块，块间没有反向传播。每个块在其局部辅助损失下独立训练并独立步进优化器，块间通信仅为前向通信。辅助设计分解为两个维度：网络架构和训练目标。网络侧，MLP辅助可被基于Transformer的辅助替代，以恢复22-41%的损失差距。目标侧，多token预测（MTP）辅助目标在第一块边界有帮助，但在更深边界有害；将MTP限制在第一块可得到最佳K=4配置（400M参数下+0.062 vs +0.075 nats）。训练时按token预算匹配，在125M和400M参数、Chinchilla最优预算下用K=1、2、4进行评估。部署式逐块执行时，各块独立运行，激活内存最多降低2.2倍。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对于端到端训练的差距", "value": "从K=2到K=4差距增大超过一倍，但在K=4时从125M到400M缩小", "setting": "125M和400M参数，Chinchilla最优token预算，K∈{1,2,4}"}, {"metric": "损失差距恢复比例", "value": "22-41%", "setting": "用Transformer辅助网络替代MLP辅助网络"}, {"metric": "nats损失", "value": "K=4时第一块限制MTP：+0.062 vs. +0.075 nats", "setting": "400M参数，Chinchilla最优预算，K=4"}, {"metric": "激活内存", "value": "最多降低2.2×", "setting": "部署式逐块执行"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在125M和400M参数、Chinchilla最优token预算、K∈{1,2,4}块设置下，对局部学习预训练损失差距进行token预算匹配的实证研究；将辅助设计分解为网络架构与训练目标两个维度；用基于Transformer的辅助网络替代MLP辅助网络，恢复22-41%的损失差距；发现多token预测（MTP）辅助目标在第一块边界有益，在更深边界有害，限制在第一块得到最佳K=4配置；部署式逐块执行可将激活内存降低最多2.2倍

Abstract: Greedy block-wise local learning splits a network into gradient-isolated blocks trained by local auxiliary losses, deleting the backward pass between blocks: inter-stage communication becomes forward-only and every block can step its optimizer independently, properties directly relevant to decentralized model-parallel training. Local learning is competitive with end-to-end backpropagation on image classification, and on small Transformers it is known to trade a worse best loss for parallel speedup. How this loss gap behaves in autoregressive language model (LM) pretraining at larger scale, and which auxiliary designs reduce it, has not been measured. We present a token-budget-matched empirical study at 125M and 400M parameters with $K \in \{1,2,4\}$ blocks at Chinchilla-optimal budgets, factorizing the auxiliary design into network architecture and training objective. We observe: (i) the gap to end-to-end training more than doubles from $K=2$ to $K=4$, but at $K=4$ shrinks from 125M to 400M; (ii) replacing an MLP auxiliary with a Transformer-based one is a strong network-side intervention, recovering 22-41% of the gap; (iii) a multi-token-prediction (MTP) auxiliary objective helps at the first block boundary, whereas adding it at deeper boundaries hurts, and restricting it to the first block yields the best $K=4$ configuration ($+0.062$ vs. $+0.075$ nats at 400M); and (iv) deployment-style per-block execution reduces activation memory by up to $2.2\times$. We frame these results as an empirically grounded method direction rather than a finalized method: local objectives should apply future-predictive pressure selectively across boundaries while resisting shortcuts that bypass predictive content.

</details>


### [151] [DiffGate: Difficulty-Gated Teacher Guidance for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04596)
*Karn Tiwari,Varnith Chordia,Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffGate proposes an outcome-gated on-policy distillation objective that applies bounded teacher guidance only to failed trajectories, scaled by group difficulty, and combines it with GRPO. It improves pass@8 across code and math for Qwen3-0.6B and 1.7B students, addressing sparse rewards and coarse credit assignment in RLVR.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing on-policy distillation objectives are token-local and outcome-agnostic, while RLVR methods like GRPO suffer from sparse rewards and coarse credit assignment; there is a need to combine dense teacher guidance with outcome-level supervision without letting noisy teacher signals dominate optimization.

Method: DiffGate operates in the LLM post-training setting with a student model, a teacher model, and a verifier providing outcome-level rewards. For each training prompt, the student generates a group of trajectories. The verifier evaluates each trajectory for correctness. The training objective combines Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with selective teacher guidance. Teacher supervision is applied only to trajectories that fail according to the verifier, determined by the outcome signal. For those failed trajectories, the teacher provides dense token-level update directions. The magnitude of teacher guidance is scaled by the difficulty of the group: groups with lower success rates receive stronger guidance. Additionally, a smooth bounding function limits extreme teacher-student discrepancies so they do not dominate the optimization. The verifier thus gates which trajectories receive teacher guidance, while the teacher supplies dense token-level adjustment within those trajectories. This yields an outcome-gated objective that integrates sparse but accurate outcome rewards with dense but weakly aligned local teacher signals. Inference follows the standard student model generation path; no changes to inference architecture are described.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "code avg@8", "value": "+1.7 points vs matched GRPO", "setting": "Qwen3-0.6B student"}, {"metric": "code avg@8", "value": "+1.8 points vs matched GRPO", "setting": "Qwen3-1.7B student"}, {"metric": "code pass@8", "value": "+1.6 points vs matched GRPO", "setting": "Qwen3-0.6B student"}, {"metric": "code pass@8", "value": "+5.7 points vs matched GRPO", "setting": "Qwen3-1.7B student"}, {"metric": "math avg@8", "value": "within 0.5 points of GRPO", "setting": "Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B students"}, {"metric": "math pass@8", "value": "+1.1 points vs GRPO", "setting": "Qwen3-0.6B student"}, {"metric": "math pass@8", "value": "+3.9 points vs GRPO", "setting": "Qwen3-1.7B student"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Outcome-gated teacher guidance: teacher supervision is applied only to failed trajectories, with the verifier deciding which trajectories receive teacher guidance.；Difficulty scaling: the strength of teacher supervision is scaled by group difficulty.；Smooth bounding of teacher-student discrepancies to prevent extreme discrepancies from dominating optimization.；Combining GRPO with selective bounded teacher guidance to alleviate sparse rewards and coarse credit assignment.

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as a widely used paradigm for post-training large language models, reducing the train--test mismatch of conventional distillation by supervising the student on its own generated trajectories. However, existing OPD objectives remain largely token-local and outcome-agnostic, optimizing teacher--student agreement at each prefix despite reasoning quality being determined at the trajectory level. Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), provides complementary outcome-level supervision but suffers from sparse rewards and coarse credit assignment. We show that OPD and RLVR exhibit complementary blind spots: teacher signals provide dense local guidance but are weakly aligned with rollout correctness, whereas group-relative rewards capture task success but provide coarse token-level credit and vanish on all-failure groups. We introduce DiffGate, an outcome-gated objective that combines GRPO with selective, bounded teacher guidance. Teacher supervision is applied only to failed trajectories, scaled by group difficulty, and smoothly bounded to prevent extreme teacher--student discrepancies from dominating optimization. The verifier therefore determines \emph{which trajectories} receive teacher guidance, while the teacher provides dense token-level update directions within those trajectories. Across Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B students, DiffGate improves code avg@8 over matched GRPO by $+1.7$ and $+1.8$ points and pass@8 by $+1.6$ and $+5.7$ points, respectively. On mathematics, avg@8 remains within $0.5$ points of GRPO while pass@8 improves by $+1.1$ and $+3.9$ points. Overall, DiffGate improves pass@8 across all four model--domain settings, demonstrating improved solution coverage under our evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [152] [PhaseGate: Phase-Aware CPU Retrieval Scheduling for On-Device LLMs on Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04537)
*Seoyoon Yum,Sehoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PHASEGATE 提出以 LLM 阶段（预填充/解码）作为 CPU 检索准入信号，在设备端统一内存系统上分别为两阶段标定检索并发上限。实验显示在饱和检索负载下，四路并发检索会使 p95 解码延迟升高 60-61%，而预填充仅升 5.7-6.9%；PHASEGATE 在满足 p95 LLM 延迟不超过无检索基线 1.25 倍的前提下，取得最佳固定策略 2.0 倍的检索吞吐，阶段盲对照 TimeGate 则各次运行均违反输出延迟限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在统一内存设备端系统中，独立的 CPU 检索任务与 GPU LLM 推理共享内存与 CPU 资源，饱和的本地检索会显著抬高解码阶段尾延迟，而预填充阶段受影响较小，需要一个能根据 LLM 阶段动态限制检索并发度的调度策略。

Method: PHASEGATE 在统一内存的设备端系统中运行：GPU 执行 LLM 推理（含预填充与解码两个阶段），CPU 上运行独立的本地检索工作负载（并发检索 worker 数量可调）。系统以 LLM 阶段作为准入信号：对预填充和解码分别标定不同的检索并发上限（在 base-M4 配置上分别为 4 和 1）。当处于预填充阶段时允许更高的 CPU 检索并发，因为此时 LLM 对 CPU/内存带宽的敏感度较低；进入解码阶段后把检索并发限制为 1，以避免干扰逐 token 生成的尾延迟。论文通过在不同 M4/M2 系统上的受控实验验证了策略排序，并设置 TimeGate 阶段盲对照（使用同一组并发限制按标定时间表切换，但不观察 LLM 阶段）来证明阶段信号本身是关键因素。推理路径为：请求到达后先进入预填充，PHASEGATE 在此期间放行更多检索；解码开始后降低检索并发以保护输出 token 延迟；检索任务在排队的后备队列中等待准入。论文未披露模型参数规模、具体训练过程或部署细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将 LLM 阶段（prefill vs decode）作为独立 CPU 检索的准入信号；为预填充和解码阶段分别标定并发上限（base-M4 上分别为 4 和 1）；提出阶段盲控制 TimeGate（基于标定时间表使用相同两个限制但不观察 LLM 阶段）作为对照，证明阶段感知的必要性

Abstract: On-device assistants run GPU-based LLM inference alongside CPU retrieval on unified-memory systems. Under a saturated local-retrieval workload, four concurrent retrieval workers raise 95th-percentile (p95) decode latency by 60-61% on two M4 systems, whereas prefill latency rises by only 5.7-6.9%. We study LLM phase as an admission signal for independent CPU retrieval under controlled LLM workloads. PHASEGATE calibrates separate concurrency limits for prefill and decode, selecting four and one on our base-M4 configuration. Under a backlogged queue, it achieves 2.0 times the aggregate retrieval throughput of the best tested feasible fixed policy, with both p95 LLM latency metrics within 1.25 times their no-retrieval baselines in all seven held-out runs. A phase-blind control, TimeGate, uses the same two limits on a calibration-derived schedule without observing LLM phase. It achieves similar retrieval throughput but violates the output-token latency limit in every run. M2 and M2 Pro Mac minis reproduce the policy ordering, while output-length sweeps show that the advantage narrows as decode occupies more of each request.

</details>


### [153] [Length Generalization Needs Proper Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04518)
*Pavlo Vasylenko,Matthias Lindemann,André F. T. Martins,Marcos Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究正则化对LLM长度泛化的影响，发现权重衰减损害外推，dropout放置位置关键，提出在线性投影前应用dropout及方差保持仿射Dropout（VPAD），在SmolLM3和Mamba2上实现显著外推提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何通过改进训练过程（正则化方法）而非仅架构选择，实现序列模型对训练时未见上下文长度的长度泛化。

Method: 论文以自回归Transformer（SmolLM3）和Mamba2为研究对象。输入为token序列，经嵌入层和位置编码（滑动窗口注意力）后进入多层Transformer块。每个块包含注意力子层和前馈网络子层，均采用前置层归一化（pre-LN）结构。标准做法是在层归一化之前应用dropout。作者通过分析发现，这种放置会导致预激活分布的系统性偏移，影响长度外推。他们提出将dropout应用于线性投影之前（即注意力输出投影或FFN第二线性层之前），以保持预激活的方差稳定。此外，作者提出方差保持仿射Dropout（VPAD），通过仿射变换调整dropout掩码的均值和方差，使预激活的方差在训练和推理时更一致。训练目标为标准语言建模交叉熵，训练过程涉及持续预训练：在原有预训练模型基础上，用包含dropout的正则化继续训练。推理时，模型直接处理更长序列，无需额外微调。评估使用Needle-in-a-Haystack、RULER和HELMET等长上下文基准，测量模型在超过训练长度时的外推能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Needle-in-a-Haystack外推倍数", "value": "64x", "setting": "修改后的SmolLM3（滑动窗口注意力）经持续预训练并应用dropout后，可完美外推到训练长度的64倍。"}, {"metric": "RULER和HELMET外推性能", "value": "远超预训练上下文长度", "setting": "同上模型，在RULER和HELMET基准上大幅超越预训练上下文长度。"}, {"metric": "预激活方差失配", "value": "显著减少", "setting": "VPAD在Transformer上应用后，相比标准dropout放置，大幅降低预激活方差失配。"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现权重衰减会损害长度外推，而dropout可改善外推，但需重新考虑其放置位置。；指出标准dropout在层归一化前放置会造成系统性分布不匹配，并证明将dropout放在线性投影前可解决该问题。；提出方差保持仿射Dropout（VPAD），显著减少预激活方差失配，进一步提升Transformer外推能力。；验证了改进对Mamba2同样有效，表明问题是架构无关的。

Abstract: Length generalization is the ability of sequential models to perform well on context lengths unseen during training. In this work, we show that the challenge of achieving length generalization is related not only to architectural choices such as positional encoding and the attention mechanism but also to the training procedure itself. We study how regularization affects length generalization and find that weight decay can hinder extrapolation. In contrast, dropout improves extrapolation when its placement within the architecture is reconsidered. In that regard, we show that the standard placement of dropout before layer normalization introduces a systematic distributional mismatch, and that applying dropout just before the linear projection resolves this issue. For example, a modified SmolLM3 with sliding window attention, continually pre-trained with dropout, can extrapolate perfectly to 64$\times$ on Needle-in-a-Haystack and far beyond the pre-training context size on RULER and HELMET. Mamba2 also benefits from dropout, suggesting an architecture-agnostic nature of the problem. We further propose Variance-Preserving Affine Dropout (VPAD), a new dropout strategy that substantially reduces the resulting pre-activation variance mismatch, leading to further extrapolation improvements in transformers.

</details>


### [154] [Self-Reflection Fine-Tuning: Enhancing Agent Security against Prompt Injection Attacks from Failure Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04269)
*Zixuan Wang,Hao Li,Fengyu Gao,G. Edward Suh,Yi Zeng,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik,Ning Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SRFT框架，让LLM智能体从提示注入攻击导致的失败轨迹中，借助专家模型生成的自反思推理进行微调，实例化为基于Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen3-8B的SR-Agent，在静态与自适应攻击基准上显著降低攻击成功率并保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐方法依赖静态专家轨迹或偏好优化，难以泛化到自适应提示注入攻击；如何让LLM智能体从自身在对抗条件下的失败经验中学习，从而提升对提示注入的鲁棒性并保持任务性能。

Method: SRFT框架包含三个主要阶段：1）在工具增强环境中对智能体施加提示注入攻击，构造被破坏的轨迹，使智能体暴露于不安全行为；2）引入专家模型生成结构化的自反思推理，对比不安全动作与最优动作，解释恶意指令识别、后果推理以及如何保持与原始用户意图对齐；3）利用这种反思监督对智能体进行微调，使其学会识别恶意指令并维持对齐。实例化系统SR-Agent以Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen3-8B为基座模型，在静态与自适应提示注入基准上评估。训练目标为通过反思监督信号优化智能体策略，推理时智能体直接执行工具增强任务。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "attack success rate", "value": "substantially reduced", "setting": "静态与自适应提示注入基准，基于Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct与Qwen3-8B"}, {"metric": "task performance", "value": "preserved", "setting": "与降低攻击成功率同时达成"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Self-Reflection Fine-Tuning (SRFT)训练框架，让智能体从被注入攻击破坏的轨迹中学习失败经验，而非被动模仿专家行为。；利用专家模型生成结构化的自反思推理，对比不安全动作与最优动作，形成反思监督信号。；实例化SR-Agent，基于Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen3-8B，在静态与自适应提示注入基准上验证攻击成功率显著降低且任务性能得以保持，并展现出对自适应攻击的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in tool-augmented environments, but their reliance on external inputs makes them highly vulnerable to prompt injection attacks that can hijack task objectives. Existing safety alignment methods rely on static expert trajectories or preference optimization, limiting their ability to generalize to adaptive attack patterns. In this work, we propose Self-Reflection Fine-Tuning (SRFT), a training framework that enables agents to improve robustness by learning from their own failure experiences under adversarial conditions. Instead of passively imitating expert behaviors, SRFT exposes the agent to compromised trajectories constructed via injected attacks, and leverages an expert model to generate structured self-reflection reasoning that contrasts unsafe and optimal actions. This reflective supervision teaches the agent to identify malicious instructions, reason about their consequences, and maintain alignment with the original user intent. We instantiate this framework in SR-Agent, built on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen3-8B, and evaluate it on both static and adaptive prompt injection benchmarks. Experimental results show that SRFT substantially reduces attack success rates while preserving task performance, and demonstrates strong generalization under adaptive attacks. These findings suggest that learning from failure via self-reflection is a promising direction for building robust and secure LLM agents. Our code is released at this https URL .

</details>


### [155] [Dynamic Harness Search: Building Multi-Agent Systems Per-Query via Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04137)
*Som Sagar,Shasha Li,Hejie Cui,Ransalu Senanayake,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHIFT通过本地LLM架构师和MCTS为每个查询动态构建多智能体系统（harness），避免推理时搜索，在六个基准上平均准确率达80%，比最强基线高7.2个百分点，且廉价模式节省32%执行token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何为每个查询动态构建最优的多智能体系统（harness），以避免推理时的重复搜索和高昂的手动设计成本。

Method: SHIFT系统包含一个本地LLM架构师和一个价值函数。架构师学习一个策略，用于选择harness构建动作（如添加角色、指令、工具等）。价值函数从实测执行中学习，预测一个平衡准确率和执行成本的效用。对于每个查询，蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）利用这些预测来构建harness，即通过树搜索选择动作序列，最终形成一个完整的harness。执行器（如Gemini 3.5 Flash）执行构建好的harness来完成任务。训练过程涉及从搜索中学习策略和价值函数，从而将执行从每查询循环中移出。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "约80%", "setting": "9,193个任务，六个基准测试，使用Gemini 3.5 Flash执行器"}, {"metric": "相对最强基线提升", "value": "7.2个百分点", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "执行token节省", "value": "32%", "setting": "SHIFT的廉价模式，平均准确率仍高于所有基线"}, {"metric": "联合选择结构、指令和工具的准确率提升", "value": "最高9.1个百分点", "setting": "与仅选择指令或仅选择工具相比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出SHIFT方法，将执行从每查询搜索循环中移出，通过本地LLM架构师学习harness构建动作的策略。；使用价值函数预测基于实测执行的效用（平衡准确率和执行成本），并利用蒙特卡洛树搜索为每个查询构建harness。；联合选择结构、指令和工具，并证明其优于仅选择指令或仅选择工具。

Abstract: Agent harnesses specify the roles, instructions, tools, and communication structure used to solve a task, and the right harness depends on the query. Because the value of each design choice is observable only through execution, tailoring a harness to each query has required either executing alternatives at inference time or costly manual design. We introduce SHIFT, which moves execution out of the per-query search loop. A local LLM architect learns a policy over harness-building actions from search, and a value function that predicts, from measured executions, a utility balancing accuracy against execution cost. For each query, Monte Carlo tree search uses these predictions to construct a harness. Across 9,193 tasks in six benchmarks, from math to document and general-assistant tasks, with a Gemini 3.5 Flash executor, SHIFT attains the highest mean accuracy, about 80%, outperforming 17 baselines that span prompting, prompt optimization, and workflow search, and exceeding the strongest baseline by 7.2 percentage points. A cheaper mode of SHIFT also attains a higher mean accuracy than every baseline while using 32% fewer execution tokens than the strongest baseline. We further show that choosing structure, instructions, and tools jointly beats choosing only instructions or only tools by up to 9.1 percentage points, and that learned value selection identifies more accurate harnesses with lower execution cost from candidate pools.

</details>


### [156] [Dual-Scale Relational Graph Transformers for Ecosystem-Aware Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04138)
*Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Xinrou Li,Yao Xiao,Zijun Yao,Xin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERMES是一种用于数字银行账户盗用欺诈检测的双尺度图Transformer，微观层通过关系感知注意力建模会话局部异构图，宏观层利用气候令牌和自适应类原型捕获全局欺诈生态。在1.3亿+真实交易会话上，比生产系统相对减少44.44%客户摩擦，提升24.66%欺诈召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产系统主要依赖表格模型独立评分会话，丢弃交互网络中的关系结构；现有图模型仅推理局部邻域，无法联合建模局部关系结构与全局欺诈生态的动态变化。

Method: HERMES由Micro-GT和Macro-GT两个尺度组成。Micro-GT对会话的时序安全邻域构建异构子图，通过结构化关系感知注意力聚合邻居信息，得到局部关系表示。Macro-GT从全局视角引入非前瞻性气候令牌（总结欺诈动态、平台转移、基础设施复用）和自适应类原型（追踪欺诈与良性会话的典型模式），通过注意力机制融合宏观生态上下文。最终表示联合输入风险评分头，训练目标为欺诈二分类，在推断时对会话输出风险分数。宏观模块使用非前瞻性设计以避免未来信息泄露，适应欺诈模式演变。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "客户摩擦相对降低", "value": "44.44%", "setting": "与生产系统对比，在1.3亿+高风险交易会话上，领先美国金融机构线上环境"}, {"metric": "欺诈召回率相对提升", "value": "24.66%", "setting": "与生产系统对比，在1.3亿+高风险交易会话上，领先美国金融机构线上环境"}]}

Conclusion: 双尺度架构HERMES，联合建模微观局部异构图结构与宏观生态上下文；Micro-GT：基于关系感知注意力的时序安全会话邻域编码；Macro-GT：使用非前瞻性气候令牌（climate tokens）总结欺诈动态、平台转移和基础设施复用；自适应类原型（adaptive class prototypes）随时间追踪代表性欺诈与良性会话模式；在真实生产环境验证并取得摩擦降低与召回提升的线上收益

Abstract: Account takeover (ATO) fraud is a growing threat to digital banking, requiring effective detection while minimizing friction for legitimate customers. Production systems predominantly rely on tabular models that score sessions in isolation, discarding the relational structure of the underlying interaction network. Although graph-based models exploit relationships among sessions and network entities, they primarily reason over local neighborhoods and therefore capture only part of the problem: fraud risk depends jointly on the local relational structure surrounding a session and the evolving global state of the fraud ecosystem. We present HERMES (HEterogeneous Relational Micro--macro graph transformer Encoder for high-risk Sessions), a dual-scale architecture that jointly models these complementary scales of information. Micro-GT captures local heterogeneous graph structure through structured, relation-aware attention over a temporally safe session neighborhood. Complementing this local representation, Macro-GT models ecosystem-level context using non-anticipative climate tokens that summarize fraud dynamics, platform shifts, and infrastructure reuse, together with adaptive class prototypes that track representative fraud and benign session patterns over time. Evaluated on more than 130 million high-risk transaction sessions from a leading U.S. financial institution, HERMES consistently outperforms production and strong graph-based baselines, achieving a 44.44% relative reduction in customer friction and a 24.66% relative improvement in fraud recall over the production system. Ablation and temporal-stability analyses further demonstrate complementary gains from local relational modeling and global ecosystem context across changing fraud regimes.

</details>


### [157] [Consideration Circuits: Depth Separation and Universality Beyond a Single Softmax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04143)
*Junjie Xiao,Huiwen Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出考虑电路（CC），用DAG中的MNL单元构建多阶段选择模型，突破单一softmax的表达限制。理论证明深度3-4可实现指数级参数效率提升，并在Expedia、Trivago等真实选择数据集上以极少参数取得更低NLL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与深度特征选择模型都只使用单一softmax对物品打分，无法在各阶段结果之间进行动态路由，导致在非共线特征和妥协效应下出现固定误差下界。论文提出考虑电路（CC），通过DAG组合多个MNL单元，提升表达能力和参数效率。

Method: 考虑电路（CC）是一种基于特征的多阶段选择模型，由有向无环图定义，图中的节点为多项式Logit（MNL）单元。源单元接收菜单项特征，直接为每个菜单项分配概率；内部单元不直接使用原始特征，而是通过其前驱节点输出的概率加权特征摘要来计算MNL权重，再基于这些权重组合前驱分布。整个DAG的输入是菜单项的特征向量，输出是最终的选择概率分布。训练目标是最小化负对数似然（NLL），优化各MNL单元的权重（taste vectors）和路由偏置。推理时，给定菜单项特征，沿DAG前向传播，源单元计算初始概率分布，内部单元逐级融合前驱分布，最终输出选择概率。理论上分析了深度与taste向量范数的关系，证明深度3足以逼近满足几何条件的任意确定性选择表，深度4可实现最优对数范数缩放。实验中，独立树形CC作为选择模型或输出头，替换线性MNL读出，在多个数据集上降低NLL。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Mean Test NLL", "value": "低于所有评估模型", "setting": "四个固定池基准 + Expedia时间划分，独立树形考虑电路参数<600"}, {"metric": "Mean Test NLL", "value": "每个测试编码器均降低", "setting": "作为输出头替换线性MNL读出，在Expedia和Trivago数据集上"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出考虑电路（CC）：由源单元和内部单元组成的有向无环图，每个内部单元用前驱分布的概率加权特征摘要计算MNL权重来组合前驱分布。；证明深度-范数分离：在三物品妥协任务上，深度2到深度3使达到误差ε所需的最大taste-vector范数从Θ(log(1/ε)/ε)降到Θ(log(1/ε))，且深度2下界适用于任意宽度和菜单无关路由偏置。；给出在有限菜单族上逼近任意确定性选择表的两个几何充要条件；满足时深度3足够，深度4在存在非单元素菜单时达到最优对数范数缩放。；实验表明少于600参数的独立树形电路在四个固定池基准和Expedia时间划分上取得最低平均测试NLL；作为输出头可泛化线性MNL读出，对每个测试编码器在Expedia和Trivago上均降低平均测试NLL。

Abstract: Most feature-based choice models, classical and deep, score items and apply a single softmax. We introduce consideration circuits (CC), feature-based models of multi-stage choice defined by directed acyclic graphs of multinomial logit (MNL) units. Source units assign probabilities to menu items, and internal units combine predecessor distributions using MNL weights computed from their probability-weighted feature summaries. On a three-item compromise task with fixed non-collinear features, menu-independent random-utility models (RUM), including a single MNL unit, suffer an error bounded away from zero. For CC, in contrast, we establish a sharp depth--norm separation: increasing depth from $2$ to $3$ reduces the optimal maximum taste-vector norm for error $\epsilon$ from $\Theta(\log(1/\epsilon)/\epsilon)$ to $\Theta(\log(1/\epsilon))$. The depth-$2$ lower bound holds for arbitrary width and menu-independent routing biases, while a five-node depth-$3$ circuit with zero routing biases attains the logarithmic rate. More generally, we characterize two geometric conditions that are necessary and sufficient for approximating arbitrary deterministic choice tables on finite menu families. Under these conditions, depth $3$ suffices, while depth $4$ achieves optimal logarithmic norm scaling whenever the family contains a non-singleton menu. In experiments, standalone tree circuits with fewer than $600$ parameters attain the lowest mean test negative log-likelihood (NLL) among the evaluated models on four fixed-pool benchmarks and the Expedia temporal split. As output heads, CC generalize the linear MNL readout and lower mean test NLL for every tested encoder on Expedia and Trivago.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond Masked Sparsity: SNACK Enables Truly Sparse Neural Networks on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04093)
*Jafar Badour,Maurice van Keulen,Elena Mocanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SNACK 提出只存储与计算非零连接的真稀疏 GPU 层及 COO 格式 SpMM CUDA 内核，针对小批量高稀疏的 LLM 训练与单流推理调优。在 95% 稀疏度下内核比掩码稠密基线快最高 7 倍，90% 稀疏度下单层训练/推理加速 8x/4x 并省 72% 显存；端到端使 GPT-2 峰值训练显存降 40%、图式推理延迟降 4.8x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏神经网络与动态稀疏训练大多只在稠密张量上用二值掩码，无法把理论上的计算、显存和能耗节省真正落到 GPU 上，导致稀疏模型训练与推理成本依旧高昂。

Method: SNACK 是一套真正稀疏的 GPU 层实现，端到端流程如下：输入为稀疏连接结构，而非稠密张量加掩码；该层只存储和计算非零连接，从而在存储与算子上都保持稀疏范式。用户通过简单的 PyTorch API 对连接进行重构（restructuring），并在此稀疏表示上直接完成前向与反向梯度回传，避免了稠密掩码路径中的无效计算。底层算子 SNACK-COO 采用 COO 格式的 SpMM CUDA 内核，并把 batch 维度映射到 Streaming Multiprocessor 上，专门针对大模型训练的小批量、高稀疏（如 90%–99%）以及单流推理场景做调优，以提升不规则的稀疏矩阵乘的并行利用率。训练目标与优化沿用标准神经网络反向传播，但梯度与连接更新都在稀疏范式中进行；推理路径则以稀疏 SpMM 为唯一计算单元，跳过所有零连接。论文在单层（90% 稀疏）与端到端（GPT-2 训练、图式推理，最高 99% 稀疏）两个层面验证了该架构，报告了相比 Dense+Mask 与完全稠密层的加速、显存与能耗收益。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "内核级加速比", "value": "最高 7x", "setting": "对比 masked dense 基线 Dense+Mask，95% 稀疏度"}, {"metric": "内核性能", "value": "与 cuSPARSE、Sputnik、FlashSparse 持平", "setting": "95% 稀疏度"}, {"metric": "单层训练加速", "value": "8x（对比 Dense+Mask）/ 3.7x（对比完全稠密层）", "setting": "90% 稀疏度，单 SNACK 层"}, {"metric": "单层推理加速", "value": "4x（对比 Dense+Mask）/ 2x（对比完全稠密层）", "setting": "90% 稀疏度，单 SNACK 层"}, {"metric": "显存占用", "value": "比稠密层减少 72%", "setting": "90% 稀疏度，单 SNACK 层"}, {"metric": "能耗", "value": "显著降低（未给出具体数值）", "setting": "90% 稀疏度，单 SNACK 层"}, {"metric": "GPT-2 峰值训练显存", "value": "最多降低 40%", "setting": "端到端 GPT-2 训练，对比 Dense+Mask"}, {"metric": "图式推理延迟", "value": "降低 4.8x", "setting": "端到端，99% 稀疏度，对比 Dense+Mask"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出真正稀疏的 GPU 层 SNACK，只存储和计算非零连接，并提供 PyTorch API 支持完全在稀疏范式下重构连接与反向传播梯度；设计 SNACK-COO，一种自定义 COO 格式 SpMM CUDA 内核，采用 batch 到 Streaming Multiprocessor 的映射，针对大模型训练的小批量、高稀疏场景调优；在 95% 稀疏度下内核级对比 Masked Dense、cuSPARSE、Sputnik、FlashSparse 等基线，覆盖单层与端到端 GPT-2 训练及图式推理场景

Abstract: Deep neural networks continue to grow in parameter count, driving up training and inference cost on GPUs. Sparse neural networks and Dynamic Sparse Training (DST) promise to reduce these costs, but most implementations rely on binary masks over dense tensors and recover little of the theoretical compute, memory, or energy savings. We propose SNACK, a truly sparse GPU layer that stores and computes only non-zero connections. SNACK exposes a simple PyTorch API for restructuring connections and backpropagating gradients entirely in the sparse paradigm, and ships SNACK-COO, a custom COO-format SpMM CUDA kernel with a batch-to-Streaming-Multiprocessor mapping tuned for the small-batch, high-sparsity regime typical of large-model training and single-stream inference. At the kernel level, SNACK is up to 7x faster than the masked dense baseline (Dense+Mask) and competitive with cuSPARSE, Sputnik, and FlashSparse at 95% sparsity. At 90% sparsity, a single SNACK layer accelerates training by 8x and 3.7x, and inference by 4x and 2x, over Dense+Mask and fully dense layers, respectively, while using 72% less memory than dense and substantially less energy. End-to-end, SNACK reduces GPT-2 peak training memory by up to 40% and graph-style inference latency by 4.8x over Dense+Mask at 99% sparsity.

</details>


### [159] [Pareto-Dominant Clarification: Post-Training Coding LLMs via PPO-Lagrangian Budget Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04089)
*Abhinav Rajput,Acey Vogelstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将编码代理的澄清行为建模为带预算约束的CMDP，用PPO-Lagrangian对Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct进行后训练，在HumanEvalComm上展示了预算约束策略可同时提升准确率并降低澄清率，并呈现对数形帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在编码智能体面对模糊用户指令时，如何在可执行的澄清预算约束下平衡回答正确率与交互成本，避免现有惩罚系数调优需为每个预算水平单独扫参的问题。

Method: 论文将编码代理的澄清决策建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）。状态对应用户指令和当前的代码/对话上下文；动作是决定是否向用户提出澄清问题或直接尝试给出解决方案；奖励同时考虑解决方案正确性和交互成本。目标是在满足期望提出问题的数量不超过预算约束的条件下最大化正确率。采用PPO-Lagrangian进行后训练，即使用拉格朗日乘子将预算约束融入PPO的奖励信号，从而无需为每个澄清预算单独调优惩罚系数即可在不同预算水平下训练出策略。训练对象为Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct。评估时在HumanEvalComm基准上进行，用户由GPT-4o-mini oracle模拟器扮演，模拟器根据代理的提问返回澄清信息。推理时，策略根据当前上下文决定是否提问或直接生成代码；约束的拉格朗日乘子控制策略的澄清倾向，从而调节效率-正确率权衡。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率和澄清率", "value": "预算约束策略同时实现更高准确率与更低澄清率，并呈现对数形帕累托前沿，随澄清增加收益递减", "setting": "Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct 在 HumanEvalComm 上评估，使用 GPT-4o-mini oracle 模拟器"}, {"metric": "澄清预算分配", "value": "训练后的策略在更难的多重退化任务上提问更频繁，无需显式监督", "setting": "HumanEvalComm 任务按退化类型分组比较"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将澄清决策形式化为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP），以期望问题预算作为约束，用PPO-Lagrangian进行带约束后训练。；在HumanEvalComm与GPT-4o-mini oracle模拟器上展示经预算约束的策略相对于未调优基线可同时获得更高准确率和更低澄清率，形成对数形帕累托前沿。；观察到无需显式监督，训练后的策略学会在更难任务（多重退化）上非均匀分配澄清预算，澄清更频繁且更有针对性。

Abstract: Coding agents operating under ambiguous instructions or user prompts must decide whether to ask clarifying questions or attempt a solution directly. While clarification from the user may improve the correctness of the agent's solution, each back-and-forth interaction incurs user and system costs, forming an explicit accuracy vs. efficiency tradeoff. Existing works study clarification behavior but do not train policies under enforceable clarification budgets; penalty-based approaches typically require separate coefficient tuning swept across all clarification budget levels. We formulate clarification as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP) and post-train Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct with PPO-Lagrangian to optimize coding accuracy, subject to an expected question-budget constraint. Evaluated on HumanEvalComm with a GPT-4o-mini oracle simulator, the resulting policies reveal that untuned clarification behavior is Pareto-inefficient: budget-constrained policies can simultaneously achieve higher accuracy and lower clarification rates than the baseline model. Across budget levels, we observe a log-shaped Pareto frontier with diminishing returns to additional clarification. Gains arise not from simply asking more questions overall, but from improved question targeting and better code generation under ambiguity. Without explicit supervision, trained policies learn to allocate clarification budget non-uniformly, asking more frequently on tougher (multi-degradation) tasks. These results suggest that unconstrained interactive LLM systems may systematically use clarification inefficiently.

</details>


### [160] [TreeWalker: Partial Evaluation for Grouped Tree-Ensemble Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03939)
*Durmus Karatay,Richard Newman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TreeWalker 将部分求值引入分组树集成推理，针对 CTR 预测、场景分析等共享特征场景，对每棵树组内只遍历一次，利用位掩码分区跳过空子树，在 Intel/Arm 上最高获得 7.8 倍加速，且不改变训练流程，兼容 LightGBM/XGBoost。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分组推理中，行组共享大量特征取值，但标准推理逐行独立计算，导致共享工作被重复 G 次，推理效率低。

Method: TreeWalker 接收标准 LightGBM 或 XGBoost 树集成模型，对一组共享部分特征取值的行进行推理。输入为行组，组内每一行特征向量包含常量特征和动态特征。方法对每棵树进行一次遍历：在常量特征对应的分裂处，所有行走向相同分支，直接合并；在动态特征对应的分裂处，使用行位掩码将行分成两个子组，分别继续遍历。当子树中没有行时跳过。遍历过程维护每个行组的位掩码，最终到达叶子节点时按位掩码写入预测值。训练过程不变，TreeWalker 只修改推理路径。理论分析将每棵树的单次遍历工作量分解为常量投影子树大小 |Tc|、G 次叶子写入以及谓词掩码供应成本 Q。对于 trace 评估器，当组大小 G 趋于无穷时，每行工作量趋近于独立逐行遍历的 (dv+1)/(d+1) 比例。推理时从树根开始，根据当前分裂特征类型决定批量走同一分支或基于动态特征值分区，递归执行直到叶子，输出每行的预测累加值。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推理加速", "value": "2.5-3.2倍", "setting": "Intel 架构，参考配置 T=500, L=8，与逐行独立遍历相比"}, {"metric": "推理加速", "value": "6.8-7.8倍", "setting": "Intel 架构，G=128，生存数据集，与逐行独立遍历相比"}, {"metric": "场景分析基准全部配置领先", "value": "16/16 配置", "setting": "Intel 和 Arm 两种架构，所有配置均比逐行独立遍历快"}, {"metric": "输出精度（f64模型）", "value": "与 treelite GTIL 在求和顺序上匹配", "setting": "f64 模型"}, {"metric": "输出精度（f32模型）", "value": "f64 累加在 99.98% 的行上比原生 f32 更接近 Kahan 补偿参考，且从不更远", "setting": "f32 模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将部分求值应用于分组树集成推理：常量特征视为静态，变化特征视为动态，每个树对一组行只遍历一次。；通过位掩码在变化分裂处分区行，跳过空子树，减少冗余计算。；提出结构性的工作量分解：每棵树的工作分为常量投影子树大小 |Tc|、G 次叶子写入和谓词掩码供应成本 Q。；证明随着组大小 G 趋向无穷，trace 评估器每行工作量趋近于独立行走的 (dv+1)/(d+1) 比例。；兼容标准 LightGBM 和 XGBoost 模型，无需改变训练。

Abstract: Many inference workloads evaluate a trained tree ensemble on row groups that share feature values: discrete-time survival models expand each patient into $G$ time steps, click-through-rate models score every item in a search session, and scenario analyses vary a few inputs while holding the rest fixed. Standard inference treats each row independently and repeats the shared work $G$ times. We present TreeWalker, which applies partial evaluation to grouped inference: constant features are static, varying features dynamic. It walks each tree once per group, partitions a row bitmask at varying splits, and skips empty subtrees. Training is unchanged: TreeWalker reads standard LightGBM and XGBoost models. We prove a structural work decomposition: per-tree work splits into the constant-projected subtree size $|T_c|$, $G$ leaf writes, and a predicate-mask provisioning cost $Q$. For the trace evaluator, per-row work approaches a $(d_v+1)/(d+1)$ fraction of a row-independent walk as $G \to \infty$. On Intel, TreeWalker is 2.5-3.2$\times$ faster than a row-independent traversal at the reference configuration ($T=500$, $L=8$) and 6.8-7.8$\times$ faster at $G=128$ on the survival datasets, with larger gains on Arm. On a scenario-analysis benchmark it is faster in all 16 configurations on both architectures. For f64 models, outputs match treelite's GTIL up to summation order; for f32 models, f64 accumulation is closer to a Kahan-compensated reference than native f32 on 99.98% of rows and never farther.

</details>


### [161] [Base Models Can Reason By Taking a Cue From Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06851)
*Sophie L. Wang,Amil Dravid,Rulin Shao,Kevin Farhat,Sewon Min,Alexei A. Efros*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现基础模型的推理能力可由训练数据中的起始 token 线索触发，固定线索可使性能大幅提升甚至超过 RL 训练模型，并通过因果数据干预验证了线索与训练数据的因果联系，为理解与改进 LLM 推理提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究基础模型响应起始 token 线索与后续推理行为之间的关联，以及这种关联如何由训练数据塑造。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是对现有基础模型（Olmo-3-7B、Qwen3-14B）进行行为分析与干预。输入为问题提示与可选的起始 token 线索，模型生成响应。核心方法包括：1) 在解码开始前固定特定的起始 token 序列，观察其对后续推理行为的影响；2) 通过因果数据干预，在训练数据中插入或删除特定词与推理行为的关联，从而改变线索的有效性；3) 分析隐藏状态表示与训练文档类型的相关性；4) 在安全场景下测试不同线索对拒绝/合规行为的影响。推理路径为标准自回归生成，但强制以指定线索开头。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MATH-500 pass@1", "value": "42% → 78%", "setting": "Olmo-3-7B 基础模型，固定起始线索 '.\\n\\nOkay'"}, {"metric": "MATH-500 pass@1", "value": "72% → 87%", "setting": "Qwen3-14B 基础模型，固定起始线索 'Alright,'"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现固定特定起始 token 线索可使基础模型在数学与代码任务上的表现与 RL 训练模型相当或更优。；通过因果数据干预将任意词（如 chicken）转化为有效推理线索，或消除已有线索的效果。；揭示不同线索诱导的隐藏状态表示与训练集中不同文档类型相关。；将 token 线索研究扩展到安全领域，发现不同线索引发不同的拒绝与合规行为。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how training data creates associations between the tokens at the start of a base model's response and the reasoning behavior that follows. First, we demonstrate that fixing particular starting token cues makes a base model's performance competitive with that of its reinforcement learning (RL)-trained counterparts on math and coding. For instance, the cue ".\n\nOkay" raises Olmo-3-7B's MATH-500 pass@1 accuracy from 42% to 78%, while "Alright," raises Qwen3-14B's from 72% to 87%. Second, RL makes these cues more likely, while fixing them recovers much of its performance gain over the base model. Third, we trace the reasoning effects of token cues to the training data. We perform causal data interventions to turn an arbitrary word, such as "chicken", into an effective reasoning cue, or remove an existing cue's effect. A similar edit makes the prompt instruction "Think duck duck goose" as effective as "Think step by step" at eliciting reasoning. We also find that the hidden state representations induced by different cues correlate with different document types from the training set. Finally, we extend our study of token cues with a case study in language model safety, finding that different cues elicit distinct refusal and compliance behaviors that correspond to different types of training data.

</details>


### [162] [How Sparse Probability Maps Shape Mixture-of-Experts Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06677)
*Tomás Brogueira,Marcos Treviso,Miguel Couceiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了 MoE 路由中稀疏概率映射（softmax、entmax、sparsemax、normmax）在训练后的实际行为，发现稀疏映射产生的零概率并不必然导致专家参与稀疏化，路由器会与映射协同适应学习不同的分数分布。在 300M 和 1B 模型上的实验表明，稀疏映射未改善验证损失，但显著提升了推理时增加专家数的鲁棒性（sparsemax 仅损失 0.02 nats，softmax 损失 0.58 nats）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究稀疏概率映射（sparsemax, α-entmax, normmax）在 MoE 路由器训练后，其零概率专家分配是否真正导致 token 级专家参与稀疏化，以及不同概率映射如何与学习到的路由分数共同影响专家选择。

Method: 论文研究的是标准 top-K MoE 路由架构：输入 token 表示经过路由器线性层得到专家分数，再通过概率映射（softmax、1.5-entmax、sparsemax 或 2-normmax）转换为概率分布，然后选择 top-K 个专家进行加权求和。训练目标为语言建模交叉熵损失。论文的核心分析在于：概率映射产生的零概率并不直接决定专家是否被选中，因为当排序后前两个分数之差小于一个固定阈值时，路由器仍会保留原本可能被丢弃的专家。不同映射在训练中会与路由器分数协同适应，学习到不同的分数分布，从而影响实际的专家参与模式。推理时可调整 K 值，实验评估了 K=2 训练后使用 K=8 推理时的验证损失变化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "validation loss", "value": "稀疏映射均未优于 softmax", "setting": "300M 和 1B top-2 MoE 语言模型训练"}, {"metric": "概率质量丢弃比例", "value": "entmax 比 softmax 少丢弃 30%", "setting": "1B 模型，top-2 路由"}, {"metric": "单专家 token 路由比例", "value": "21%", "setting": "1B 模型，2-normmax，top-2 路由"}, {"metric": "推理时 K=2 到 K=8 验证损失变化", "value": "sparsemax 损失 0.02 nats", "setting": "K=2 训练，推理时 K=8"}, {"metric": "推理时 K=2 到 K=8 验证损失变化", "value": "softmax 损失 0.58 nats", "setting": "K=2 训练，推理时 K=8"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 对 300M 和 1B 规模 top-2 MoE 语言模型进行匹配训练，系统对比 softmax、1.5-entmax、sparsemax 和 2-normmax 四种路由概率映射。；发现每个映射仅在排序前两分数差距达到固定阈值时才会丢弃已选专家，且不同映射学习到的分数分布差异（entmax 分数跨度约为 softmax 的一半）决定其实际专家参与度。；揭示稀疏映射训练后对推理时增加专家数（K=2 到 K=8）的鲁棒性显著不同：sparsemax 仅损失 0.02 nats，而 softmax 损失 0.58 nats。；提出自适应 MoE 路由应基于概率映射与学习分数的联合行为设计，而非仅依赖映射本身的稀疏特性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) routers typically apply softmax to the router scores and keep the top-K experts, making every token use exactly K experts. Sparsity-inducing probability maps such as sparsemax, alpha-entmax and normmax can adaptively assign exact zeros to selected experts, and therefore appear to offer token-dependent expert participation, even when using the same top-K machinery. In this work, we study whether and how this sparsity survives training. We train matched 300M and 1B top-2 MoE language models with softmax, 1.5-entmax, sparsemax and 2-normmax, and find that the maps behave very differently once trained: at 1B, entmax discards 30% less probability mass than softmax while almost never dropping a selected expert, sparsemax retains the most mass, and normmax routes 21% of tokens to a single expert. These outcomes are not properties of the maps alone. Each map drops a selected expert only when the gap between the two largest scores reaches a fixed threshold, and the trained routers differ in the score distribution they learn: the entmax router learns scores with roughly half the spread of softmax's, which keeps its top-2 gaps below its threshold, while sparsemax and normmax, which share the same threshold, learn different gap distributions and hence different participation. Routers thus co-adapt their scores to the map, and a map's capacity to produce zeros does not by itself determine expert participation. While none of the sparse maps improves validation loss over softmax, they make the trained models far less sensitive to selecting more experts at inference: sparsemax trained with K=2 loses 0.02 nats when run with K=8, where softmax loses 0.58. Our results indicate that adaptive MoE routing has to be designed around the joint behavior of the probability map and the learned scores, rather than around the map alone.

</details>


### [163] [What Matters for Latent Reasoning with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06666)
*Yassine Ouali,Adrian Bulat,Georgios Tzimiropoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出隐空间推理应满足有用、多样、可解释、可精炼、高效五项要求，并给出基于流匹配的FLaRe训练配方：定义隐空间编码与塑造方式、流模型训练位置、答案读出路，以及基于模型自身已验证思维的最终训练阶段。五项探针均优于先前隐空间方法，算术基准上以约四分之一延迟达到显式CoT 97%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何设计满足有用性、多样性、可解释性、可精炼性与高效性五项要求的隐空间连续推理，使其在同等准确率下比显式思维链（CoT）更省算力。

Method: 论文聚焦于在习得隐空间中用流匹配进行隐推理，但摘要未披露端到端的具体网络结构、隐空间维度、条件方式与训练损失细节。已披露的流程为：LLM在连续隐空间中思考，仅在最终输出答案；训练上采用流匹配建模隐空间中的推理轨迹分布，并明确考虑隐空间编码什么内容、如何塑造隐空间、流模型在何处训练、如何从隐空间读出答案，最后增加一个在模型自身已验证思维上继续训练的收尾阶段。推理阶段支持通过重采样产生不同推理轨迹，并可通过增加推理算力进行精炼，从而在算术基准上以约四分之一延迟达到显式CoT 97%的准确率。摘要未给出模块顺序中的数据流、参数量、优化目标公式与具体训练数据，故不作推断。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "在五项要求探针上相对先前隐空间方法的提升", "value": "均优于先前隐空间方法（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "五项要求的探针评估"}, {"metric": "算术基准准确率与延迟", "value": "达到显式CoT准确率的97%，延迟为显式CoT的四分之一", "setting": "算术基准对比显式CoT"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出隐空间推理必须满足的五项要求（有用、多样、可解释、可精炼、高效），并分别为每项设计探针验证；论证流匹配（flow matching）是满足这些要求的最合适隐空间推理族，并系统识别其关键训练选择；提出FLaRe配方：涵盖隐空间编码内容与塑造方式、流模型训练位置、答案读出方式，以及基于模型自身已验证思维的最终训练阶段

Abstract: Latent reasoning lets a large language model (LLM) think in a continuous space and verbalize only the answer. We argue that an effective latent thought must meet five requirements: it should be useful, helping produce the correct answer rather than merely changing it, diverse, so that resampling yields different reasoning trajectories, explainable, so that a decoded chain of thought (CoT) reflects reasoning the answer actually follows, refinable with more inference compute, and efficient, costing less than an explicit CoT at comparable accuracy. Current methods rarely meet these requirements: they learn shortcuts from the question, distill the explicit CoT into their weights, or imitate it one token at a time. We focus on flow matching in a learned latent space, the family we argue is best placed to meet them, and identify the training choices that make it work. The result is Flow-based Latent Reasoning (FLaRe), a simple recipe covering what the latent space encodes and how to shape it, where to train the flow, how to read out the answer, and a final stage of training on the model's own verified thoughts. A probe for each requirement shows that FLaRe improves on prior latent methods in all five. It also compares favorably with them on arithmetic benchmarks, while reaching 97% of the accuracy of explicit CoT at a quarter of its latency.

</details>


### [164] [Steering by Influence: Curvature Aware Data Weighting for Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06383)
*James A. E. Dixon,Stephen J. Roberts,Francesco Quinzan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出影响加权激活传输方法，利用影响函数识别最能表达概念主题的示例，并通过最优传输在推理时引导激活，在毒性抑制、概念诱导和真实性任务上优于现有基线，同时基本保持模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时激活引导方法通过对比数据集的激活平均值构建概念表示，会混入无关概念和噪声，且被少数token主导，导致激活传输编码的是token级而非主题级概念。

Method: 该方法在推理时对语言模型激活进行干预。输入为对比数据集（概念文本与非概念文本）以及目标提示。首先，利用影响函数估计每个概念示例对模型概念表示的贡献，该估计考虑模型损失景观的曲率，从而赋予主题相关示例更高权重。然后，使用最优传输将非概念文本的激活表示映射到概念文本的激活表示，其中概念示例按影响分数加权。在推理时，对非概念输入的激活施加此传输映射，将其推向概念相关激活区域，从而在不重新训练模型的情况下实现概念引导。训练目标未涉及参数更新，而是基于影响函数的后验加权。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "激活引导性能", "value": "优于现有激活传输基线", "setting": "毒性抑制 (Jigsaw)、对象概念诱导 (OneSec)、真实性诱导 (TruthfulQA)"}, {"metric": "困惑度与MMLU准确率", "value": "引导效果提升的同时基本保持模型质量", "setting": "能力保持评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 使用影响函数（influence functions）估计每个数据点对模型概念表示的贡献，利用损失景观曲率捕捉超出表面token相似性的概念相关关系。；提出影响加权激活传输（influence-weighted activation transport），用最优传输将非概念文本的激活导向概念文本激活，并以影响分数加权概念示例。；通过实验展示影响函数与基于激活的方法在数据点排序上显著不同，证明影响函数捕获了激活方法遗漏的概念相关信息。

Abstract: Inference-time steering offers cheap, fine-grained control over a language model's outputs by estimating a concept's representation in activation space and shifting activations towards it. Existing methods build these representations from activation averages over contrastive datasets. These averages incorporate unrelated concepts and noise, and are dominated by a few tokens, meaning the activation transport encodes token-level rather than thematic concepts. In this work, we steer towards examples that most express a concept thematically, rather than towards an expectation over all. We identify these examples using influence functions, which estimate how much each data point contributes to a model's representation of a concept. Unlike simple model activation similarity, they incorporate the curvature of the model's loss landscape, allowing them to capture concept-relevant relationships beyond superficial token-level similarity. We then propose influence-weighted activation transport, which uses optimal transport to steer activations of non-concept text towards those of concept text, weighting concept examples by their influence scores. We evaluate on toxicity suppression (Jigsaw), object-based concept induction (OneSec) and truthfulness induction (TruthfulQA), outperforming existing activation-transport baselines. We track capability after steering using perplexity and MMLU accuracy, finding that our method improves steering while largely preserving model quality. We further show that influence functions capture concept-relevant information that activation-based methods miss with the two approaches ranking data points significantly differently. Together, these results demonstrate the value of curvature-aware influence information for activation steering.

</details>


### [165] [StagQ: Constraint-Driven Multi-Precision Weight Quantization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05977)
*Zhe Wei,Mengqi Guo,Yuan Yuan,Jiunn Bin Lim,Boyi Pan,Michael Bi Mi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StagQ 提出一种多精度权重量化格式：2 比特分组仿射基加可配置 1 比特细化平面，任意精度都是可读前缀，共享元数据实现免查表解码，并用稀疏侧记录补偿网格最差权重。在 Llama-3.1-8B、Phi-4、OLMo-2-7B 的 MMLU 上，2 比特配置领先最强多精度基线 3.1-7.0 点；A100 上批量为一矩阵-向量内核多数情形快于两条基线内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在只存储一条权重流的前提下，让同一模型权重同时支持 2/3/4 比特等多种精度运行点，并保持各精度下的模型质量与解码效率。

Method: StagQ 是一种多精度权重量化格式及其解码路径。存储端由三段组成：主码流先存放 2 比特分组仿射量化基，随后是可配置数量的 1 比特细化平面，各平面按二进位步长调度，因此读取任意前缀即可得到对应精度的合法编码，无需为每个精度单独存储一份权重；仿射解码所需元数据在所有精度间共享，解码时由元数据推导仿射映射直接还原权重，省去逐权重查表。第三部分是稀疏侧记录，在网格拟合之前和之后两次填充，专门保存均匀网格拟合误差最大的少数权重，以补偿网格尾部误差。推理端实现了一个面向批量为一矩阵-向量乘积的解码内核，按上述前缀结构逐精度解码，并在 NVIDIA A100 上以合成权重对多种形状与精度组合计时。编码器提供两种配置，分别在 2 比特与 3 比特点位上与多精度基线在 Llama-3.1-8B、Phi-4、OLMo-2-7B 上比较 MMLU 质量；未披露训练目标与训练资源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MMLU 点数", "value": "在 2 比特配置下，较最强多精度基线在 Llama-3.1-8B、Phi-4、OLMo-2-7B 上领先 3.1 至 7.0 点，且逻辑比特率略低", "setting": "2 比特配置，Llama-3.1-8B、Phi-4、OLMo-2-7B，MMLU 评测"}, {"metric": "MMLU 对比结果", "value": "在 3 比特配置下，Llama-3.1-8B 上领先，Phi-4 上以更高比特率领先，OLMo-2-7B 上打平", "setting": "3 比特配置，Llama-3.1-8B、Phi-4、OLMo-2-7B，MMLU 评测"}, {"metric": "MMLU 对比结果", "value": "在 4 比特配置下，三个模型均打平，但逻辑率更高", "setting": "4 比特配置，Llama-3.1-8B、Phi-4、OLMo-2-7B，MMLU 评测"}, {"metric": "内核速度", "value": "在批量为一的矩阵-向量乘积中，多数形状-精度组合下快于两个基线内核", "setting": "NVIDIA A100 GPU，合成权重，批量为一矩阵-向量乘"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 多精度分级格式：主码流为 2 比特分组仿射基，后接可配置数量的 1 比特细化平面，采用二进位步长调度，使每个支持精度都是可读前缀；所有精度共享同一份元数据，解码通过由该元数据推导的仿射映射完成，无需逐权重查表；稀疏侧记录同时收纳网格拟合前后表现最差的少数权重，弥补均匀网格的尾部误差；面向批量为一的矩阵-向量乘解码内核，在 NVIDIA A100 上多数形状-精度组合下快于两个基线内核

Abstract: Serving a large language model (LLM) across a fleet of deployments requires several weight-precision operating points. Multi-precision formats serve them all from one stream whose prefixes are valid lower-precision codes, instead of storing multiple copies. We present StagQ, a multi-precision weight format whose main stream is a 2-bit group-wise affine base followed by a configurable number of 1-bit refinement planes on a dyadic step schedule. Every supported precision is a readable prefix, decoded by an affine map derived from metadata shared across all precisions, with no per-weight lookup. A sparse side record, filled both before and after the grid is fitted, holds out the few weights the grid serves worst. We report two configurations of the encoder. At two bits the cheaper one leads the strongest multi-precision baseline on Llama-3.1-8B, Phi-4, and OLMo-2-7B by 3.1 to 7.0 MMLU points, at a slightly lower logical rate. At three bits it leads on Llama-3.1-8B, leads on Phi-4 at a higher rate, and ties on OLMo-2-7B. At four bits it ties on all three, at a higher rate. In a batch-one matrix-vector product on an NVIDIA A100 GPU, timed on synthetic weights, our kernel is faster than the two baseline kernels in most shape-precision cases.

</details>


### [166] [Selecting Long-Horizon Trajectories for Reliable and Efficient Terminal-Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05831)
*Cuong Dang,Hoang Anh Just,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 系统研究了长时程终端智能体训练中的监督范围选择问题，发现可靠性随监督token数增加而饱和，并提出选择性长时程精炼方法，在Terminal-Bench等多个基准上以更少训练时间取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在长时程终端智能体训练中选择监督范围（保留的轨迹token数），以在可靠性、训练成本和行为模式之间取得最优平衡。

Method: 论文聚焦于LLM智能体监督训练过程，未提出新的模型架构。端到端流程为：输入为教师智能体在终端任务上产生的长时程轨迹，每条轨迹由多个token组成；训练目标为模仿学习（行为克隆），即在token级别上最小化负对数似然。关键设计轴是监督范围H：训练时仅保留每条轨迹的前H个token进行监督，丢弃后续token。训练分为两个阶段：首先在短前缀（小H）上训练一个预热模型；然后进行选择性长时程精炼，即用预热模型对长轨迹的延续部分进行评估，仅保留在预热模型下概率最高（即最可能）的延续片段，仅在这些片段上继续训练。该选择策略旨在降低长时程监督带来的有限样本估计误差，同时保留长时程监督减少时间监督偏差的优势。推理时，智能体按标准自回归方式生成动作序列直到终止，训练中不同H的选择会诱导不同的行为模式（短H导致过早终止，中H促进错误恢复，长H导致过度坚持）。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Terminal-Bench任务解决数", "value": "12K token监督范围下29±0.7，优于16K的26±0.8", "setting": "Terminal-Bench，训练时间减少30%"}, {"metric": "成功尝试次数", "value": "从110±2.7提升至126±2.1", "setting": "16K监督范围，选择性长时程精炼对比全量训练"}, {"metric": "至少6/8次尝试解决的任务数", "value": "从9±0.7提升至14±0.6", "setting": "16K监督范围"}, {"metric": "成功尝试次数", "value": "122±2.4", "setting": "仅使用一半长时程数据，训练时间减少23%"}, {"metric": "Terminal-Bench v2.0任务解决数", "value": "从64±2.6提升至73±2.1", "setting": "跨基准迁移"}, {"metric": "OpenThoughts-TBLite任务解决数", "value": "从137±2.7提升至155±2.2", "setting": "跨基准迁移"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统研究监督范围（supervision horizon）这一设计轴，发现可靠性随范围增长但饱和，并伴随短时程早停、中时程错误恢复、长时程过度坚持的行为变化。；通过偏差-复杂度界解释饱和现象：长监督减少时间监督偏差但增加异质后段历史带来的有限样本估计误差。；提出选择性长时程精炼（selective long-horizon refinement）：先在短前缀上训练，再仅对在预热模型下概率最高的延续进行精炼，一致优于全量长时程训练。

Abstract: Terminal agents are commonly trained by imitating long teacher trajectories, yet how much of each trajectory to supervise remains unexplored. We study the \emph{supervision horizon}, the number of trajectory tokens retained for training, and show that it is a key design axis for reliability and cost. Reliability improves with longer horizons but saturates: on Terminal-Bench, a 12K-token horizon solves more tasks than 16K ($29\pm0.7$ vs.\ $26\pm0.8$) while requiring 30\% less training time. The horizon also shapes agent behavior: short horizons cause premature termination, intermediate horizons yield productive error recovery, and long horizons induce over-persistence. We analyze this saturation through a bias--complexity bound, in which longer supervision reduces temporal supervision bias but increases finite-sample estimation error from more heterogeneous late-stage histories. Guided by this analysis, we propose \emph{selective long-horizon refinement}, which first trains on short prefixes and then refines only on continuations that are most likely under the warm-start model. It consistently outperforms full long-horizon training. At 16K, it raises successful attempts from $110\pm2.7$ to $126\pm2.1$ and tasks solved in at least six of eight attempts from $9\pm0.7$ to $14\pm0.6$; with half of the long-horizon data, it still reaches $122\pm2.4$ while cutting training time by 23\%. The gains transfer across benchmarks, from $64\pm2.6$ to $73\pm2.1$ on Terminal-Bench v2.0 and from $137\pm2.7$ to $155\pm2.2$ on OpenThoughts-TBLite. For long-horizon supervision, selecting the right trajectories matters more than training on all of them.

</details>


### [167] [AdaSpark: Adaptive DSpark with Online Learning for Tree Verification and N-gram Fill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05774)
*Liquan Liu,Yifan Zhang,Bowei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AdaSpark 是一种用于树验证投机解码的在线学习调度器，无需预先生成 profile 或宽度扫描，即可在线学习验证宽度价值和接受概率，并统一排序草稿与 n-gram 候选。在六个数据集、三个稠密和一个 MoE 目标模型上，相对 DSpark 实现 1.5-3.1 倍解码加速，imparo 引擎端到端加速 1.17-1.52 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树验证投机解码中，验证行数（树宽度）的选择需要在期望接受更多 token 与更宽验证耗时之间权衡，大多数调度器使用离线表或模型并仅在线修正一个缩放因子，且接受概率来自草稿器置信度或离线拟合映射，缺乏在线自适应能力。

Method: AdaSpark 是一个自适应调度器，集成在投机解码框架中。输入包括草稿器提出的候选 token（树结构）以及请求自身文本的 n-gram 续写候选。核心模块包括：(1) 在线验证宽度选择：维护一个模型，将验证时间拟合为上下文（如批次大小、序列长度等）的函数，并学习哪些验证宽度值得提供；同时估计每个候选的接受概率，该概率模型以草稿器的置信度头作为输入之一，并在线拟合到目标模型的实际验证结果上。(2) 候选排序与树构建：使用拟合的接受概率对草稿候选和文本衍生的 n-gram 候选进行最佳优先排序和树大小确定，使它们在统一的顺序中竞争验证行。(3) 宽度决策：通过将时间按长期解码速率定价，选择最优验证宽度。推理时，调度器实时学习并适应，无需预先生成 profile、离线校准或宽度扫描。整个流程在服务过程中持续更新接受概率和时间模型，以最大化解码吞吐。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "解码速度", "value": "1.5-3.1x faster", "setting": "与 DSpark 使用相同草稿器对比，在六个公开数据集单轮和多轮对话、三个稠密目标模型和一个混合专家目标模型上"}, {"metric": "端到端引擎速度", "value": "1.17-1.52x faster", "setting": "imparo 引擎使用 AdaSpark 对比 imparo 使用三 token 链（默认设置），仅来自调度器"}, {"metric": "相对于最佳固定宽度的延迟差距", "value": "never more than 0.3% slower", "setting": "稠密目标模型和上下文带，无需宽度扫描"}, {"metric": "混合专家目标模型性能", "value": "ties the best pinned width", "setting": "混合专家目标模型"}, {"metric": "其他固定宽度性能", "value": "5-14% slower", "setting": "混合专家目标模型上，固定宽度 4 到 16 行"}]}

Conclusion: 在线学习验证宽度的价值与验证时间函数，无需预先 profile、校准或扫描。；将每个候选的接受概率拟合到目标模型的验证结果上，以草稿器置信度头作为输入之一，并据此排序和确定树大小。；同一模型对请求自身文本的 n-gram 续写进行定价，使草稿候选与文本候选在同一最佳优先顺序中竞争验证行。；以长期解码速率对时间定价来选择验证宽度。

Abstract: Block drafters such as DSpark propose ranked candidates for several positions in one forward pass, and a tree verifier checks them in one pass of the target. The number of rows to verify trades the tokens a wider tree is expected to accept against the time a wider verify takes. Most schedulers that choose this number take the verify time from a table or model measured before serving, corrected online by at most one scale factor, and take acceptance from the drafter's confidence estimates or from a map fitted offline. AdaSpark learns both quantities while it serves, with no profile, calibration or sweep in advance. It learns which verify widths are worth offering and fits each one's verify time as a function of context. It fits each candidate's acceptance probability to the target's verify outcomes, with the drafter's confidence head as one input, and orders and sizes the tree by that fit instead of by the head. The same model prices n-gram continuations of the request's own text, so drafted and text-derived candidates compete for rows in one best-first order. The width is chosen by pricing time at the long-run decode rate. On single- and multi-turn conversations from six public datasets, on three dense targets and one mixture-of-experts target, AdaSpark decodes 1.5-3.1x faster than this http URL 's DSpark with the same drafters. Our imparo engine with AdaSpark is 1.17-1.52x faster than imparo running with a three-token chain (the default this http URL setting); this gain comes from the scheduler alone. Without a width sweep, AdaSpark is never more than 0.3% slower than the best pinned tree width on any dense target or context band. On the mixture-of-experts target it ties the best pinned width, and the other pinned widths from 4 to 16 rows are 5-14% slower.

</details>


### [168] [Voltic: Distinguishing Volatility from Stochasticity in Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05700)
*Parsa Hejabi,Morteza Dehghani,Payam Piray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Voltic将门控delta规则记忆的更新解释为卡尔曼滤波增益，区分波动性与随机性两种不确定性来源，通过各向异性协方差和输入依赖噪声方差实现向量值写入，并用对角/准对角近似保持delta规则形式以复用分块并行核。在受控回忆任务和45M参数语言模型上超越基线，外推能力更好，吞吐接近门控基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环序列模型在每个token处需要决定以多强的程度覆写其记忆；这一写入强度受两个相反方向的不确定性影响：关联变化的波动性（volatility）与单次观测噪声的随机性（stochasticity），现有方法未能区分二者。

Method: Voltic将循环记忆的写入视为贝叶斯滤波中的卡尔曼增益。输入为序列token，模型维护一个各向异性的协方差矩阵以表示不确定性。两个噪声方差——波动性（关联变化速度）与随机性（观测噪声）——均为输入依赖，因此每个token的记忆覆写量是向量值，并携带序列中累积的不确定性。为避免稠密协方差逐token传播所需的O(n^2)开销，作者提出两种假设密度近似：对角和准对角近似，二者均使记忆更新保持delta规则形式，从而可以直接复用门控delta规则记忆的分块并行核（chunked kernels）进行并行训练。训练目标为标准语言建模或受控回忆任务目标，优化方式与delta规则记忆兼容。推理时，模型按token顺序更新记忆，使用近似协方差，吞吐接近门控基线。该设计通过不确定性递归推导写入强度，使记忆对关联变化更敏感，同时保持高效并行。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "受控回忆任务表现", "value": "超过所有基线", "setting": "在关联变化且观测被腐蚀的受控回忆任务上"}, {"metric": "波动性与随机性组合任务的领先幅度", "value": "外推规模下优势大于训练规模下", "setting": "组合波动性与随机性的任务，训练规模与外推规模对比"}, {"metric": "八任务推理平均", "value": "在45M参数语言模型上领先", "setting": "45M参数语言模型，八任务推理平均"}, {"metric": "超出训练上下文长度的检索准确率", "value": "高于门控基线", "setting": "45M参数语言模型，检索准确率，超出训练上下文长度"}, {"metric": "吞吐", "value": "接近门控基线", "setting": "45M参数语言模型推理"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明门控delta规则记忆的更新在各向同性不确定性下是卡尔曼滤波增益的形式。；提出Voltic，保持协方差各向异性并让两个噪声方差均输入依赖，使写入为向量值并携带序列累积的不确定性。；给出两种假设密度近似（对角与准对角），避免稠密协方差逐token传播，保持delta规则形式并复用其分块核，从而支持并行训练。

Abstract: Recurrent sequence models must decide how strongly to overwrite their memory at each token. Read as Bayesian filtering, this write is the gain of a Kalman update, set by uncertainty from two sources that pull it in opposite directions: volatility, how quickly the underlying associations change, and stochasticity, how noisy each observation of them is. First, we show that the update of gated delta-rule memories is the form this filter takes under isotropic uncertainty. Next, we introduce Voltic, a recurrent memory that keeps the covariance anisotropic and makes both noise variances input-dependent, so the write is vector-valued and carries uncertainty accumulated over the sequence. A dense covariance would have to be propagated token by token, ruling out the parallel training these models depend on. We therefore give two assumed-density approximations, diagonal and quasi-diagonal, both of which leave the memory update in delta-rule form and reuse its chunked kernels. On controlled recall tasks in which associations change and observations are corrupted, Voltic leads all baselines.
